### [1] [Quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot Interferometry in Monolayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14633)
*Himadri Chakraborti,Charles L. Tschirhart,James Fang,Saisab Bhowmik,Chengrui Yu,Shujian Cao,William Caro,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Jie Shan,Kin Fai Mak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在单层WSe2中首次实现栅定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪，观测到稳健的Aharonov-Bohm干涉，并从温度与偏压依赖中提取出边态速度约(4.9±0.2)×10^4 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范德华材料平台的量子霍尔干涉仪受多重自旋与谷自由度影响，使干涉图样解释复杂；单层WSe2因强自旋轨道耦合与自旋-谷锁定消除了这些简并，产生本征单分量朗道能级，为干净、无味简并的干涉测量提供理想平台。

Method: 制备单层WSe2栅定义的量子霍尔Fabry-Pérot干涉仪；在同一器件中连续调节腔体尺寸，测量量子霍尔边道的Aharonov-Bohm干涉；结合静电学估算体-边耦合强度Δ，并开展温度与偏压依赖测量。

Result: 观测到跨干涉仪的稳健AB干涉；AB区间占主导，与静电学估算的弱体-边耦合(Δ≈0.034)一致；温度与偏压测量一致给出边态速度v≈(4.9±0.2)×10^4 m/s。

Conclusion: 单层WSe2可作为一个干净、味分辨的量子霍尔干涉平台，为后续研究单分量分数量子霍尔态及任意子统计的干涉探测奠定基础。

Abstract: Electronic Fabry-Pérot interferometers provide a powerful probe of coherent quantum Hall edge transport, yet the presence of multiple spin and valley degrees of freedom in existing van der Waals material platforms can complicate their interpretation. Monolayer WSe$_2$ offers a distinct opportunity because strong spin-orbit coupling and spin-valley locking lift these degeneracies, producing intrinsically single-component Landau levels. Here we realize the first gate-defined quantum Hall Fabry-Pérot interferometer in monolayer WSe$_2$. By continuously tuning the cavity size in a single device, we observe robust Aharonov-Bohm (AB) interference of quantum Hall edge channels across the interferometer; the observed predominance in the AB regime is consistent with a weak bulk-edge coupling ($Δ\approx 0.034$) estimated by electrostatics. Moreover, temperature and bias dependence measurements consistently yield an edge velocity $v \approx (4.9 \pm 0.2) \times 10^4\,\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}}$. Our results establish monolayer WSe$_2$ as a clean, flavor-resolved platform for quantum Hall interferometry and provide a foundation for future studies of single-component fractional quantum Hall states and interferometric probes of anyonic statistics in transition-metal dichalcogenides.

</details>


### [2] [A discrete realization of the boundary sine-Gordon model and the Schmid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14637)
*Nicolas Paris,Oscar Bouverot-Dupuis,Christophe Mora*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一个可精确映射到边界sine-Gordon模型（bsG）的离散模型，用团簇蒙特卡罗高效模拟，确认Schmid转变存在并验证Schmid-Bulgadaev图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bsG模型（等价于电阻分流约瑟夫森结）的相图近年存在争议，需要无歧义地判定Schmid转变是否存在及其性质。

Method: 构造与bsG性质精确对应的离散模型，采用团簇蒙特卡罗算法进行大范围参数模拟，计算弱隧穿与强隧穿区间的完整普适交叉行为；证明模型的自对偶性，并沿临界不动点竖直线与共形场论预言作定量对比。

Result: 明确识别出Schmid转变所特有的竖直临界不动点线，与CFT预言高精度吻合；厘清Schmid转变与BKT转变的本质区别；给出完整普适交叉曲线。

Conclusion: 结果确凿地确立了Schmid转变的存在与性质，证实Schmid-Bulgadaev图像的有效性。

Abstract: The phase diagram of the boundary sine-Gordon model (bsG), or equivalently of the resistively shunted Josephson junction, has been the subject of recent debate. We introduce a discrete model that reproduces exactly the properties of the bsG model and can be simulated extremely efficiently using cluster Monte Carlo algorithms. This enables us to explore a broad range of parameters and compute the complete universal crossover between the weak- and strong-tunneling regimes. We establish the self-duality of the model and unambiguously identify the vertical line of critical fixed points characteristic of the Schmid transition, recovering the conformal field theory predictions along this line with high accuracy. We further clarify the essential distinctions between the Schmid and Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless transitions. Our results definitively establish the existence and nature of the Schmid transition and demonstrate the validity of the Schmid-Bulgadaev scenario.

</details>


### [3] [Controlling the Vacuum: Tunable Magnonic Casimir Effect in Magnetic Moiré Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14809)
*Alejandro Roldan,Dante Navarrete,Mario A. Castro,Sebastián Allende,Roberto E. Troncoso,Alvaro S. Nunez*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过调节莫尔晶格可调控磁自由度的量子涨落，进而使磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力成为莫尔图案参数的函数，可作为"工质"驱动原型引擎，产生新型磁-机械效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明莫尔结构可调控电子能带等性质，但对其磁自由度量子涨落（真空涨落）是否也可由莫尔参数调控、并由此产生可用的机械效应缺乏探讨。作者意在证明莫尔结构的磁涨落可调，并将其作为真空涨落工程与磁-机械耦合的新平台。

Method: 采用Bistritzer-MacDonald连续模型方法，以"莫尔梯子（moiré Ladder）"为范例，计算磁自由度的量子涨落以及由此产生的磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力，并将其表达为莫尔图案参数（如扭角、晶格尺度）的函数；据此设计以真空涨落为工质的原型引擎循环。

Result: 磁振子真空能与磁振子卡西米尔力均可由莫尔晶格参数连续调节；据此可实现一个以真空涨落为工作介质的原型引擎循环，从而给出一种新型磁-机械效应。该结论对具体莫尔结构不敏感。

Conclusion: 莫尔结构可调的真空涨落构成一类普适机制，不依赖于所涉激发的物理性质（磁振子、声子、等离激元等），因此可推广到任意莫尔结构，为莫尔真空涨落工程与磁-机械器件提供通用框架。

Abstract: We prove that the quantum fluctuations of the magnetic degrees of freedom of moiré structures can be tuned by adjusting the moiré lattice. Using the Bistritzer-MacDonald approach, we explore the consequences of this fact for the magnonic vacuum energy and the magnonic Casimir force, which become functions of the moiré pattern parameters. This allows a prototypical engine to be run with the vacuum fluctuations as a working agent and, in that way, to furnish a novel type of magneto-mechanical effect. Our calculations focus on the moiré Ladder as an example but can be generalized to any moiré structure in a straightforward fashion. We anticipate that our results belong to a generic property of moiré structures, regardless of the physical nature of the excitations at hand.

</details>


### [4] [Interplay of the Fano-Andreev Effect and Nonlinear Screening in Electric and Thermoelectric Transport through a T-Shaped Double Quantum Dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14991)
*Sachin Verma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了T型双量子点与正常/超导电极耦合时弱非线性电学与热电输运，采用特征势形式化处理非线性屏蔽，揭示Fano-Andreev效应与屏蔽共同导致的偏压不对称、热电压抑制及线性区外交叉热电响应及其符号竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T型双量子点中侧耦合点诱导的Fano-Andreev效应与非线性屏蔽（非平衡电荷再分布引起的能级重整化）如何共同影响电学与热电输运，尤其在非线性区缺乏系统理论刻画。

Method: 理论计算。以特征势形式化（characteristic-potential formalism）处理非线性屏蔽，用内部静电势描述与电极耦合的中心点上的非平衡电荷再分布；体系为T型双量子点耦合正常与超导电极，侧耦合点引入Fano-Andreev效应。

Result: 电压偏置下，屏蔽诱导的能级重整化产生显著的Andreev微分电导偏压不对称；纯热偏置下，增强点间耦合削弱能级重整化并抑制准粒子热电势响应，使热电压与非线性Seebeck系数降低；电压与热偏置同时施加时，线性区外出现有限亚能隙交叉热电响应。

Conclusion: 该交叉热电响应的符号由能量依赖输运贡献（受Fano-Andreev效应修正）与非线性屏蔽贡献之间的竞争决定，进而决定热偏置是增强还是抑制低偏压Andreev电流。非线性屏蔽与Fano-Andreev效应是调控此类器件热电性能的关键因素。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of weakly nonlinear electric and thermoelectric transport through a T-shaped double quantum dot coupled to normal and superconducting leads. Nonlinear screening is treated within the characteristic-potential formalism, in which an internal electrostatic potential accounts for nonequilibrium charge redistribution on the lead-coupled central dot. The side-coupled dot induces the Fano-Andreev effect, which, together with nonlinear screening, significantly modifies the transport properties. Under voltage bias, the screening-induced level renormalization produces a pronounced bias asymmetry in the Andreev differential conductance. In response to a pure thermal bias, increasing the interdot coupling weakens the level renormalization and suppresses the quasiparticle thermoelectric response, leading to reduced thermovoltage and nonlinear Seebeck coefficient. When voltage and thermal biases are applied simultaneously, a finite subgap cross thermoelectric response emerges beyond the linear regime. The sign of this response is governed by the competition between the energy-dependent transport contribution, modified by the Fano-Andreev effect, and the nonlinear screening contribution, thereby determining whether a thermal bias enhances or suppresses the low-bias Andreev current.

</details>


### [5] [Momentum-Space Mismatch Enables Non-Hermitian Valley Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15036)
*Kai Shao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出以“体系带接触节点与耗散库之间的动量空间失配”作为普适机制，实现节点选择性非厄米趋肤效应，从而获得近100%的谷极化输运，并在1D二聚体链与2D Dirac谷体系中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谷过滤器多针对特定材料或晶格几何设计，缺乏普适性。作者希望寻找一种不依赖具体材料/晶格的共同微观要素来实现谷选择性输运。

Method: 将体系与耗散库耦合，把节点与库之间的动量失配编码进推迟自能，产生节点依赖的耗散与不同的准粒子寿命；构造有效非厄米哈密顿量，分析两节点处相反的point-gap谱绕数，得到节点分辨的非厄米趋肤效应；在最小1D二聚体链及2D π-flux 矩形与蜂窝晶格中做输运计算。

Result: 两节点呈相反谱绕数与强不对称耗散率：一节点快速衰减入库，另一节点在器件中存活，输出电流近乎谷极化；所有模型输运计算给出接近100%的谷极化，且可栅压调控、对无序与边缘缺陷鲁棒。

Conclusion: 动量空间失配是一种无需材料特化的通用机制，可实现非厄米谷过滤，为谷电子学器件设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Valley-selective transport is central to valleytronics, but existing valley filters are largely tailored to specific materials or lattice geometries, motivating the search for a common microscopic ingredient. Here we identify the momentum-space mismatch between the band-touching nodes of a system and its dissipative reservoir as a route to node-selective non-Hermitian skin effects (NHSE). When the two are coupled, the mismatch is encoded in the retarded self-energy, producing node-dependent dissipation and contrasting quasiparticle lifetimes. The resulting effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian exhibits opposite point-gap spectral winding at the two nodes, giving rise to a node-resolved NHSE with strongly asymmetric dissipation rates: one node decays rapidly into the reservoir while the other survives over the device, yielding a nearly valley-polarized outgoing current. We demonstrate this mechanism in a minimal one-dimensional dimer chain and in two-dimensional systems hosting genuine Dirac valleys, including $π$-flux rectangular and honeycomb lattices. In all cases, transport calculations yield nearly 100\% valley polarization, while remaining gate-tunable and robust against disorder and edge imperfections. Our results show that momentum-space mismatch provides a route to non-Hermitian valley filtering.

</details>


### [6] [Magnon Chirality Selection at Equal Linewidths in Open Collinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15113)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在空间关联损耗与相干交换共存的无源共线模式网络中，损耗可使具有相同线宽的共振中选择单一磁振子手性，初始无极化混合态可达26.9%极化且保留29.9%布居；联合自旋-晶体旋转可反转该信号并交换强损耗下的奇异轮廓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为在等线宽的共振之间，损耗无法区分手性自由度；本文旨在探究纯耗散机制能否在对称性允许的简并子空间中选择磁振子手性，并厘清"极点约束"与"制备/本征矢/物理库约束"两类限制的区分。

Method: 构建无源共线模式网络，利用空间关联的损耗与相干交换使相反角动量获得不同的存活概率；引入非厄米共线自旋-空间小群作用组织手性-复频率平面上的允许极点配对；以等谱正常对照实验验证非厄米性；通过联合自旋与晶体旋转检验信号的反对称性；并设计第二种实现，在总绕数为零时于相对的对角边缘分离共轭手性扇区。

Result: 初始无极化混合物实现26.9%极化、剩余布居29.9%；等谱正常对照无净极化；自旋+晶体旋转同时反转信号符号并交换强损耗下的奇异（例外）轮廓；两种读出方案分别对应极点约束与制备、本征矢及物理库约束。

Conclusion: 非厄米损耗结构可在等线宽条件下通过与相干交换的协同实现手性选择，且该效应可由对称群框架系统描述；该工作提供了区分极点半相关约束与态制备/库相关约束的实验判据。

Abstract: Loss can select one magnon chirality even when all resonances have the same linewidth. We construct a passive collinear mode network in which spatially correlated loss and coherent exchange give opposite angular momenta different survival probabilities. An initially unpolarized mixture reaches 26.9\% polarization with 29.9\% of the population remaining; an isospectral normal control gives none. A combined spin and crystal rotation reverses the signal and exchanges the exceptional contours reached at stronger loss. We organize the allowed pole pairings by the action of a non-Hermitian collinear spin-space little group on chirality and complex frequency. A second realization separates conjugate chirality sectors at opposite diagonal edges despite zero total winding. The two readouts distinguish constraints on poles from constraints on preparations, eigenvectors, and physical reservoirs.

</details>


### [7] [Magnetically Compensated Spin-Polarized Edge States in Coplanar Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15134)
*Dinghui Wang,Lulu Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出边界对称性判据：在共面交换磁体中，允许的偶宇称边缘自旋空间等于允许的均匀磁化空间，从而对称性保护的磁补偿在垂直于磁矩平面的方向上选择出奇宇称自旋织构，并给出六类极大边缘自旋扇区及电学读出方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性补偿（自旋简并）的体态层通常被认为无自旋极化，但边界可能释放自旋极化电子态；论文旨在建立一套对称性判据，从根本上说明何种边界能够释放何种宇称的自旋织构，并把磁补偿与边缘平衡自旋织构及电学读出联系起来。

Method: 基于共面交换磁体的对称性分析，推导“允许的偶宇称边缘自旋空间＝允许的均匀磁化空间”这一判据；据此将可能的边缘织构归入六个极大扇区；用同一交换模型的正交切割分别实现奇宇称与偶宇称扇区；并以 FeSe 自旋涡旋与 NiI₂ 周期四螺旋的 Wannier 模型作为材料实例，计算半无限边界的电学响应。

Result: 对称性保护的补偿使奇宇称自旋织构垂直于磁矩平面；六类极大扇区组织了所有可能的边缘织构；在材料模型中，奇宇称边缘表现为垂直方向的自旋积累，偶宇称边缘表现为面内自旋流响应，二者构成互补的电学特征。

Conclusion: 边界对称性将磁补偿与平衡自旋织构及电学自旋读出相连接，为在补偿磁体边缘实现并探测自旋极化态提供了对称性框架与可操作的材料判据。

Abstract: A boundary can release spin-polarized electronic states from a magnetically compensated, spin-degenerate layer. We establish the symmetry criterion governing this release in coplanar exchange magnets: the allowed even-parity edge-spin space equals the allowed uniform-magnetization space. Symmetry-protected compensation therefore selects an odd spin texture perpendicular to the moment plane. Six maximal sectors organize the possible edge textures. Orthogonal cuts of one exchange model realize odd and even sectors, while FeSe spin-vortex and NiI$_2$ period-four spiral Wannier models provide material realizations. Their semi-infinite boundaries exhibit complementary electrical responses: perpendicular spin accumulation at the odd edge and in-plane spin-current response at the even edge. Boundary symmetry thus connects magnetic compensation to equilibrium spin textures and electrical spin readout.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum-Metric-Induced Distinct Optoelectronic Responses in Triple $M$ Valleys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15145)
*Yongliang Song,Shuxian Sun,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用量子度规诱导的光电响应使三重M谷变得可区分，实现非线性谷霍尔效应与光激发谷极化霍尔输运，并在反铁磁MoI3双层中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谷电子学主要聚焦于性质互补的K±谷，而M谷缺乏可靠操控手段，导致大量M谷材料未被开发利用。作者希望将谷电子学从二元K±谷拓展到三重M谷。

Method: 紧束缚计算：通过调节电场取向，使任意一对M谷产生方向相反的非线性霍尔电流，从而实现非线性谷霍尔效应；并利用线偏振光选择性激发单个M谷，驱动光激发谷极化霍尔输运。第一性原理计算：在反铁磁MoI3双层中验证上述量子度规诱导的光电响应。

Result: 三种M谷可通过量子度规诱导的光电响应加以区分；电场取向可调控成对M谷的非线性霍尔电流反向；线偏振光可实现单谷选择性激发；反铁磁MoI3双层具有显著、可输运测量的非线性谷霍尔电导。

Conclusion: 研究将谷电子学范围从常规二元K±谷扩展到三重M谷，为设计具有多量子态、高可调性的先进光电器件提供途径。

Abstract: While $K_{\pm}$ valleys with contrasting optoelectronic properties have been the main focus of valleytronics, the lack of reliable manipulation methods for $M$ valleys leaves numerous $M$-valley materials untapped. Here, we discover that three flavors of $M$ valleys become distinguishable via quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses. The tight-binding calculations demonstrate that by tuning the electric-field orientation, opposite nonlinear Hall currents from any pair of $M$ valleys can be chosen to realize the nonlinear valley Hall effect. Moreover, linearly polarized light allows for preferential excitation of individual $M$ valleys, driving photoexcited valley-polarized Hall transport. Using first-principles calculations, we further validate the quantum-metric-induced optoelectronic responses in antiferromagnetic $\mathrm{MoI}_3$ bilayers, which exhibit a significant nonlinear valley Hall conductivity amenable to transport measurements. Our findings extend the scope of valleytronics from conventional binary $K_{\pm}$ valleys to triple $M$ valleys and pave the way for designing advanced optoelectronic devices with multiple quantum states of high tunability.

</details>


### [9] [Interface-Driven Remote-Surface States in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15192)
*Pierre A. Pantaleon,Alejandro Jimeno-Pozo,Francisco Guinea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 菱方多层石墨烯两个外表面存在低能态，作者提出逐层扩展机制：一端表面的低能态波函数逐层展宽并向远端表面转移归一化权重；该规律同样支配圆形空腔与锯齿边缘的亚晶格极化零模，并在周期性莫尔界面下逐谐波独立作用，使低能带层极化依赖Bloch动量与调制周期，从而解释近期实验观测到的远端表面调制。


<details>

At periodic moire interfaces, it acts separately on each momentum harmonic, making the layer polarization of the low-energy bands depend on Bloch momentum and modulation period. This provides an intrinsic single-particle explanation for the remote-surface modulations observed in recent multilayer graphene experiments.

Abstract: Structural chirality is ubiquitous in magnets, yet its impact on spin transport in insulators remains largely unexplored, with only a few recent experiments offering the first glimpses. Here we predict the chiral response in the electric spin injection-detection configuration in both ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic spirals. The screw symmetry of the spiral forbids any nonzero diagonal element of the magnon spin matrix: every eigenmode is linearly polarized and carries no spin, so spin transport is mediated entirely by superposition between distinct modes. Reversing chirality flips the magnon spin along the spiral axis and the injected quantum coherence in lockstep, leaving the associated injected spin current invariant. Spiral chirality acts instead at the detection interface, where electron--magnon scattering amplitude is reversed and causes the magnon accumulation to be read out as a voltage of opposite sign. This interfacial readout is consistent with reversed first-harmonic voltage under electric-field switching in multiferroic BiFeO$_3$, establishing structural chirality as an intrinsic control parameter for magnon coherence and its electrical readout.

Abstract: Chiral molecules adsorbed on a magnetic film interact with its magnetization and can thereby alter the thermal motion of magnetic skyrmions. Here we develop a theoretical model in which an aligned layer of tilted molecules modifies the interfacial magnetic interactions according to its handedness. Within this model a skyrmion responds only to spatial variations of the coupling, so a uniform layer leaves its position unaffected, whereas boundaries and coverage gradients exert forces on it. Near a chiral region, these forces deflect the skyrmion transversely in a direction that reverses between enantiomers, which provides a direct signature of the chiral interaction. In extended periodic patterns, the same forces reshape the long-time diffusion, suppressing it in the direction in which the potential varies and enhancing it in the perpendicular direction. The net effect on the mean diffusion is set by the balance between the gyrotropic and dissipative responses of the skyrmion, as we show exactly for one-dimensional landscapes. Stochastic micromagnetic simulations and collective-coordinate calculations confirm these predictions and show how molecular orientation, spatial patterning, and skyrmion properties together determine the motion.

Abstract: Magnetic domain walls (DWs) are central to emerging spintronic technologies for memory, logic and neuromorphic computing, where their controlled displacement is a key functionality. While current-induced DW motion driven by spin-orbit torques is well established under direct currents, it remains unclear how radio-frequency (RF) currents couple to DWs and whether they can generate directed motion under a symmetric periodic drive. Here we show that RF currents drive DW oscillations, which can be rectified into directed motion through symmetry breaking. Using time-resolved and static magneto-optical Kerr microscopy, we demonstrate that RF spin-orbit torques efficiently excite low-frequency DW oscillations well below the ferromagnetic resonance frequency. Whereas these oscillations remain symmetric in the absence of perturbations, symmetry breaking by a transverse magnetic field produces self-propulsion, while symmetry breaking by an initial current pulse gives rise to sustained DW motion. We further show that in initially uniform magnetic states, RF-driven expansion of a pulse-generated edge domain ultimately results in efficient RF-assisted magnetic switching. Together, these results establish a general framework for understanding how RF-driven DW oscillations are rectified into directed motion, providing new opportunities for their high-frequency control.

</details>

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases remain a leading cause of mortality worldwide, highlighting the need for rapid, sensitive and reliable detection of cardiac injury at the point of care. Here, we develop a dual-sensor platform that combines an aptamer-functionalized molybdenum disulfide (MoS2) field-effect transistor (2D-FET) with a graphene-based ion-sensitive field-effect transistor (ISFET) for the detection of cardiac troponin I (Trop-I) in complex biological matrices. The atomically thin MoS2 channel enables highly sensitive detection of Trop-I, achieving a limit of detection of 0.001 ng mL-1. However, the high ionic strength of serum substantially attenuates the field-effect response, resulting in approximately 80% degradation of the sensor response. To address this matrix effect, the ISFET is employed as a secondary sensor to characterize the ionic environment of the sample. Machine-learning-assisted integration of the 2D-FET and ISFET responses is investigated as a strategy for compensating serum-induced signal distortion and recovering key electrical figures of merit extracted from the 2D-FET transfer characteristics, including maximum on-state current (Ids_max), subthreshold slope (SS) and transconductance (gm). This dual-sensor architecture combines molecular recognition, high analytical sensitivity and matrix-aware electrical signal correction, providing a potential pathway towards more reliable FET-based biomarker detection in complex biological fluids. The approach may further enable multiplexed detection of cardiac biomarkers through incorporation of target-specific aptamers, offering potential for broader assessment of cardiac stress and myocardial injury risk.

</details>

Boltzmann theory predicts that the rank $m=2,4,6$ of the first nonzero spin-odd velocity moment sets the leading angular harmonic, its asymptotic radial dependence $r^{2-m}$, and its amplitude scaling $τ^{m-3}$ at fixed distance and injected current. For $g$-wave order, the fourth moment produces a fourth angular harmonic with an asymptotic $r^{-2}$ tail. Quantum transport simulations support the radial profiles predicted by finite-geometry Boltzmann theory without an amplitude fit. Across the three symmetries, the quantum amplitudes exceed the Boltzmann predictions based on the Born scattering time by $15-65$\% in the windows analyzed. In a two-contact ribbon, we detect the $g$-wave fourth harmonic at $6.8\,σ$ in an independent set of 1800 disorder realizations using a preselected radial window. Its amplitude decreases when the altermagnetic splitting is reduced.

The resulting contrast converts the band-effective ferroelectric layer asymmetry into a shift of the $K$ edge, and so displaces the $K$--$Γ$ landscape: a finite-field fit that does not impose the rule leaves the $K$ coupling consistent with zero, and the shift reaches \polshiftdft~meV, the most that asymmetry can give a single valley. Reversing the polarization mirrors the valley landscape in displacement field, so the two registries have equal and opposite differential transconductance $g_d$ at zero field. In the ideal symmetric limit a second parity holds about each registry's own compensation field and places a zero of $g_d$ there. The valley-dependent screening calculated here displaces that zero by only \gdnulllow\% at the read point, so device asymmetry limits the accuracy. The result links a known stacking selection rule to ferroelectric valley transduction, with a quantitative valley shift and a directly testable transport signature.

Abstract: Frustrated lattices with exactly flat bands and compact localized states provide an ideal setting to uncover the topology of flat bands, leading to intriguing physics in two dimensions. Using non-Hermitian breathing Kagome and checkerboard models as illustrations, we reveal how flat bands acquire a basis-independent skin effect induced by the other bands; in the breathing Kagome model it appears exactly when the flat band enters the area spanned by the periodic-boundary spectra of the dispersive bands. A flat band becomes singular when its eigenvectors hold immovable discontinuities at band crossings. Such singularity in non-Hermitian systems is sensitive to boundary conditions and unprotected by band flatness. Non-Bloch topology underlying is uncovered in a bulk-boundary correspondence with the formation of a singular band touching in the bulk and a robust flat band skin mode on the boundary. We present a geometric approach to construct this boundary skin mode from a macroscopic sum of compact localized skin modes and trace its topological origin to two linearly independent noncontractible loop states on a non-Bloch torus. To characterize the correspondence, an invariant is proposed based on biorthogonal polarization of the zero-energy corner mode. Breaking exact flatness, we further realize nearly flat non-Bloch Chern bands.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 从 Peierls-Boltzmann 输运方程出发，为温度场与声子漂移场的耦合动力学推导等效薛定谔方程，将微观声子散射性质映射为非厄米动力学转变；并由解析推导给出流体力学输运存在的判据，即以第一性原理声子性质为输入。

Result: 一对奇异点定量给出流体力学窗口的下、上边界；解析判据表明，仅当有效热扩散率超过声子动量扩散率时，有限流体力学窗口才存在；理论准确复现实验观测的随温度和长度变化的超快第二声动力学。

Abstract: Phonon hydrodynamics enables collective heat flow beyond the classical diffusive limit and offers a promising route to controlling thermal transport in low-dimensional materials and nanoscale devices. However, quantitative criteria for determining when this hydrodynamic state emerges and where its boundaries lie remain a longstanding challenge in condensed matter physics. Here, we show that the onset of phonon hydrodynamics can be formulated as a non-Hermitian dynamical transition that converts microscopic phonon scattering properties into predictive boundaries for wavelike heat transport. Starting from the Peierls-Boltzmann transport equation, we derive an effective Schrodinger equation for the coupled dynamics of temperature and phonon drift fields, allowing the hydrodynamic regime to be identified directly from material transport properties. Within this framework, a pair of exceptional points quantitatively define the lower and upper boundaries of the hydrodynamic window, providing a critical design criterion beyond the qualitative scattering-rate inequalities proposed by Guyer and Krumhansl six decades ago. Furthermore, we derive an analytical criterion for the existence of hydrodynamic transport, showing that a finite hydrodynamic window can be supported only when the effective thermal diffusivity exceeds the phononic momentum diffusivity. Using ab initio phonon properties as inputs, our theory accurately captures the experimentally observed temperature- and length-dependent ultrafast second-sound dynamics. These results establish a symmetry-based framework for understanding collective energy transport and provide quantitative design principles for identifying the materials, temperatures, and characteristic length scales that support wavelike heat transport in low-dimensional materials and functional devices.

Abstract: We report the growth and transport properties of GaAs1-xSbx/InAs core-shell nanowires. Compared with the well-studied GaAs-InAs system, incorporating Sb substantially reduces the lattice mismatch between the core and shell. Accordingly, both the nanowire crystal quality and device performance improved significantly. Increasing x from 0.15 to 0.35, the density of crystalline defects, such as stacking faults in the InAs shell, decreases by half. Moreover, the field-effect mobility increases from 3850 cm2/Vs to 4800 cm2/Vs, consistent with the reduction of the lattice mismatch. The incorporation of Sb makes the nanowire easier to pinch off via the gate voltage, enhancing the electric field tunability over the nanowire. Magneto-conductance measurements reveal Aharonov-Bohm-like oscillations, suggesting a phase-coherent tubular InAs shell. Our results identify lattice mismatch as a critical disorder source in core-shell nanowires and provide a practical route toward low-disorder core-shell quantum devices.

Abstract: Semiconductor InSb nanowires are a major material candidate for topological quantum computation. The nanowires in current devices were mostly grown using metal organic chemical vapor deposition, incompatible with in situ growth of superconductor, a crucial requirement for low-disorder devices. Growth of InSb nanowires using molecular-beam epitaxy (MBE) can address this issue. So far, studies on MBE-grown InSb remain limited due to an extremely narrow parameter window for the growth and reproducibility. Moreover, the use of foreign catalysts can introduce deep-level impurities. Here, we report a self-catalyzed MBE growth of InSb nanowires without those foreign catalysts. We further find that a low flux suppresses radial growth, enabling linear length tuning from 0.8 to 2.6 micrometer with growth time, while diameter stays nearly constant. These InSb nanowires have a pure zinc-blende structure free of twins, stacking faults. Transport measurement reveals an electron mobility near 19000 cm2 V-1s-1. Moreover, the observation of quasi-ballistic transport, quantum dots, and Kondo correlations demonstrates the coherent quantum transport capability of the MBE-grown InSb nanowires. Our work provides a substrate-independent, self-catalyzed route for the controllable synthesis of high-quality InSb nanowires, establishing a robust material platform for high-performance InSb-based electronic and quantum devices.

Abstract: Sliding ferroelectricity offers a non-volatile route to electrically programmable electronic and optical functionality, but deterministic control of excitonic photoluminescence is often obscured by moiré reconstruction and domain averaging. Here we study an artificially assembled WSe$_2$ bilayer with near-zero twist in rhombohedral stacking and observe reversible, hysteretic electrical switching through excitonic photoluminescence. Under an out-of-plane electric field, momentum-indirect QK exciton emission exhibits Stark-slope reversals and abrupt energy jumps, resolving a four-stage sequence separated by two band realignments and one ferroelectric switching event. Polarization reversal also selects the momentum-direct KK interlayer-exciton branch that hybridizes with an intralayer exciton, producing history-dependent photoluminescence. Ferroelectric modulation of resonant Raman scattering provides a complementary readout, with hysteretic intensities correlated with the polarization-dependent interlayer field. These results establish an exciton-based quantification of the electronic structure in ferroelectric WSe$_2$ and open a route to electrically programmable optoelectronic functionality.

*Alex Krasnok*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明反射符号 $Q$ 计数无反射态：当复能平面下半平面有奇数个无反射态时 $Q=-1$；在有限纳米线中这意味着两个 Majorana 模的劈裂小于其与电极的耦合；等耦合条件下 $Q$ 每次变号（含体能拓扑相变）对应两个无反射态合并的奇异点，零温下 $Q=-1$ 当且仅当零偏压电导超过 $e^2/h$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Majorana 模的输运证据（零偏压电导峰）可被普通束缚态模仿，其是否被观测仍有争议。长线中零能电子反射符号 $Q$ 标记拓扑相，但 $Q$ 在有限器件中究竟"认证"了什么此前不清楚。

Method: 解析研究有限超导纳米线（含两端接触）的散射矩阵，将零能反射符号 $Q$ 与复能平面上无反射态（从一端进入、完全从另一端离开的波）的数目联系起来；分析等耦合电极下 $Q$ 变号与奇异点（exceptional point）的对应关系，并给出 $Q$ 与零偏压电导的定量判据。

Result: 首次表明 $Q$ 计数无反射态：$Q=-1$ 当且仅当下半复能平面内无反射态数为奇数。有限线中这等价于两个 Majorana 模劈裂小于其与接触的耦合。等耦合下 $Q$ 的每次变号（包括体能拓扑相变）均为两态合并、透射最大平坦的奇异点。零温下 $Q=-1$ 恰对应于接触处测得的零偏压电导超过 $e^2/h$。

Conclusion: 该工作厘清了有限 Majorana 器件中输运可"认证"的内容，并给出从电导读数直接提取拓扑不变量的方法，为区分真 Majorana 信号与平庸束缚态提供判据。

Abstract: Majorana modes, zero-energy excitations of superconducting wires that are their own antiparticles, could store quantum information protected from local noise. In transport experiments, however, ordinary bound states mimic their signature, a conductance peak at zero voltage, and whether Majorana modes have been observed remains debated. A sign, $Q$, of the electron reflection at zero energy marks the topological phase of a long wire, but what $Q$ certifies in a finite device has been unclear. Here we show for the first time that $Q$ counts reflectionless states, waves that enter through one contact and leave entirely through the other: $Q=-1$ when an odd number of them lie in the lower half of the complex energy plane. In a finite wire this means that the two Majorana modes are split by less than their coupling to the contacts. With equal contacts, every sign change of $Q$, including the bulk topological transition, is an exceptional point at which two such states merge and transmission is maximally flat. At zero temperature, $Q=-1$ exactly when the zero-bias conductance measured at a contact exceeds $e^2/h$. These results show what transport can certify about a Majorana device and how to read the invariant from conductance.

</details>

Abstract: Two-dimensional electron gases in terahertz cavities are among the most mature platforms for ultrastrong light--matter coupling, yet their collective response is captured by the linear Hopfield model, which has an exact classical correspondence. The precise adjustment of self-compression dynamics vis-a-vis the desired compressed pulse output is, however, not yet delegable to AI agents, as the criteria for such search are still largely subjective, operating with fine aspects of subcycle field structure, not yet formalizable as clear search-stopping instructions. An ultimate precision in such subcycle field engineering is attainable via a management of field-phase-sensitive nonlinear processes, enabling pulse self-compression to high-power subcycle field waveforms and opening paths toward fiber-optic attosecond lightwave engineering.

Abstract: A vector spherical wave expansion (VSWE) is used to engineer an elemental multipole source whose radiated field is a superdirective, extremely unidirectional (SDEU) needle beam. Its radiated field is linearly polarized with an azimuthally symmetric magnitude; its back lobe vanishes identically in the far field. The unidirectional behavior is quantified at any finite far-field radius by a closed-form front-to-back ratio, obtained from an exact per-degree cancellation of the electric and magnetic multipole contributions in the backward direction. Uniform circular arrays (UCAs) of these elements inherit the SDEU behavior. Driving the arrays with zero-area pulses (ZAPs) transfers the frequency-domain performance intact into the time domain. It is shown that whether the radiated energy beam is narrower or wider than the corresponding intensity beam is decided by a single property of the radiated waveform, not by the size of the radiating aperture. A simple single-cycle ZAP is used to quantify the performance of the UCAs; its energy beam is the narrower one. The same criterion is shown to govern the focal spot of near-field-focused pulse-driven arrays. A custom-designed intensity-enhancement (IE) ZAP is introduced that raises the focal intensity by almost 200-fold over a narrow-band reference while producing a focal spot more than ten times smaller in both the axial and transverse directions, deeply subwavelength with respect to its own effective wavelength. It is demonstrated that these stunning IE results are produced simply with an array of pulse-excited Huygens dipole sources. These results identify the pulse shape, rather than the aperture, as the design variable that governs localized electromagnetic energy and information delivery.

Abstract: Compact, high-performance ultra-stable lasers that can operate outside the laboratory remain a long-standing goal. Here we report, to our knowledge, the most compact field-deployable ultra-stable laser, packaged in a standard 2U rack chassis. It is stabilized to a 50 mm-long, 19 mL optical reference cavity with low vibration sensitivity by optoelectronic oscillator (OEO) locking, an approach particularly suited to compact, transportable systems. The laser delivers an integrated linewidth of about 0.42 Hz and a fractional frequency stability of about 5E-15 at 1 s averaging time (about 2.2E-15 at 0.4 s). Measured phase noise approaches the cavity thermal-noise limit below 100 Hz and the theoretical OEO-locking limit from 100 Hz to 100 kHz. The system volume is roughly one quarter of the volume of previously reported integrated, transportable ultra-stable lasers of comparable performance, making it well suited for field applications in time-frequency metrology, quantum technologies, and environmental sensing.

Abstract: We develop an analytic T-matrix formalism for two-dimensional circular acoustic Helmholtz resonators with thin rigid walls. Abstract: Plasmonic metasurfaces enable strong nonlinear optical responses due to sub-wavelength field confinement, yet their performance is fundamentally limited by low laser-induced damage thresholds (LIDT). Motivation: 3D纳米光子增材制造带来了极大的几何自由度，但要在巨大的设计空间中高效寻优并同时满足制造约束，现有逆向设计方法（依赖伴随法梯度优化）存在局限：需要求解伴随方程、难以处理不可微或导数不可得的制造约束（如结构连通性），只能采用可微近似。

Result: 紧凑的水平集表示使无梯度优化在全三维纳米光子器件上变得可行，无需伴随计算；可配合任意Maxwell求解器或导数不可得/难以获得的辅助算法，并支持高度并行的“embarrassingly parallel”工作流；在Y型分束器优化中，严格保证了材料与空隙的结构完整性（连通性）。

Conclusion: 无梯度LSM为3D增材制造纳米光子器件提供了一种灵活、可并行的逆向设计框架，能够严格施加二值化制造约束，避免梯度优化中常用的可微近似，扩展了可用求解器与约束算法的范围。

Abstract: Advances in 3D nanophotonic additive manufacturing enable increasingly complex, almost free-form devices for manipulating light at sub-wavelength scales. This compact representation makes gradient-free optimization practical for fully 3D nanophotonic devices, eliminating the need for adjoint calculations. This unlocks the use of any Maxwell solver or auxiliary algorithms that should express fabrication constraints for which the required derivatives are unavailable or impractical to obtain, and enables embarrassingly parallel workflows. Consequently, binary fabrication requirements, such as structural connectivity, can be rigorously enforced, avoiding the differentiable approximations commonly used in gradient-based optimization. Here, we demonstrate the combination of the gradient-free LSM with a connected-component analysis algorithm to enforce the structural integrity of the design. We demonstrate the applicability of the gradient-free LSM by optimizing a Y-splitter while strictly enforcing the structural integrity of the material and the void in the design.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In conventional optical multiplexing, $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~orthogonal spectral or spatial modes multiplex $N_{\mathrm{f}}$ independent data streams transmitted over an optical channel (e.g., a multimode optical fiber supporting $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~guided modes). We introduce here a new optical communications scheme that we call `Stokes communications' (S-Comm), whereby $N_{\mathrm{D}}=N_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}-1$ independent data streams are multiplexed into $N_{\mathrm{f}}$~optical modes, potentially yielding a dramatic increase in the channel capacity. Rather than modulating the amplitude of each mode to encode a data stream, we make use of multimode partially coherent light and modulate each generalized Stokes parameter in the $N_{\mathrm{f}}\times N_{\mathrm{f}}$ Hermitian coherence matrix associated with the field. We present here designs for the S-Comm transmitter and receiver making use of bimodal fields ($N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$) in which $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams are encoded via on-off keying and multiplexed in two modes. We describe the main blocks in the S-Comm transmitter: coherence modulators that encode a data stream in the degree of coherence of a bimode (a two-mode partially coherent field), Stokes encoders that assign each data stream to a prescribed Stokes parameter, and Stokes multiplexers that combine bimodes into physical modes. We also describe the reverse operations implemented in the S-Comm receiver to recover the data streams. Significantly higher channel capacity is expected for a larger number of physical modes, which is facilitated by the amenability of the presented designs to on-chip integration. These results suggest that the long-appreciated but unexploited higher information-carrying capacity of partially coherent light can help realize dense optical communications.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Partially coherent light has a higher informational-carrying capacity than coherent light when both fields are spanned by the same modes, which implies that partially coherent light may increase the channel density in optical communications. We have proposed in an accompanying paper a realization of S-Comm for bimodal fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}=2$ that multiplex $N_{\mathrm{D}}=3$ data streams. Here we extend this proposed strategy for S-Comm to multimode fields $N_{\mathrm{f}}>2$. We find that the diagonal and off-diagonal Stokes parameters require different treatments and we provide detailed designs for the transmitter and receiver for both diagonally and off-diagonally multiplexed data streams. The building blocks of the proposed system can be readily implemented in integrated photonic circuit platforms. Our results indicate that on-chip processing of multimode partially coherent light (or `structured coherence') may have profound implications for optical communications and for optical signal processing in general.

Abstract: Metasurfaces come in a great variety of unit cell geometries and material composition. Abstract: The discrete dipole approximation (DDA) is a flexible numerical method for simulating electromagnetic scattering by particles of arbitrary shape and internal structure. In many applications, such as optical-database generation, the same particle geometry must be simulated for many values of the complex refractive index, which are currently handled independently with proportional increase of computational time. For homogeneous particles, however, variations of the refractive index affect only the diagonal polarizability term of the interaction matrix. The corresponding linear systems therefore differ only by scalar shifts of the identity matrix, making them suitable for shifted Krylov-subspace methods. The shifted solver reproduces the extinction cross sections obtained from independent simulations with relative differences below the iterative-method tolerance, while reducing the number of matrix-vector products by a factor of 130 for the tested configuration. We further analyze how the achievable speedup depends on the number of refractive-index values, the computational backend, and the variation of the convergence rate across the parameter space. The final wall-time acceleration for large refractive-index sweeps was demonstrated to be up to 40 times, which can be further improved by optimizing the scattered-fields calculations that became a new bottleneck.

Method: 以展望/观点（perspective）方式组织论述：综述超快光谱在量子材料研究中的原理与已覆盖参数空间（硬X射线至THz、强场非平衡激发、阿秒脉宽），据此梳理仍缺失的超快光源能力（如脉宽、光子能量、场强、重复频率、相干性/多维测量等维度的组合），并将其与具体的量子/强关联材料难题相对应。

Result: 摘要本身不报告实验数据或定量结果；其结论性产出是一份关于"缺失能力"的清单，以及对下一代超快光源可产生决定性科学影响的研究问题（outstanding problems）的识别与归类。

Conclusion: 超快光谱已成为量子材料研究的核心手段，但光源能力仍不完整；明确当前缺失的技术能力并针对性地发展下一代超快光源，将决定性地推进对量子与强关联材料中涌现序及非平衡现象的理解。

Abstract: Ultrafast spectroscopy of quantum materials has emerged as a principle application of modern ultrafast optical sources. Measurements made with ultrashort pulses allow the normally strongly coupled internal degrees of freedom inside these systems to be de-coupled in the time-domain, revealing the key interactions for the different orders that lead to dramatic emergent phenomena. This has naturally motivated a wide range of experiments, and quantum materials have now been studied with ultrafast sources spanning from the hard X-ray to the THz, with fields intense enough to create non-equilibrium states, and using pulses down to the attosecond duration. As the optical spectroscopist's toolbox becomes increasingly complete, it is worth pausing and reflecting on what are the capabilities that we currently lack for examining quantum materials, and what are the problems that require the next generation of ultrafast sources now coming on-line or being designed. Here I will offer an overview of the remaining missing capabilities in ultrafast optical sources as required for studying quantum materials, and identify some outstanding problems where I believe these new sources can provide decisive impact in our understanding of quantum and strongly-correlated materials.

Result: 网格吸附到芯层界面会使界面处场采样受浮点舍入影响；界面内外平均法使群速度色散D与机器无关，网格间稳定度约1 ps/(nm·km)。给出1550 nm（700×1000 nm，D=372.9±0.6 ps/(nm·km)）与780 nm（350×500 nm，D=196.4±0.9 ps/(nm·km)）两套空气包层设计，均含200 GHz环、临界与过耦合间隙、光栅耦合器及光纤-光纤预算。色散预测与文献两器件实测偏差在1%与4%以内；独立Lugiato-Lefever求解器基于导出参数获得稳定单孤子。

Conclusion: TARANG提供了可复现、成本可控且数值可信的端到端微梳设计流程，两套设计方案已具备流片条件，色散模型获实测验证并支持孤子仿真。

Abstract: Designing a microresonator frequency comb chip means solving a chain of coupled problems: a waveguide with anomalous dispersion that survives fabrication errors, a ring with the right free spectral range and quality factor, a bus gap that sets the coupling regime, and grating couplers, all made in one lithography step. We present TARANG, an open-source Python package that takes a silicon nitride platform from tabulated refractive indices to a GDSII mask in fourteen stages, built on the Tidy3D mode, FDTD and eigenmode-expansion solvers. Every cloud simulation is priced before it runs and capped, every result is cached by a fingerprint of the full simulation, and every quoted number carries a numerical check. We show that snapping the grid onto the core's faces, a common way to steady dispersion calculations, leaves the field samples on the faces to floating-point rounding, and that averaging solves with the grid just inside and just outside the faces makes the group-velocity dispersion $D$ independent of the computer and steady to about 1 ps/(nm km) from mesh to mesh. Two complete air-clad designs are presented, at 1550 nm ($700\times1000$ nm core, $D = 372.9 \pm 0.6$ ps/(nm km)) and at 780 nm ($350\times500$ nm, $D = 196.4 \pm 0.9$ ps/(nm km)), each with a 200 GHz ring, critical and over-coupled gaps, grating couplers, and a fibre-to-fibre budget. The predicted dispersion agrees with two measured devices from the literature to within 1 and 4%, and an independent Lugiato-Lefever solver forms stable single solitons from the exported parameters of both designs, which are ready for fabrication.

Conclusion: 该框架为光子 tape-out 前的精度约束下 Pareto 优化提供了可扩展、可复现的基础。

Abstract: Photonic neural accelerators offer ultra-low latency linear algebra, yet their usable numerical precision, sustainable throughput, and application accuracy remain tightly bounded by surrounding electro-optical interfaces and physical non-idealities. Pre-fabrication design-space exploration (DSE) requires a cross-layer electronic design automation (EDA) framework capable of evaluating disparate optical topologies under identical neural workloads and physical assumptions. We present \textbf{PISim}, a unified Photonic Inference Simulator that couples pretrained deep neural network execution with physics-based models of six accelerator architecture families, six electro-optic modulator technologies, four optical source organizations, and direct or coherent readout schemes. PISim embeds stage-resolved impairment operators---covering control and analog-to-digital quantization, finite extinction ratio, shot noise, correlated relative-intensity noise (RIN), thermal drift, propagation loss, and channel crosstalk---alongside an analytical cycle-level interface timing model to expose electronic-optical bottlenecks. Using simulated MNIST multilayer perceptron (MLP) benchmarks, we demonstrate how PISim identifies optimal precision operating points under multi-impairment stress (peaking at 6 bits for Mach--Zehnder and microring modulators), quantifies a 52.5 percentage-point accuracy gap between VCSEL-array and parallel-fanout laser architectures arising, under the modeled conditions, from power partitioning and correlated source noise, and models interface-limited throughput scaling across emitter-array widths from 32 to 128 channels. This comprehensive framework establishes an extensible, reproducible foundation for accuracy-constrained Pareto optimization prior to photonic tape-out.

Abstract: We investigate multiphoton absorption-driven visible luminescence in a thulium-doped aluminosilicate optical fiber excited by an ultrafast 1030-nm femtosecond pulse train in the {MHz domain}. By combining calibrated spectral band integration with a structure-informed rate-equation model based on host thermodynamics, we show that the visible spectra evolution is governed by non-radiative multi-phonon transfer and phonon-assisted cross-relaxation activated by cumulative core heating, rather than permanent color-center solarization. Abstract: Inferring the three-dimensional configuration of a flexible optical fiber from a single accessible end remains a fundamental challenge in photonic sensing. Existing approaches typically require specialised multicore fibers, coherent interferometry, or distributed spectral encoding. Here, we demonstrate a fundamentally different sensing paradigm in which fiber geometry is reconstructed from the spatiotemporal dynamics of returning single photons. Using a laser-engineered multimode fiber interrogated by a time-resolved 32x32 single-photon avalanche diode array, we show that fiber deformation imprints unique signatures onto the distribution and arrival time of back-reflected photons. We map these high-dimensional photon-return states onto a low-dimensional geometric manifold, enabling continuous bend-angle estimation and reconstruction of previously unseen two- and three-dimensional shapes from measurements acquired entirely at the proximal fiber end. Measurements show a median pixel-wise signal-to-noise ratio of approximately 26 dB and below 1% repeatability variation. Our results establish multimode single-photon dynamics as an information-rich channel for distributed geometric sensing, introducing a new framework for single-ended fiber shape reconstruction and minimally invasive photonic navigation.

Abstract: Circularly polarized light, with its specific handedness, is a valuable tool for investigating the chirality related properties of materials, including their structural, electronic, magnetic and topological characteristics. This has led to efforts in creating wavelength-tunable coherent light with circular or elliptical polarization through a sub-cycle ultrafast process high-harmonic generation (HHG). Existing approaches to chiral HHG, however, typically rely on multidimensional driving fields, materials engineering or complex optical architectures. Here we uncover a distinct mechanism without such complexity, where a single linearly polarized driving field generates chiral HHG from a pristine crystal. Semi-conductor Bloch equations (SBEs) reveal that this ellipticity originates from the coherent superposition of multiple interband transitions, each contributing harmonics with distinct linear polarizations and relative phases. We further substantiate this mechanism by shifting the driving photon energy into the mid-infrared, which strongly suppresses the ellipticity as the number of accessible interband transition channels is reduced. These results establish a simple and general mechanism for chiral HHG, and show that quantum materials can transform an apparently achiral driving field into polarization-tunable ultrafast chiral emission.

Abstract: An all-garnet photonic-crystal substrate compatible with epitaxial growth on gadolinium gallium garnet is proposed for controlling the optomagnetic response of a single bismuth iron garnet nanodisk. ### [62] [Circuit-aware protection against burst errors in lattice surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14583)

TL;DR: 在大规模量子计算中，晶格手术期间测量类型随补丁合并/分裂而改变，为此比较三种静态保护布局；在距离9、相关背景匹配下，保护每次合并所测量的检查类型相比深层几何带可减少 $38^{+6}_{-5}\%$ 的突发诱导超额风险，并以约少 $49^{+2}_{-14}$ 个受保护辅助比特达到浅层带参考风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子计算需要能抵御一次性扰动多个超导比特的高能冲击的逻辑操作。能隙工程可抑制冲击引起的弛豫，但仅保护部分比特时须决定保护位置的选取；而晶格手术中测量随补丁合并与分裂而变化，不同辅助比特与时间边界暴露于突发错误，因此需要研究如何按电路测量结构布置保护。

Method: 对模拟的晶格手术 CNOT 比较三种静态保护布局，并控制受保护辅助比特数量及 X:Z 检查类型划分保持一致。在距离9下，采用相关背景匹配、且不向解码器提供冲击参数的设定，评估保护合并期间所测检查类型的布局；并与深层几何带及浅层带参考进行比较。

Result: 距离9时，保护每次合并所测检查类型的布局相对深层几何带使突发诱导超额风险降低 $38^{+6}_{-5}\%$；该差异归因于时间边界处的报告错误与传播至数据的辅助比特故障相互作用，从而抬升浅层带的超额风险。该按类型布局以估计少 $49^{+2}_{-14}$ 个受保护辅助比特达到浅层带参考风险。距离13时，在预言机解码下优势未能分辨；五轮突发上限会反转浅层带的排序。

Conclusion: 在所测试的模拟解码器与突发时长范围内，逻辑电路的测量结构可指导保护位置的布置。

Abstract: Large-scale quantum computing will require logical operations protected against high-energy impacts that disturb many superconducting qubits at once. Gap engineering suppresses relaxation induced by these impacts, but protecting only a subset of qubits requires choosing where that protection is placed. During lattice surgery, measurements change as patches merge and split, exposing different ancillas and temporal boundaries to burst errors. Here we compare three static protection layouts for a simulated lattice-surgery CNOT, matching the numbers of protected ancillas and the X:Z check-type splits. At distance 9, protecting the check type measured during each merge reduces burst-induced excess risk by $38^{+6}_{-5}\%$ relative to a deep geometric band under correlated background matching, without giving the decoder the impact parameters. We attribute this difference to report errors at temporal boundaries and ancilla faults propagating to data, which interact to increase the excess risk of a shallow band. We also find that the typed layout reaches the shallow-band reference risk with an estimated $49^{+2}_{-14}$ fewer protected ancillas. At distance 13, the advantage is not resolved under oracle decoding, and a five-round burst cap reverses the shallow-band ordering. Abstract: How much memory does a quantum device need to prepare systems one at a time? By comparing sequential with collective preparation, we show that under general assumptions both have the same asymptotic memory cost per output. Still, sequential preparation can require an unboundedly larger memory than collective one, and we investigate how this gap depends on the physical restrictions on the allowed interactions. Finally, for sequential protocols that repeat a fixed interaction, we show that a $d$-dimensional memory that succeeds for $d^2$ rounds will succeed forever, and that such a sequential preparation is equivalent to quantum catalysis.

Abstract: We study stable synchronization in qubit XX chains with site-dependent frequencies and exchange couplings under multi-local amplitude damping noise, extending the uniform-chain results of B. Çakmak et al., [Phys. Rev. A 114, L030202 2026] in several directions. We establish that the dark-mode energies are precisely the eigenvalues shared by all undamped segments separated by the damped sites. Generic stable synchronization of the edge qubits occurs if and only if these segments share exactly one eigenvalue and this eigenvalue is nonzero, in which case every qubit on the support of the corresponding dark mode is synchronized with locking constants independent of the initial preparation. This spectral matching condition identifies the Hamiltonian parameters that support synchronization, while the couplings to the damped sites control the relative oscillation amplitudes. We present analytical examples which show how inhomogeneity can create synchronization absent in uniform chains and turn preparation-dependent locking into generic synchronization. We also calculate the asymptotic pairwise concurrence and prove that, in the single-dark-mode regime, every pair participating in synchronized oscillations remains entangled with a nonzero, constant concurrence.

Abstract: Weak probes can record whether neighboring spins are aligned without revealing their common orientation. Reading the probes can then herald a projection of an unknown spin state onto the aligned subspace that preserves every coherence within it. We ask how often this projection succeeds when the probes are read separately or jointly. For binary pure product records and zero false positives, we derive the exact optimum over all separable measurements, and hence over all local operations and classical communication. It is the probability that one-sided local witnesses exclude every alternative, and simultaneous local measurements attain it. Joint readout can do better by accepting coherent combinations of ambiguous outcomes in which the alternatives cancel. For pairwise parity records on a connected simple graph, it beats every local strategy exactly when the graph has a loop, at all record overlaps strictly between zero and one. For general binary linear records, the condition is an irreducible dependency among at least three records. The advantage lowers the leading power of the efficiency at weak recording. On a triangle the efficiency becomes linear rather than quadratic in the witness probability, and a three-pulse coherent-light receiver captures this gain with a single click. Abstract: Quantum reinforcement learning (QRL) has emerged as a promising approach for controlling quantum systems and accelerating learning tasks with quantum resources. However, most existing QRL formulations still retain a classical reinforcement learning interface: states and actions are often encoded in computational bases and rewards are extracted through measurements. This interface is restrictive for genuinely quantum environments: choosing a measurement can discard entanglement information, while repeated intermediate measurements collapse the evolving state and require costly re-preparation. This motivates a fully QRL framework in which the agent, environment, rewards, and return accumulation are all quantum. In this paper, we take a step toward this goal and introduce a new QRL framework in which both the policy and the environment are modeled by conditional quantum channels. We then propose a quantum policy-gradient algorithm for parameterized conditional quantum channels with a trainable control basis. We also derive finite- and infinite-horizon gradient estimators using a new parameter-shift rule. This rule is needed because policy parameters are shared across repeated interaction blocks and the infinite-horizon objective is not a finite circuit; rather than shifting every occurrence and evaluating the whole trajectory, our estimator shifts only one sampled policy block. Sampling this block from a geometric distribution yields an unbiased gradient estimator under bounded rewards. Finally, we establish convergence of the resulting stochastic gradient-ascent procedure to stationary points under standard smoothness and step-size assumptions.

</details>

Conclusion: 因果网络嵌入提供了无需测量独立性假设的非经典性认证途径，MDC不等式框架计算高效、抗噪性强，适用于多结果与多体场景，并拓展了对Tsirelson界之外非局域性的理解。

Abstract: Standard Bell inequalities rely on measurement independence, the assumption that measurement settings are perfectly decoupled from the source of correlations. We demonstrate that embedding Bell scenarios into larger causal networks naturally overcomes this limitation. This embedding yields experimentally accessible lower and upper bounds on measurement dependence, the incompatibility of which certifies nonclassicality directly from data, without assuming a priori freedom of choice. We formulate a general linear-programming framework to systematically derive these measurement-dependence consistency (MDC) Bell inequalities. By bypassing standard non-convexity hurdles, this approach significantly outperforms existing techniques in computational simplicity and noise robustness. Finally, we reveal that genuine quantum nonlocality can be certified even for correlations exceeding the Tsirelson bound, and demonstrate our method's broad applicability to multi-outcome and multipartite Bell tests.

Abstract: Cellular communication is done over an imperfectly predictable multimode environment, where the sender only knows about the distribution of possible intermode coupling and scattering a signal faces. It may thus seem surprising that such communication is efficient. Telatar proved that the sender's lack of exact knowledge about the channel, on average, does not significantly hinder multimode communication. As the number $N$ of modes increases at fixed signal-to-noise ratio, the average capacity of a classical multimode fading channel grows linearly in $N$, like that of $N$ decoupled single-mode channels. We study the quantum analogue in a Haar-random passive bosonic network and prove a similar scaling for unassisted quantum information transmission. Suppose that the sender drives a fixed fraction of the input modes and the receiver collects more than half of the output modes. Then the quantum capacity of such channels at zero temperature grows linearly with the number of modes at fixed photon number per mode, just like the classical capacity grows with the number of antennas. At nonzero temperature, the Haar-averaged coherent information of the isotropic thermal input bounds the capacity from below, and its value per mode converges to an integral against Wachter's law. We numerically identify positive achievable rates in models motivated by cryogenic microwave networks and room-temperature multimode fibers.

### [73] [Evaluating Quantum Circuit Protection Methods After Compilation: An Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14919)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在编译优化（optimisation level 3）之后统一评估多种量子电路混淆方案，发现多数方案的结构伪装在编译中失效，并提出Q-MaskCut组合方案以同时隐藏结构与输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子云服务商可读取用户提交的电路及其输出，因此需要电路隐藏/混淆技术；但现有方法通常只在电路"书写原样"上评估，未考虑编译优化会破坏保护效果。论文主张保护效果必须在编译后、以及服务商二次编译后重新评估。

Method: 在统一协议下比较三种ObfusQate方案（inverse、composite、delayed gates）、ECQCO与Q-MaskCut，所有受保护电路先以优化级别3编译再测量。Q-MaskCut为已有技术的组合：一次性密钥、诱饵线、带随机旋转对的电路切割、编译后的Pauli twirling与动力学解耦掩蔽。实验包括：11个3–19比特电路的无噪声与两种噪声模型仿真；23个3–127比特电路的编译结构与执行时间测量；以及对服务商二次编译的鲁棒性测试。

Result: Composite与delayed gates在编译后丧失大部分结构伪装且输出可读；inverse gates仅通过使执行时间延长7–112倍来维持较高的归一化图编辑距离（normGED）；ECQCO的输出用作者提供的实现无法解码。Q-MaskCut可隐藏输出（无密钥时平均总变差距离0.94），无噪声且片段概率精确时可精确解码；其编译后normGED为0.88，其中大部分来自掩蔽、二次编译后被移除，剩余0.42来自密钥、诱饵与挂锁。含噪声时其保真度与原电路相当；仿真中每电路需7–91个片段变体（ibm_marrakesh实机运行7–61个）并进行经典重构。

Conclusion: 多数量子电路混淆方案在编译优化下失效，仅靠结构伪装或高开销维持保护；Q-MaskCut在隐藏输出上有效且噪声下保真度可接受，但结构保护经二次编译后明显削弱，并带来片段变体开销与经典重构代价。保护方案应在编译后电路上评估，并在服务商再次编译后复评。

Abstract: Quantum cloud providers can read the circuits they receive and their outputs, yet methods for hiding a circuit are usually evaluated on the circuit as written, before compilation optimises it. We compare three ObfusQate schemes (inverse, composite and delayed gates), ECQCO and Q-MaskCut under one protocol that compiles every protected circuit at optimisation level 3 before measuring it; the goal is to keep the provider from learning the user's algorithm and answer. Q-MaskCut is one example combination of existing techniques: a one-time-pad key, decoy wires, circuit cutting with a random rotation pair across each cut, and Pauli twirling with dynamical-decoupling masking after compilation. We simulate 11 circuits of 3 to 19 qubits without noise and on two noise models, measure the compiled structure and execution time of 23 circuits of 3 to 127 qubits, and test whether the structural disguise survives a second compilation by the provider. Composite and delayed gates lose most of their structural disguise in compilation and leave the output readable; inverse gates keep a high normalised graph edit distance (normGED) only by making execution 7 to 112 times longer; ECQCO's output could not be decoded with the implementation provided by its authors. Q-MaskCut hides the output (mean total variation distance without the key 0.94), and without noise, with exact fragment probabilities, it decodes exactly. Its compiled normGED of 0.88 comes mostly from the masking, which a second compilation removes, leaving 0.42 from the key, decoys and padlocks. With noise, its fidelity is comparable to the original's; it needs 7 to 91 fragment variants per circuit in the simulations (7 to 61 in a run on ibm_marrakesh) and a classical reconstruction. Protection should therefore be evaluated on the compiled circuit and again after the provider recompiles it.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码在解码可靠性与校验测量所需资源之间存在权衡；仅以码距等几何指标指导设计，未必反映实际解码器下的失效概率，因此需要在固定编码资源下显式刻画"可靠性—校验重量"的权衡并给出可认证的选择依据。

Method: 将固定编码资源下的CSS码设计表述为解码器感知的多目标问题：(1) 目标为扇区式简并最大似然解码下的失败概率，以及校验重量（独立纯型生成元基的最小总重量）；(2) 针对独立比特/相位翻转噪声且伴随式提取完美的情形，推导确定性截断上界，用于在失败率约束下进行选择；(3) 在共享同一退火内核、相同查询预算的配对搜索中，比较解码器感知引导与距离引导、距离—重数引导，并以共同坐标（截断可靠性、归一化校验重量）计算最终超体积，采用Holm校正检验显著性。

Result: 在每扇区错误概率0.01时，界证明一个将2个逻辑量子比特编码进40个物理量子比特的码可在校验重量139下满足0.001的失败率目标，且对应(40,2)主菜单中所有更廉价候选的下界均超出该目标；八组配置中解码器感知引导的平均最终超体积均显著高于距离引导；与距离—重数引导相比五组显著占优、三组不显著（其中一组平均差为负）。

Conclusion: 该框架把可靠性—校验重量权衡与界认证的选择统一起来，适用于所评估CSS码池；结论的适用范围限于给定码池与截断可靠性坐标下的比较，而非对任意码族的普适最优性断言。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes trade decoding reliability against the resources needed to measure their checks. We formulate Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code design at fixed encoding resources as a decoder-aware multiobjective problem. The objectives are failure probability under sector-wise degenerate maximum-likelihood decoding and check weight, the minimum total weight of an independent pure-type generator basis. For independent bit- and phase-flip noise with perfect syndrome extraction, deterministic truncation bounds support selection under a failure-rate constraint. At a per-sector error probability of 0.01, the bounds certify that a code encoding two logical qubits into 40 physical qubits meets a failure-rate target of 0.001 at check weight 139. The lower bound of every cheaper candidate in the corresponding (40,2) main menu exceeds this target. In paired searches sharing one annealing kernel and equal query budgets, decoder-aware guidance gives significantly higher mean final hypervolume than distance guidance in all eight tested configurations under the reported Holm correction. Against distance-multiplicity guidance, five configurations show significant gains and three do not, including one with a negative mean difference. All arms are scored in common truncated-reliability and normalized-check-weight coordinates. The framework connects reliability-check-weight trade-offs to bound-certified selection within evaluated CSS pools.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSS电路（仅由X、Z、M_X、M_Z及CNOT构成）虽受限，却能实现隐形传态、纠错、纠缠纯化等关键协议，因此需要高效模拟手段并厘清其可模拟边界。

Conclusion: CSS电路具有强可模拟性且与局域隐变量模型等价，经典可高效模拟，但边界严格（不含H/CZ/S）；其结构特性使其成为含原子丢失误差的量子纠错基准测试的有力候选。

Abstract: "CSS circuits" are quantum circuits generated by $X$, $Z$, $M_X$, $M_Z$, and CNOT gates. Although CSS circuits are extremely restrictive, they can implement important quantum protocols like teleportation, error correction, and entanglement purification.
  We present a simple CSS circuit simulator that uses $O(1)$ work per gate. The simulator corresponds to a local hidden variable model, so we use Bell inequalities to prove the simulator can't also support Hadamard gates, CZ gates, or S gates. We note that CSS circuits appear ideal for benchmarking quantum error correction in the presence of atom loss errors.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子测量是制备量子纠错码与长程纠缠态的直接途径，但实际实现的有限测量强度会使编码映射偏离投影极限。某些非局域长程序特征虽可存留，却不能保证编码量子信息被保护，因此需要厘清有限测量强度下恢复可行性及其对逻辑操作的影响。

Method: 分析固定测量强度下沿逻辑算符支撑的局部逻辑泄漏累积；推导测量强度与码距的对数关系；对固定toric寄存器在足够弱的局部随机误差下，仅增强选定逻辑支撑附近消失比例的校验并进行理论证明；进而比较严格三维去禁闭相与二维情形下宏观分离逻辑代表的等价性。

Result: 固定强度下局部逻辑泄漏使渐近忠实恢复不可行，且沿逻辑算符支撑的累积迫使所需测量强度随码距对数增长；对固定toric寄存器，仅增强消失比例校验即可恢复可恢复性而体部保持有限测量强度；三维去禁闭相中增强管可恢复宏观分离代表的等价性，二维中即使两种编码均可恢复，代表仍可能不等价。

Abstract: Stabilizer measurements provide a direct route to preparing quantum error-correcting codes and long-range entangled states. In realistic implementations, however, finite measurement strength can coherently deform the encoding map away from the projective limit. Certain nonlocal signatures associated with long-range order can survive such a deformation, but this alone does not guarantee that the resulting state protects encoded quantum information. We show that, at fixed strength, local logical leakage obstructs asymptotically faithful recovery; along logical operators' supports, its accumulation forces the relevant measurement strength to grow logarithmically with code distance. We then prove that this obstruction can be removed locally: for fixed toric registers under sufficiently weak local-stochastic errors, enhancing only a vanishing fraction of checks near selected logical supports restores recovery while the bulk remains at finite measurement strength. With recoverability restored, we prove a further dimensional distinction in logical operations. In the strict three-dimensional deconfined phase, suitable enhanced tubes restore the equivalence of macroscopically separated representatives, so either representative asymptotically implements the same controlled logical action on a fixed encoding. In two dimensions, recoverability alone does not restore this equivalence: the representatives can remain inequivalent even when both associated encodings are recoverable.

</details>


### [77] [From optimization to construction: Expanding quantum measurement designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14986)
*Taotao Zhao,Qianjun Yao,Mengdi Sun,He Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出方差引导的测量分组构造方法（VGMC），通过改变哪些哈密顿量项共享测量次数来进一步降低能量估计量的方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机上估计分子能量需重复测量大量Pauli项，采样是主要资源开销；现有测量设计仅在固定测量基上优化系数与测量次数分配，方差降低受限。

Method: 先纳入每个已有基下所有可测的哈密顿量项，联合优化估计量系数与测量次数分配，以区分测量利用低效与测量组合本身的局限；再用"候选收益与从已有基撤回测量次数成本"构成的评分，贪心构造qubit-wise commuting分组，并进行联合重优化。

Result: 在精确协方差下，VGMC在引入额外测量基的分子基准上优于现有最先进测量设计方法，方差降低18.19%–23.38%；近似协方差下增益仍保持，有限采样模拟显示在化学精度附近所需估计测量次数更少。

Conclusion: 优化测量组合方式也可指导哪些项应一起测量，为在现有及近期设备上保留局域测量的同时降低变分量子模拟的采样开销提供了途径。

Abstract: Estimating molecular energies on quantum computers requires repeated measurements of many Pauli terms, making sampling a major resource cost. Measurement design chooses which compatible terms are measured together and allocates a finite shot budget among bases to minimize the variance of an energy estimator. We introduce variance-guided measurement group construction (VGMC), which reduces variance beyond optimization on existing measurement bases by changing which Hamiltonian terms share measurement shots. After including every Hamiltonian term measurable in each existing basis, we jointly optimize estimator coefficients and shot allocations to separate inefficient use of these measurements from limitations of their measurement combinations. We compare a candidate's benefit with the cost of withdrawing shots from existing bases, and use the resulting scores to guide greedy construction of qubit-wise commuting groups followed by joint re-optimization. With exact covariances, VGMC outperformed the state-of-the-art measurement design methods across the molecular benchmarks where it introduced additional measurement bases, achieving variance reductions of 18.19%-23.38%. The gains persisted with approximate covariances, and simulations with finite shots indicated lower estimated shot requirements near chemical accuracy. These results show that optimizing how measurements are combined can also guide which terms are measured together, offering a route to reducing the sampling overhead of variational quantum simulation while retaining local measurements on current and near-term devices.

</details>


### [78] [QSim-Arena: an evolving developer for quantum simulation advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15000)
*Jue Xu,Yue Wang,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 QSim-Arena——一个由 AI 智能体驱动的研究框架，通过"验证-基准测试闭环 + 开放探索"结合的方式，对量子动力学模拟中的量子优势进行可复现、可迭代的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子动力学模拟中量子优势的判定存在争议：一方面，宣称的优势常被更强的经典方法迅速缩小或推翻；另一方面，量子与经典双方进展速度都可能超过期刊发表周期。因此需要一个动态、可复现、能持续响应算法、误差分析与硬件进展的评估机制。

Method: 框架以哈密顿量、初始态和精度目标（演化态或可观测量期望值）作为显式规格来评价模拟方法。AI 智能体：(1) 将已发表技术转化为可执行组件，复现其结果并在多样哈密顿量族上基准测试；(2) 实现并独立交叉校验竞争性经典方法，基于严格误差界与经验校准给出可信的端到端量子资源估计基线；(3) 在闭环之外，从文献中提取知识，重组经典模拟技术并协同设计容错量子线路。

Result: 初步应用通过整合已有技术改进了基线资源估计；框架可将量子优势评估转化为能响应算法、误差分析与硬件进展的演化式研究过程。

Conclusion: QSim-Arena 将量子优势评估视为一个持续演化、可复现的研究流程，而非一次性结论，从而在快速变动的量子-经典竞争中提供更可靠的比较与资源估计。

Abstract: Where practical advantage emerges in quantum dynamics simulation remains a central debate in quantum computing. Claimed advantages can be rapidly narrowed or overturned by stronger classical methods, while progress on both sides can outpace the journal publication cycle. We present QSim-Arena, a research framework in which AI agents combine a closed loop of verification and benchmarking with open exploration. Hamiltonians, initial states and accuracy targets for evolved states or observable expectation values provide explicit specifications for evaluating simulation methods. Agents translate published techniques into executable components, reproduce reported results and benchmark their performance across diverse Hamiltonian families. Competing classical methods are implemented and independently crosschecked, providing credible baselines for end-to-end quantum resource estimates based on rigorous error bounds and empirical calibration. Alongside this closed loop, agents recombine classical simulation techniques and co-design fault-tolerant quantum circuits using knowledge extracted from the literature. Initial applications improve baseline resource estimates through the integration of established techniques. QSim-Arena thus treats quantum advantage assessment as an evolving, reproducible research process that can respond to advances in algorithms, error analysis and hardware.

</details>


### [79] [Time- and Photon-Optimal Paths for the Preparation and Logical Flip of Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15018)
*Aidan Curtis,Juan S. Salcedo-Gallo,Sergei Leonov,Kevin Limanta,Peter Chin,Salil Bedkihal,Mattias Fitzpatrick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以 brachistochrone（有界速度下最快路径）为工具，构造 GKP 态制备与逻辑门所需的理想非线性哈密顿量，并与光子最优路径对比，提出"切向容量"量化门集对路径的跟踪能力，发现 conditional squeezing 性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP 态高度非高斯，在存在光子损耗时难以控制。需要为态制备与逻辑门寻找在光子损耗约束下最优的控制路径，并评估不同门集实现这些路径的能力。

Method: 1) 采用 brachistochrone（有界速度下最短时间路径）构造态制备与逻辑门的理想非线性哈密顿量；2) 与 photon-optimal path 对比；3) 提出 tangential capacity（切向容量）指标，量化门集对目标路径的跟踪效率；4) 在 conditional squeezing、conditional displacement、SNAP 三类门集上评估容量，比较制备时间、保真度及对路径的最小接近度。

Result: 两条路径在态制备上几乎重合，但在逻辑操作上显著不同：brachistochrone 在 bit flip 过程中保持编码量子比特，而 photon-optimal path 经由真空翻转已知态。容量评估显示 conditional squeezing 最高：制备 GKP 态用时 824 ns，保真度 0.99，路径最小接近度 0.90；conditional displacement 为 0.98 与 0.51，SNAP 门为 0.83 与 0.19。

Conclusion: brachistochrone 可作为 GKP 控制中区分"保编码"与"经真空"两类逻辑路径的判据；切向容量能有效筛选门集，conditional squeezing 在速度、保真度与路径跟踪三方面综合最优，是 GKP 态制备与逻辑门的优选方案。

Abstract: Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) states are highly non-Gaussian, making their control challenging, particularly in the presence of photon loss. Here, we use the brachistochrone, the fastest path at bounded speed, to construct ideal nonlinear Hamiltonians for state preparation and logical gates, and compare it with the photon-optimal path. We find that the two paths nearly coincide for state preparation but differ markedly for logical operations, namely the brachistochrone preserves the encoded qubit during a bit flip, whereas the photon-optimal path flips a known state through the vacuum. We then introduce the tangential capacity to quantify how effectively gate sets track these paths. Using this analysis, we find that conditional squeezing achieves the highest capacity, preparing GKP states in 824 ns with a fidelity of 0.99 and a minimum closeness to the path of 0.90, compared with 0.98 and 0.51 for conditional displacements and 0.83 and 0.19 for SNAP gates.

</details>

TL;DR: 通过沉积并随后去除固体氖薄膜，可显著降低NbTiN共面波导谐振器中的两能级系统损耗，从而提升超导器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两能级系统（TLS）是超导器件的主要损耗来源，限制量子比特、探测器与谐振器性能；其源自表面氧化物、晶体缺陷及难以去除的制备残留，而许多敏感材料无法承受强效清洗工艺。

Method: 采用NbTiN共面波导谐振器，在其表面沉积并随后蒸发去除固体氖薄膜；通过温度与功率依赖测量分析损耗机制，并用原子力显微镜对污染前后的六方氮化硼薄片进行形貌对比。

Conclusion: 固体氖沉积-蒸发是一种简便、非破坏性的TLS损耗抑制方法，适用于无法承受强效清洗工艺的超导器件及其他敏感材料。

Abstract: Two level systems (TLSs) are a major source of loss in superconducting devices, limiting the performance of qubits, detectors, and resonators. These defects arise from a range of sources, including surface oxides, crystal defects, as well as fabrication residues which are often difficult to remove. Using NbTiN coplanar waveguide resonators, we show that depositing and subsequently removing a solid neon film can significantly improve device performance. Analysis of the temperature and power dependence indicates that this enhancement arises from reduced TLS dissipation, which we attribute to the redistribution and removal of surface contaminants during neon deposition and evaporation. Atomic force microscopy images of contaminated hexagonal boron nitride flakes before and after neon exposure show that neon treatment can redistribute and remove surface residues. These results demonstrate a simple, non-destructive approach for mitigating TLS losses in superconducting devices and other sensitive materials that cannot withstand aggressive cleaning processes.

</details>

Result: 在两种模型中，DQN发现的脉冲序列均提升了对应测量模型下的传感精度；相位—失谐联合调制进一步提高性能，使经典费舍尔信息（CFI）接近相应的量子费舍尔信息（QFI）。

Conclusion: 强化学习脉冲优化可在实验可行的控制测量协议下实现高精度、可靠的能级泄漏估计，联合相位—失谐调制可逼近QFI所给定的精度极限。

Abstract: Level leakage constitutes a dominant error source in quantum state control and necessitates accurate characterization for large-scale quantum computing. This work proposes a reinforcement learning-based quantum sensing algorithm for Hermitian three-level and effective dissipative two-level systems. In both models, DQN-discovered pulse sequences enhance the measurement-model-specific sensing precision, while joint phase--detuning modulation further improves the performance and yields a CFI close to the corresponding QFI.
