### [82] [Sensing frequencies at the discrete Grover-Heisenberg limit using a single qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15057)
*Boyang Chen,Andrea Coladangelo,Jerry Li,Haocheng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究用单个量子比特检测未知强度、未知频率的微弱振荡场，证明在强度已知、频率取自有限候选集的离散版本中，单比特即可达到所需感知时间随候选频率数平方根增长的"离散Grover-Heisenberg极限"，并证明该标度在最优最坏情况下不可超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Allen等人此前给出了感知时间的基本下界（"Grover-Heisenberg极限"）并构造了达到该极限的协议，但该协议依赖辅助量子比特运行Grover算法作为子程序，因此单传感比特能否实现相同标度的问题悬而未决。本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 针对问题的离散版本（信号强度已知、频率属于任意但已知的有限候选频率集），设计仅使用单个传感量子比特的感知协议，其感知时间仅随候选频率数的平方根增长；并通过理论分析证明该平方根依赖在最坏情况下是最优的。

Result: 单比特即可完成离散版本任务，感知时间标度为候选频率数N的平方根量级（O(√N)）；并证明了该"离散Grover-Heisenberg极限"在 worst-case 意义下最优，无法进一步改进。

Conclusion: 辅助量子比特并非必需，单个传感比特足以在离散设定下达到Grover-Heisenberg型标度，且该√N标度为最优下界，深化了对量子传感中资源（辅助比特）必要性的理解。

Abstract: We consider a fundamental quantum sensing task: detecting weak oscillating fields with unknown strength and frequency. Recent work by Allen et al. established a fundamental lower bound on the required sensing time, which they named the "Grover-Heisenberg limit", and gave a protocol attaining this limit. The protocol relies on auxiliary qubits, which are used to run Grover's algorithm as a subroutine, leaving open the question of whether a single sensing qubit can achieve the same scaling.
  We show that a single qubit suffices for a discrete version of this problem, in which the signal strength is known and the frequency belongs to an arbitrary, but known, finite set of candidate frequencies. Our protocol's sensing time grows only as the square root of the number of candidate frequencies. We prove that this square-root dependence, which we refer to as the "discrete Grover-Heisenberg limit", is optimal in the worst case.

</details>


### [83] [Scalable Compilation of Resource-Efficient Tapers for High-Confidence Quantum Phase Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15076)
*David Raveh,Andrew Sornborger,Yiğit Subaşı,Lukasz Cincio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出可扩展的张量网络方法，为最优集中的DPSS(Slepian)锥形态构造高效态制备电路，将tQPE的可构造规模从 l+m=24 推广到 l=1,...,50、m=1,...,4，并在Quantinuum H2-2上实现失败概率3×至18×的降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相位估计是众多量子算法的核心组件。高置信度的锥形QPE(tQPE)能以双指数衰减的失败概率显著减少所需辅助量子比特数，但其关键瓶颈在于辅助寄存器上锥形态制备电路的构造，此前方法仅局限于 l+m=24 的小规模。

Method: 采用可扩展的张量网络方法，针对最优集中的DPSS(Slepian)锥形进行态制备电路的优化综合，生成浅层、资源高效的电路，并建立 l=1,...,50、m=1,...,4 的电路数据库；随后在Quantinuum H2-2离子阱量子计算机上对初态为基态的1量子比特系统运行tQPE电路进行验证。

Result: 成功构造出大规模优化的态制备电路，浅层电路在小 l 值时可用于近期设备演示；在含噪硬件上，对 l=1,...,10 实现了失败概率相对基准的3倍至18倍降低。

Abstract: Quantum phase estimation is a key component of many quantum algorithms. Recently, high-confidence, tapered QPE (tQPE) has been introduced, significantly reducing the number of ancilla qubits necessary for a given failure probability, $ε$. We consider tQPE with $p = l+m$ ancillas, where $l$ represents the number of ancillas necessary to estimate a phase to a given resolution and $m$ represents the number of ''confidence'' qubits added to ensure a desired success probability. With tQPE the failure probability decays double exponentially in $m$, implying that only a few confidence qubits suffice for virtually any problem of interest. A key challenge for tQPE is the construction of the quantum circuit for preparing the taper state on the ancilla register. Here, we develop scalable tensor network methods (previous work was limited to ($l + m = 24$)) giving highly optimized state preparation circuits for optimally concentrated DPSS (Slepian) tapers. We provide an accompanying database of resource-efficient state preparation circuits for $l = 1, \dots, 50$ and $m = 1, \dots, 4$. Our circuits are shallow enough for a demonstration on near-term devices for small values of $l$. We run tQPE circuits for a 1-qubit system initialized in the ground state on Quantinuum's H2-2 quantum computer, demonstrating a $3\times$ to $18\times$ reduction in failure probability for $l=1,\ldots,10$ on a noisy device.

</details>


### [84] [One molecule, many outputs; many molecules, one output: exact diagnostics for atom-level compositional quantum encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15092)
*Gautami Sanjay Naik,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将QNLP的lambeq框架移植到分子图：重原子为词、化学键为cup，电路布线即分子图，并精确刻画这类电路保留哪些信息、丢失哪些信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将句子语法→张量网络→带可训练角度的量子电路的QNLP流程用于分子表示，系统厘清此类"分子电路"的表达能力边界，而非追求量子优势。

Result: typed共享可区分所测全部异构体对；简化共享下不可分对子的输出态几乎相同（训练前后均如此），差异主要藏在测量成功概率中而被标准读出忽略；简化共享输出仅依赖以键为顶点的图，可解释除一例外的全部同输出对，包括232,191个MOSES分子中的1.19%；烷基侧链退化为树形，可区分至15个原子、16个起不可分；小规模上与1-WL互为补充；SMILES书写顺序会改变输出；n个键时运行成功概率至多2^{-n}；无cup设计可规避该代价。

Conclusion: 该分子QNLP电路无量子优势，Tox21上无电路胜过简单原子计数模型；其价值在于给出了可证的同输出对刻画与表达能力分析。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) turns the grammar of a sentence into a tensor network and then into a quantum circuit with trainable angles. We build such a model for molecules: each heavy atom is a word with one wire per bond, and each bond is a ``cup'' joining two wires, so the circuit's wiring is the molecular graph. We work out exactly what these circuits keep. With the typed angle sharing of lambeq, the circuits separate every isomer pair we tested. Yet for isomers that simpler sharing confuses, the output states stay almost the same, before and after training. On 7 Tox21 pairs and 1,988 MOSES pairs, nearly all the difference lies in how often the measurements succeed, which a standard readout ignores. Simpler sharing, by element and bond type, shows why: the output then depends only on a graph whose vertices are the bonds. This explains all but one of the identical-output pairs we certify, including 1.19\% of 232{,}191 MOSES molecules, and for alkyl side chains it reduces to a tree shape that tells chains apart up to 15 atoms but not from 16. On small pairs, this circuit and the 1-WL graph test each separate pairs the other misses. We also show that SMILES writing order can change the output, that a run succeeds with probability at most $2^{-n}$ for $n$ bonds, and that a cup-free design avoids this cost. No circuit beats a simple atom-count model on Tox21, and we claim no quantum advantage.

</details>


### [85] [An Architecture for Real-Time Quantum Error Correction with Neutral Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15104)
*X. Wang,Y. Jing,Z. Yu,S. Wang,D. Niu,Y. Liu,T. Xiao,A. Yang,J. Han,Y. Wu,X. Lv*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并实现了一种异构级联读出与解码架构：FPGA逐行处理相机数据，GPU在下一逻辑块采集期间解码已采集块，将后曝光处理延迟降至亚毫秒级（d=3,5,7小码负载）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量与经典解码的延迟制约自适应容错电路的执行；在冷原子处理器中，该需求使空间分辨荧光探测与经典推断强耦合，需降低从曝光到解码的端到端延迟以实现实时纠错。

Conclusion: 通过采集与处理在空间两尺度上的重叠，该架构实现了低延迟综合征解码，为原子阵列中的实时容错量子计算提供了可行路径，并保持编码方案与解码算法的灵活性。

Abstract: The latency of quantum measurement and classical decoding constrains the execution of adaptive fault-tolerant circuits. Here we implement cascaded readout and decoding in a heterogeneous architecture that overlaps acquisition and processing at two spatial scales. Field-programmable gate arrays process camera rows as they arrive, while graphics processors decode an acquired logical block during acquisition of the next. This heterogeneous architecture offers flexibility in the choice of error-correcting codes and decoding algorithms. Direct communication preserves microsecond-scale transfer times for kilobyte payloads and supports interchangeable decoding algorithms. Hardware characterization and decoding benchmarks using circuit-level simulated syndromes yield a submillisecond post-exposure processing latency for selected small-code workloads ($d = 3, 5, 7$). By enabling logical blocks to be decoded before full-frame readout is complete, the architecture implements low-latency syndrome decoding, realizing real-time quantum information processing as required for fault-tolerant computation in atom arrays.

</details>

Abstract: The dissipaton theory was proposed more than a decade ago as a statistical quasiparticle theory for open quantum systems and was soon extended to nonlinear system-bath coupling. Although numerical results provided strong evidence for its validity, a rigorous mathematical foundation, particularly for nonlinear system-bath coupling, was established only recently. On this basis, we formulate the dissipaton-embedded quantum master equation for nonlinear system-bath coupling. This in-principle exact master equation describes the joint dynamics of the reduced system and all dissipaton degrees of freedom.

*Sebastian Issel*

TL;DR: 提出将LCU块编码的选择深度从固定总量转化为可调控的并行装箱问题，给出$g$-分支实现的紧界$Θ(C+\log(g\max\{n,m\}))$与工作量下界$\sum_j d_j/g$，并分析两种选择器实现的空间-深度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LCU块编码$A=\sum_j α_j U_j$在单寄存器上串行执行$U_j$，其选择深度恒等于总工作量$\sum_j d_j$，成为块编码约束中唯一缺乏可调旋钮的指标。作者希望引入并行多寄存器分组（分支）机制，为深度约束提供可调自由度。

Method: 将$T$项分为$g$个并行寄存器，装箱方案（binning）作为调节旋钮；在$k$-局部原子预言机模型下分析$g$-分支实现的选择深度。通过双向归约联系经典多处理器调度：任一实现诱导一个$g$机调度，构造可实现任意装箱，故最优装箱达到下界。并考察McNaughton规则允许预言机拆分时的工作量下界，以及稠密（Möttönen）与one-hot两种选择器实现的空间-深度权衡。

Result: 对$g\ge2$，选择深度紧界为$Θ(C+\log(g\max\{n,m\}))$，其中$C$为装箱完工时间，$m$为最大箱规模；附加对数路由项为光锥代价，与选择器编码无关，且对寄存器复用、测量、复位和隐形传态稳健；宽度至少$gn$。允许拆分预言机时可达工作量下界$\sum_j d_j/g$。$1/α$次归一化与成功概率不变；端点为顺序LCU（$g{=}1$）与全并行实现（$g{=}T$）。稠密选择器在标准足迹下内存紧但有$\log\log(m+1)$深度惩罚；one-hot选择器深度紧但需$T$个选择量子比特，二者无法同时最优。

Conclusion: 并行分支将LCU块编码的深度从固定量变为可优化量，深度由装箱完工时间加对数路由项决定，工作量下界由经典调度理论刻画。空间与深度之间存在本质权衡，不存在同时达到内存与深度最优的选择器实现。

Abstract: An LCU block-encoding of $A=\sum_jα_j U_j$ runs the $U_j$ in series on one register, so its select depth is the total work $\sum_j d_j$. Depth was the one block-encoding constraint with no knob: grouping the $T$ terms into $g$ registers that run in parallel gives it one, and the binning is the knob. For a $g$-branched realisation (up to $g$ oracles in parallel) the select depth is tight at $Θ(C+\log(g\,\max\{n,m\}))$ for $g\ge2$, with $C$ the binning makespan and $m$ the largest bin, in the $k$-local atomic-oracle model. The work floor is a two-way reduction to classical multiprocessor scheduling: every realisation \emph{induces} a $g$-machine schedule, and the construction realises any binning, so an optimal binning attains it. The additive logarithmic routing term is a light-cone cost, independent of the selector encoding and robust to register reuse, measurement, reset, and teleportation; width is at least $gn$. The standard $1/α$ subnormalisation and success probability are unchanged; the endpoints are sequential LCU ($g{=}1$) and the fully parallel realisation ($g{=}T$). Allowing the oracles to be split (McNaughton's rule) reaches the work floor $\sum_j d_j/g$. Two selector realisations trade space for depth: a dense (Möttönen) one is memory-tight at the standard LCU footprint but its multiplexed controls cost a $\log\log(m{+}1)$ factor on the makespan; a one-hot one is depth-tight at $T$ selector qubits. Neither reaches both at once.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子模块植入生产级大模型时，把全部隐藏神经元编码到量子寄存器的方案不可行：振幅编码电路深度过大，角度编码所需量子比特数过多。因此需要一种在NISQ约束下可实用的窄宽编码+并行处理方案。

Method: 将隐藏空间划分为k个互斥头，每头用角度编码进n_q比特寄存器，多头并行处理，再经受限经典线性层（mixer）耦合。在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上做"手术"：D=2048、k=32、n_q=8、L=4，共145,281可训练参数替换连续8个Transformer块。经典端用精确态矢模拟训练，并将融合检查点的量子头部署到156比特IBM Heron处理器执行。设置对照：关闭纠缠器、冻结电路于初始角/随机角、参数匹配的经典MLP。

Result: 替换16.33%参数后精度损失很小。单块替换时功能由量子头（电路+学习读出）而非mixer恢复；整段融合时仅mixer即可达到完整模块困惑度1–3%以内。然而对照实验表明关闭纠缠（2个种子）或冻结电路角度（1个种子）在留出困惑度上优于完整模块，参数匹配的经典MLP也领先几个百分点。

Conclusion: NISQ规模量子模块可安装进生产级LLM并在真实硬件上运行，工程可行性得到验证；但量子纠缠与训练电路带来的增益未被确立，量子优势主张仍缺乏证据。

Abstract: We introduce the multi-head quantum neural network (QNN), a quantum module compatible with noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices that can replace transformer blocks inside pre-trained large language models (LLMs) with little loss in accuracy and with far fewer parameters than the blocks it replaces. Encoding a model's $D$ hidden neurons into a qubit register is the obstacle: amplitude encoding scales poorly in circuit depth and angle encoding in qubit count, so neither is practical at production-scale width. We instead partition the hidden space into $k$ disjoint heads, angle-encode each into an $n_q$-qubit register, process the heads in parallel, and couple them through constrained classical linear layers, the mixers. Performing surgery on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, we show that a single multi-head QNN with $145\,281$ trainable parameters ($D=2048$, $k=32$, $n_q=8$, $L=4$) can replace a contiguous span of eight transformer blocks, displacing $16.33\%$ of the model's parameters. Ablations show that, for a single replaced block, the quantum heads (circuits followed by a learned readout), and not the classical mixers alone, recover the displaced function, whereas for fused spans the mixers alone come within $1$-$3\%$ of the full module in perplexity. Whether this benefit is specific to trained, entangling quantum circuits is not established: controls that switch the entangler off (two seeds), or freeze the circuit at its initial or at uniformly random angles (one seed), beat the full module on held-out perplexity, and a parameter-matched classical multilayer perceptron remains a few percent ahead of the full module. We train classically with exact statevector simulation, then execute the quantum heads of our fusion checkpoints on a $156$-qubit IBM Heron processor, showing that NISQ-scale quantum modules can be installed in a production-scale LLM and run on hardware.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GME/GMNL见证多依赖具体态与粒子数，且GME界常与允许某一划分侧内任意通信的Svetlichny-hybrid界混淆；需要区分二者，并给出适用于GHZ态与Bell对树的、设备无关且可实验检验的见证与网络界。

Method: 构造二元结果的四全局测量上下文不等式族，证明其紧致双可分界与N无关；与Svetlichny-hybrid界对比；对GHZ给出2N上下文GMNL见证并与MABK、Svetlichny检验比较；对Bell对树用CHSH与链式不等式求和，明确输入协议与多比特局域输出；证明四上下文网络界I_λ≤2（N≥3, λ≥2）并约束二分源量子网络的MABK得分；在IBM Quantum上对最多10比特GHZ态在局域测量与采样假设下基准测试三类见证。

Result: 四上下文二元结果族的双可分界紧致且不随粒子数N增长；成功将该GME界与Svetlichny-hybrid界区分；GHZ的2N上下文GMNL见证与MABK、Svetlichny检验完成对比；Bell对树的CHSH/链式求和分别认证GME与GMNL；证明网络界I_λ≤2（N≥3, λ≥2）并给出二分源网络的MABK得分约束；在IBM Quantum上对最多10比特GHZ实现MABK、四上下文、Svetlichny见证的实验基准。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套设备无关、随粒子数可扩展的GME/GMNL见证框架，清晰刻画了GME与GMNL、Svetlichny-hybrid及网络非局域界之间的关系，并在实际量子硬件上验证了其可行性与比较性能。

Abstract: We derive and compare device-independent witnesses of genuine multipartite entanglement (GME) and genuine multipartite nonlocality (GMNL) for GHZ states and trees of Bell pairs. A binary-outcome family using four global measurement contexts has the tight biseparable bound independent of the number of parties. We distinguish these GME bounds from Svetlichny-hybrid bounds, which permit arbitrary communication within either side of a bipartition. For GHZ states, we also give a $2N$-context GMNL witness and compare it with MABK and Svetlichny tests. For Bell-pair trees, sums of CHSH and chained inequalities certify GME and GMNL, respectively, with explicitly specified input protocols and multi-bit local outputs. Separately, we prove the sharp four-context network bound $I_λ\leq2$ for every $N\geq3$ and $λ\geq2$, and bound the MABK scores of quantum networks assembled from bipartite sources. We successfully benchmark MABK, four-context, and Svetlichny witnesses on IBM Quantum GHZ preparations up to $10$ parties, under the local-measurement and sampling assumptions.

</details>


### [90] [Quantum Recurrent Architectures for Financial Time Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15298)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Gerhard Hellstern,Martin Zaefferer,Martin Braun,Tanja Döhler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在月度营收序列上对比QLSTM与QRC等量子-经典混合循环模型，未发现量子优势：QLSTM与同参数预算的经典对照无显著差异，量子储备池不如等维经典储备池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估量子-经典混合循环架构（QLSTM、QRC）在金融时间序列预测中是否具有量子准确性或参数效率优势，并通过对齐参数预算的经典对照来剥离"量子性"本身的贡献。

Method: 采用幅度编码将经典滞后向量映射为量子态，QLSTM门与QRC储备池由参数化量子电路实现，经典优化器训练接口层与读出层。数据为经高斯过程-隐马尔可夫生成器扩展至1056个月的月度营收序列，固定时间顺序70/15/15划分、一步预测、10个随机种子；对照包括全尺寸LSTM、参数预算与接口结构匹配的经典控制、同预算小型LSTM、经典储备池，以及persistence、seasonal-naive、SARIMA基准；另做24个月滚动起点回测。

Result: 2,105参数的QLSTM与大约126倍大的LSTM统计上不可区分，但与匹配经典控制和同预算普通LSTM同样不可区分，故参数效率不能归因于量子电路；量子储备池在全部10个种子下均劣于等特征维经典储备池且劣于SARIMA。所有循环模型与两种经典储备池较SARIMA的RMSE约改善2%；滚动回测中经典LSTM退化慢于QLSTM。

Conclusion: 在这一低维设定下，没有证据支持量子模型在精度或参数效率上的优势；观察到的参数效率来自架构/接口设计而非量子电路，且量子储备池表现更差。

Abstract: This study evaluates quantum classical hybrid recurrent architectures for financial time-series forecasting: a Quantum Long Short-Term Memory (QLSTM) network and Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) with lag-based inputs. Classical lag vectors are encoded into quantum states via amplitude encoding, which compactly represents normalized observations but requires nontrivial state preparation; the QLSTM gates and the QRC reservoir are implemented with parameterized quantum circuits, while classical optimizers train the interface and readout layers. On a monthly revenue series extended to 1056 months by a Gaussian-process--hidden-Markov generator, with a fixed chronological 70/15/15 split and one-step-ahead forecasts, we compare both models against a full-size LSTM, a classical control matched in parameter budget and interface architecture, a small plain LSTM, and classical reservoir computing, over ten seeds and against persistence, seasonal-naive and SARIMA benchmark forecasts. The QLSTM, with 2,105 parameters, is statistically indistinguishable from an LSTM 126 times larger, but equally indistinguishable from the matched classical control and from a plain LSTM of the same budget, so its parameter efficiency cannot be attributed to the quantum circuits. The quantum reservoir is worse than a classical reservoir of equal feature dimension in all ten seeds and worse than SARIMA, whereas all recurrent models and both classical reservoirs improve on SARIMA by about 2\% in RMSE. In a 24-month rolling-origin backtest the classical LSTMs degrade more slowly than the QLSTM. We find no evidence of a quantum accuracy or parameter-efficiency advantage in this low-dimensional setting.

</details>


### [91] [On the domain of the effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian in quantum markovian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15322)
*Franco Fagnola,Zheng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子马尔可夫半群无界生成元的GKLS表示中，算子G的定义域是否等于G₀与H定义域之交，在Fock空间二次多项式情形下给出肯定条件，并给出反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKLS表示中的无界算子G形式上是负自伴算子G₀与-iH（H对称）之和，但一般情形下三者的定义域关系不明；判断Dom(G)=Dom(G₀)∩Dom(H)是否成立对表示的有效性至关重要。

Method: 在有限模玻色Fock空间中，假设G₀与H为湮灭、产生算子的自伴二次多项式，且G₀≤0，或假设G₀与H的对易子满足某半界条件；另构造反例说明一般情形下结论不成立。

Result: 在所述二次多项式与半界条件下证明了Dom(G)=Dom(G₀)∩Dom(H)；反例表明该等式在一般情形下不成立。

Conclusion: 定义域等式在Fock空间二次多项式模型中成立，但非普遍规律，取决于G₀的符号或对易子的半界性质。

Abstract: The GKLS representation of the unbounded generator of a quantum Markov semigroup crucially depends on an unbounded operator $G$, generating a strongly continuous contraction semigroup on the underlying Hilbert space, which is formally the sum of a negative self-adjoint operator $G_0$ and $-\mathrm{i}H$ with $H$ symmetric.
  In many situations it is useful to know the relationships between the domains of the three operators, in particular whether $\Dom(G)=\Dom(G_0)\cap\Dom(H)$ when both $G_0$ and $H$ are unbounded. In this note we show that this happens when $G_0$ and $H$ are self-adjoint quadratic polynomials in annihilation and creation operators in a finite-mode bosonic Fock space and $G_0\leq0$ or assuming a certain half bound on the commutator of $G_0$ and $H$.
  A counterexample shows that the conclusion does not hold in general situations.

</details>


### [92] [Hybrid Classical-Quantum Optimization for CVRPTW: A Systems Framework with Provable Feasibility and Empirical Hardware Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15343)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出MILP+车辆级QUBO的混合经典-量子架构求解CVRPTW，在伦敦数据基准上SQBM+实现可验证的2.80%/0.22%车队距离缩减，并给出量子硬件可靠约束满足的规模边界（16量子比特）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模CVRPTW因严格运营约束与规模限制，精确方法与启发式方法均受限；单体式CVRPTW QUBO存在变量膨胀与约束难以强制的问题，故需可分解的混合架构。

Method: MILP与车辆级QUBO相结合的分解式混合经典-量子架构，QUBO用Toshiba SQBM+及Rigetti Ankaa-3、IQM Emerald上的QAOA求解；在源自伦敦交通与位置数据的5个基准（10–70客户）上评估，并采用强制可行性与对经典基线单调改进的验证流水线。

Result: SQBM+在25客户实例上实现经验证的车队距离缩减2.80%（7,927 m），55客户实例上0.22%（1,186 m）；QAOA在最多4客户（16量子比特）子问题上确立了当前硬件可靠约束满足的边界；诊断表明任务分配层面的时间窗紧致度、而非局部路径优化，是主要可扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 混合经典-量子优化在近期具有实际作用，但当前量子硬件的可靠求解规模仍有限，瓶颈在于分配层的时间窗紧致度。

Abstract: Solving large-scale Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problems with Time Windows (CVRPTW) remains challenging because strict operational constraints and scale limit both exact and heuristic methods. We present a hybrid classical-quantum architecture combining Mixed Integer Linear Programming (MILP) with vehicle-level Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO), solved using Toshiba SQBM+ and QAOA on Rigetti Ankaa-3 and IQM Emerald quantum processors. The decomposition avoids the variable growth and constraint-enforcement difficulties of monolithic CVRPTW QUBO formulations. We evaluate the framework on five realistic benchmarks derived from London traffic and location data, spanning 10--70 customers, using a validation pipeline enforcing feasibility and monotone improvement over classical baselines. SQBM+ achieves verified fleet-distance reductions of 2.80% (7,927 m) for the 25-customer instance and 0.22% (1,186 m) for the 55-customer instance. QAOA experiments establish a current hardware boundary for reliable constraint satisfaction at subproblems of up to 4 customers (16 qubits). Diagnostic results identify assignment-level time-window tightness, rather than local route optimization, as the dominant scalability bottleneck. The results demonstrate a practical near-term role for hybrid classical-quantum optimization while establishing empirical limits for current quantum hardware.

</details>


### [93] [Quantization Breaks Classical Mpemba Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15349)
*Yue Liu,Tan Van Vu,Frédéric van Wijland,Hisao Hayakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于Caldeira-Leggett模型建立量子-经典热弛豫对应：经典能量扩散的局域性在单势阱与对称双势阱中禁止Mpemba效应，而量子跃迁可跨越非相邻能级，从而在这些体系中实现Mpemba效应；ℏ→0时非局域跃迁被抑制，恢复经典约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应已在经典与量子体系中被广泛研究，但要辨识"量子化"本身的独特作用，必须与对应的经典动力学做一致比较，此前缺乏可靠且对应的量子-经典对照框架。

Method: 以Caldeira-Leggett模型为出发点，构建热弛豫的量子-经典对应关系；在此框架下分别分析经典能量扩散的局域性约束与量子非局域能级跃迁的影响，并考察ℏ→0的半经典极限。

Result: 经典情形下，能量扩散的局域性使得单势阱与对称双势阱中不可能出现Mpemba效应；量子情形下，非相邻能级间的直接跃迁突破该局部性限制，使上述势场中出现Mpemba效应；随ℏ→0，非局域跃迁逐渐被抑制，经典约束得以恢复。

Conclusion: 量子化的根本作用是"解锁"违反经典局域性的反常弛豫路径，而非仅仅改变弛豫速率；这为区分量子与经典Mpemba效应的来源提供了明确判据。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect, in which an initially hotter system relaxes faster than a colder one, has been widely studied in both classical and quantum systems, yet identifying the specific effects of quantization requires a consistent comparison with the corresponding classical dynamics. Starting from the Caldeira-Leggett model, we establish a quantum-classical correspondence for thermal relaxation. We find that the locality of classical energy diffusion forbids the Mpemba effect in single-well and symmetric double-well potentials. Conversely, quantum dynamics allows for direct transitions between nonadjacent energy levels, overcoming the constraints of classical local energy diffusion and enabling the Mpemba effect in these potentials. In the semiclassical limit $\hbar\to0$, the nonlocal transitions become increasingly suppressed, restoring the classical constraints. Our findings reveal that quantization fundamentally unlocks anomalous relaxation pathways, rather than merely modifying relaxation rates.

</details>


### [94] [When Do Quantum Correlations Matter for Quantum Annealing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15351)
*Rongfeng Xie,Evgenii Ievlev,Alex Kamenev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过比较量子退火(QA)与保留哈密顿量结构与调度但不含非局域量子关联的量子启发式退火(QIA),发现二者的标度差异强烈依赖模型:QA在干净低维系统中优势明显,但随维度升高而衰减,三维及以上可忽略;无序与阻挫进一步缩小差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联能否为非绝热退火带来标度优势仍是开放问题,需要一个能剥离非局域量子关联的经典基线来定量评估其作用。

Method: 构建量子启发式退火(QIA)作为经典对照协议,保留与QA相同的哈密顿量结构和退火调度,仅排除非局域量子关联;在维度、无序与阻挫各异的模型族上比较QA与QIA的标度行为。

Result: QA与QIA的标度差异强烈依赖模型:在干净低维系统中QA具明确优势;随维度增加优势减弱,三维及以上可忽略;无序与阻挫进一步压缩差异。

Conclusion: 研究界定了量子关联对退火标度产生定性影响的模型区域,并为评估其作用提供了经典基线。

Abstract: Whether quantum correlations give non-adiabatic annealing a scaling advantage remains an open question. We address it by comparing quantum annealing (QA) with quantum-inspired annealing (QIA), a classical protocol that retains the Hamiltonian structure and annealing schedule of QA while excluding nonlocal quantum correlations. Across models varying in dimensionality, disorder, and frustration, we find that the difference in scaling between the two protocols is strongly model dependent. QA shows a clear advantage in clean, low-dimensional systems, but the advantage diminishes with increasing dimensionality and becomes negligible in three or more dimensions. Disorder and frustration further narrow the difference. These results identify where quantum correlations have a qualitative effect on annealing scaling and provide a classical baseline for assessing their role.

</details>


### [95] [A Unified Benchmarking Study of Variational Quantum Eigensolver Ansatz for Electronic Structure Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15365)
*Julie Maria Raju,Abhoy Kole,Julen Larrucea,Kamalika Datta,Lennart Weingarten,Rolf Drechsler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统基准比较了UCCSD、fermionic ADAPT-VQE、BasicPCA HEA与Excitation-Preserving HEA四类ansatz在H₂、LiH、N₂上的电路深度、参数量、基态能量误差与NISQ可行性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子相关增强使经典电子结构计算代价高昂，VQE被视为NISQ上的替代方案，但ansatz选择至关重要且缺乏统一比较，各方法在不同分子规模下的可扩展性与权衡不清晰。

Method: 在H₂、LiH、N₂等不同规模分子上，对UCCSD、fermionic ADAPT-VQE、BasicPCA HEA和Excitation-Preserving HEA进行统一基准测试，评估电路深度、参数数量、基态能量误差与硬件可行性之间的权衡。

Result: ADAPT-VQE以更低资源开销达到与UCCSD相当的精度，但在约10电子规模因电路复杂度上升而变得不实用；HEA降低硬件成本但参数更多、精度下降且强烈依赖分子规模；Excitation-Preserving ansatz对H₂和LiH未达化学精度，对N₂失效。

Conclusion: VQE ansatz在精度与资源效率间存在关键权衡；未来需提升ADAPT-VQE的可扩展性与HEA的精度，并解决近期硬件上的优化难题。

Abstract: Electronic structure calculations become computationally expensive on classical computers as electron correlation increases. Quantum computing offers a promising alternative through hybrid quantum-classical Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) on Noisy-Intermediate Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices. The choice of ansatz plays a pivotal role in the performance and scalability of the VQE, yet selecting an optimal ansatz remains challenging. Existing approaches including chemically inspired ansatz, adaptive schemes and Hardware Efficient Ansätze (HEA) lack a unified comparison. This gap makes the scalability and trade-offs of each method unclear for varying molecular sizes.
  This work presents a systematic benchmarking study comparing Unitary Couples Cluster Singles and Doubles Ansatz (UCCSD), fermionic ADAPT-VQE, BasicPCA HEA, and Excitation Preserving HEA across molecules of varying size like $H_2$, $LiH$, $N_2$. We evaluate trade-offs among circuit depth, parameter count, ground-state energy error, and NISQ feasibility. ADAPT-VQE achieves accuracy comparable to UCCSD with lower resource overhead, but becomes impractical for modest system sizes ($\sim$10 electrons) due to increasing circuit complexity. In contrast, HEA reduces hardware costs but requires more parameters and sacrifices accuracy, with performance strongly dependent on molecular size. The Excitation Preserving ansatz remains below chemical accuracy for $H_2$ and $LiH$, and fails for $N_2$, underscoring the need to balance expressibility and trainability. Overall, these results highlight key trade-offs between accuracy and resource efficiency across VQE ansätze, emphasizing the need to improve ADAPT-VQE scalability and HEA accuracy while addressing optimization challenges for near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [96] [Full Mesh Dynamic Four-User Entanglement-based Switched QKD Network Integrated with SD-KMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15374)
*Nikolas Makris,Persefoni Konteli,Zafeirios Georgiadis,Alkinoos Papageorgopoulos,Grigorios Anastasiou,Panagiotis Papanastasiou,Konstantinos Tsimvrakidis,George T. Kanellos*

Method: 采用偏振纠缠光源构建四用户全mesh拓扑的QKD网络；引入软件定义密钥管理（SD-KMS）系统，对光路进行动态切换编排（optical switching），并向应用侧提供实时密钥下发。

Result: 实现了四用户全mesh互连的可重构QKD网络演示，光交换可动态配置，密钥由管理系统实时编排并供给量子安全应用。

Conclusion: 将偏振纠缠QKD网络与软件定义密钥管理相结合，可支撑多用户、可重构的量子安全通信架构，为网络化QKD的实用化部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate a reconfigurable, four-user, full-mesh quantum key distribution network using polarization entanglement, integrated with a software-defined key management system to orchestrate dynamic optical switching and real-time key provisioning for quantum-safe applications.

</details>

*Alessandro Ciattoni*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏观量子电动力学(MQED)处理真空中有限物体时，需借助渐近极限程序才能纳入自由空间辐射模，缺乏对散射模的显式描述，难以刻画非平衡下散射场与热涨落的相对独立贡献。

Method: 采用MLNF，将渐近散射模与耗散材料产生的电磁场独立量子化；利用精确二阶Magnus展开推导横向动量分辨能量交换概率的闭合主公式；在无照明与有照明两种情形下分析散射场与热涨落的统计关系，并推广至含量子光学态的情形。

Result: 无照明情形下，有限温度时真空散射与热材料涨落在数学上不再重组为并矢格林函数的虚部总；该结果不违反涨落-耗散定理，因为零温散射场是独立动力学系统，使体系偏离全局热平衡。由此揭示本征的Stokes–Anti-Stokes不对称性，导致能量损失与热能量获得具有不同的横向衍射图样，并给出广义细致平衡关系，用于描述纳米尺度电子测温中表观温度的辐射降低；有照明情形下，框架严格将量子光学态纳入宏观耗散电动力学。

Conclusion: MLNF+二阶Magnus展开提供了统一描述快电子与纳米结构间量子电动力学相互作用的非微扰框架，清晰分离散射场与热涨落，揭示非平衡Stokes–Anti-Stokes不对称性、广义细致平衡与电子测温中的表观温度修正，并可自然扩展至量子光照明情形。

Abstract: Theoretical descriptions of the quantum electrodynamical interaction between fast electrons and nanostructures conventionally rely on standard macroscopic quantum electrodynamics (MQED), which, when applied to bounded objects in vacuum, formally requires an asymptotic limiting procedure to account for free-space radiation modes. To explicitly incorporate these modes from the outset, we employ the modified Langevin noise formalism (MLNF), which independently quantizes both the asymptotic scattering modes and the electromagnetic field generated by dissipative material sources. Utilizing an exact second-order Magnus expansion, we derive a closed-form, nonperturbative master formula for the transverse-momentum-resolved energy exchange probability. In the unilluminated regime, we demonstrate that at finite nanostructure temperatures, the vacuum scattering and thermal material fluctuations do not mathematically recombine into the total imaginary part of the dyadic Green's function. This breakdown does not violate the Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem, because the explicit zero-temperature scattering field acts as an independent dynamical system that maintains the configuration out of global thermodynamic equilibrium. This fundamental separation reveals an intrinsic Stokes--Anti-Stokes asymmetry, yielding distinct transverse diffraction patterns for energy loss and thermal energy gain, and establishing a generalized detailed-balance relation that governs the radiative reduction of the apparent temperature in nanoscale electron thermometry. In the illuminated regime, our framework rigorously incorporates quantum-optical states into macroscopic dissipative electrodynamics, demonstrating that...

</details>


### [98] [The Quantum Euclid Algorithm and its Application to Factoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15387)
*Asma Al-Qasimi,Daniel F. V. James*

Main category: quant-ph

Conclusion: 该工作属于概念性/原理验证性质：展示了量子 GCD 与因式分解之间的直接联系，但受限于可扩展性，尚不构成实用的大数分解方案。

Abstract: We present a quantum implementation of the Euclid algorithm for calculating the Greatest Common Divisor when one of the integers involved is a compound of two odd-valued primes. We discuss a possible application to the quantum factoring problem, for which modular exponentiation is no longer required, and the factors are revealed directly by measurements, although the method does not scale for large numbers in a favourable manner.

</details>

Result: 金属顶栅诱导的剪切应变可能足以激活k1谷耦合机制，从而减弱对大谷劈裂所通常要求的"超陡峭界面"的依赖；所提出的窄量子阱方案可在不要求超陡峭界面的条件下，配合剪切应变实现大的确定性谷劈裂，并带来强自旋轨道耦合与显著的g因子修正。

Conclusion: k1谷耦合可由金属顶栅的剪切应变激活，窄量子阱方案无需超陡峭界面即可获得大谷劈裂、强自旋轨道耦合与g因子修正，具有重要的实验实现价值。

Abstract: Several heterostructure profiles have been proposed for Si/SiGe quantum wells, to improve the operation of quantum-dot spin qubits. These structures share a common goal of generating either of the two wave vectors that govern the conduction-band valley coupling, dubbed $k_0$ and $k_1$. Here, we focus on the long-wavelength $k_1$ term that couples valleys when paired with shear strain, and which governs the Dresselhaus spin-orbit coupling. We show that shear strain induced by metal top gates may be enough to activate the $k_1$ valley-coupling mechanism, mitigating the need for "ultrasharp" interfaces, as commonly assumed for large valley energy splittings. We propose a scheme featuring narrow quantum wells that does not require ultrasharp interfaces, but should still provide large deterministic valley splittings (when paired with shear strain), and large spin-orbit couplings and $g$-factor corrections, which are of great interest for experimental implementations.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中T色心兼具O波段光跃迁与长相干自旋，但零声子线（ZPL）发射弱导致测量缓慢；高Q腔虽可增强ZPL，却需逐器件调谐到T色心波长，妨碍对T色心性质的统计性研究。

Method: 研制低Q值、共聚焦可寻址的微腔阵列：制作400个腔，与T色心波长失谐0.0±1.1 nm，平均品质因子298±78；以高对准精度配合中等Q值消除单腔调谐需求，并结合蒙特卡洛模拟分析腔增强统计分布。

Conclusion: 低Q共聚焦腔阵列在免调谐条件下实现显著增强与加速，并为色心密度的免标定测量及T色心群体统计研究提供新途径。

Abstract: T centres in silicon combine an optical transition in the telecom O-band with a long-lived electron and nuclear spin in a photonics- and CMOS-compatible host. However, weak emission of photons in the zero-phonon line (ZPL) makes measurements slow. Cavities can increase the ZPL photon emission, but high-Q cavities require individual tuning to the T centre wavelength, hindering statistical understanding of T centre properties. Here, we overcome this limitation by developing low-Q confocally-addressable cavities. We create an array of 400 cavities, detuned $0.0\pm 1.1$ nm from the T centre, with an average quality factor of $298\pm 78$. The combination of high accuracy and moderate quality factors removes the need for individual tuning. The array yields a median T centre signal increase of $6.5\times$, corresponding to a $42\times$ measurement speedup. Studying the cavity statistics enables new measurements: comparing to a Monte Carlo simulation we infer a T centre density in the sample of $4.2^{+1.3}_{-0.8}~\mathrm{μm^{-2}}$, without relying on an estimate for the setup collection efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [Extractable Work from Collective Dark-State Charging of Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15416)
*Jian-Jian Cheng,Ming-Liang Hu*

Abstract: We consider two ensembles of superconducting qubits coupled to a common lossy resonator, one serving as the charger and the other the battery. Destructive interference between their collective decay amplitudes selects a tunable family of many-body dark states, allowing controlled excitation transfer from the charger to the battery. The collective charging dynamics preserves the symmetric Dicke subspace of the battery, so its reduced state has at most $N+1$ nonzero eigenvalues. Together with the $N$-fold degeneracy of the battery's first excited energy level, this restriction keeps the passive energy of the entire battery below the single-qubit excitation energy $ω_0$, independently of the battery size. When the average stored energy per battery qubit is fixed and nonzero, the total stored energy grows linearly with $N$, while the ratio of global ergotropy to stored energy approaches unity as $N$ increases.

</details>


### [102] [Subadditivity and the failure of strong subadditivity in the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15431)
*Lin Zhang,Wenlin Zhang,Haobo Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明Nichols–Wootters熵族（介于von Neumann熵与subentropy之间）在统一环境维数下满足次可加性；除von Neumann熵外，等式成立当且仅当至少一个边缘态为纯态，从而解决subentropy次可加性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nichols–Wootters熵族在von Neumann熵与subentropy之间插值，其subentropy的次可加性一直是未解猜想；同时该族熵的非标准性质（强次可加性、缺陷行为、纠缠态下的界）缺乏系统刻画，需要统一框架来理解并给出定量结果。

Method: 在共同环境维数下对所有谱进行评估；利用压缩矩阵熵的Haar平均正表示，导出作为核心工具的pinching不等式；结合秩约束下的乘积间隙分析、边缘纯度下界估计，以及两量子比特态下最优系数的确定。

Result: 建立次可加性并证明除von Neumann熵外等式仅当某边缘为纯态时成立；该族除von Neumann熵外均不满足强次可加性（在最大纠缠两量子比特态张量混态量子比特上即失效）；强次可加性缺陷在量子Markov态上取正负两号；对纯双分态张量辅助态，缺陷等于一个各自凹的乘积间隙并给出秩约束下的精确最大值；任意双分态得到关于边缘纯度的显式下界；两量子比特态下确定各共同环境维数下乘较小边缘特征值乘积的最优系数（subentropy对应1/3）；并给出无孔量子信道的熵增界。

Conclusion: 该族熵的次可加性与强次可加性行为被完整刻画，subentropy猜想得证；压缩矩阵熵的Haar平均表示是统一证明与界的核心工具，并可用于量子信道熵增分析及作为指定各向同性部分投影测量下保持的经典通信容量。

Abstract: We prove subadditivity for the Nichols--Wootters family of entropies interpolating between the von Neumann entropy and the subentropy, with all spectra evaluated in one common ambient dimension. Beyond the von Neumann entropy, equality holds exactly when at least one marginal is pure. This settles the subadditivity conjecture concerning subentropy. Every member except the von Neumann entropy fails strong subadditivity, already on a maximally entangled two-qubit state tensored with a mixed qubit. Its strong-subadditivity defect takes both signs on quantum Markov states. For a pure bipartite state tensored with an ancillary state, we identify the defect with a separately concave product gap and determine its exact maximum under rank constraints. For arbitrary bipartite states, we obtain an explicit lower bound in the marginal purities. For two-qubit states, we determine the optimal coefficient multiplying the product of the smaller marginal eigenvalues in every common ambient dimension, including the subentropy coefficient $1/3$. A positive representation by Haar averages of compressed matrix entropies gives the pinching inequality underlying these results and also yields entropy-gain bounds for unital quantum channels.As an application, each entropy equals the classical communication capacity retained after a specified isotropic partial projective measurement, at a prescribed average input state, even when entangled block encodings are allowed.

</details>


### [103] [The infrared vacuum reduces the laser energy needed to break molecular bonds of simple molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15450)
*Johan F. Triana,Felipe Herrera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究单个分子与红外纳米腔共振模式强耦合下的红外光解离，表明通过向腔场注入光子可比直接驱动分子降低解离所需驱动能量达三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在室温红外纳米腔中实现了少分子振动强耦合，为利用受限电磁真空调控化学反应提供新途径，但量子化电磁模式如何改变反应性的内在机制尚不清楚。

Method: 将单分子与共振红外纳米腔模式强耦合，用单色红外激光驱动，可分别直接振动激发分子或向腔场注入光子；对比腔内CO2与H2O分子的腔内动力学，分析核非谐性、强光-物质耦合与真空场极化（依分子键偶极性质）之间的相互作用。

Result: 腔内注入光子路径所需解离驱动能量比直接驱动分子低最多三个数量级；光解离增强源于核非谐性、强光-物质耦合与真空场极化的共同作用。

Conclusion: 研究为在腔量子电动力学框架下理解化学过程调控、设计少分子级纳米光子学实验提供了概念与数值工具。

Abstract: Vibrational strong coupling within a few molecules has recently been demonstrated in infrared nanocavities at room temperature, opening new alternatives to explore chemical reactions assisted by the confined electromagnetic vacuum. However, the intrinsic mechanisms of how the quantized electromagnetic mode modifies reactivity are not fully understood. We study infrared photodissociation of a single molecule strongly coupled to a resonant infrared nanocavity mode. The coupled system is driven by a monochromatic infrared laser that vibrationally excites the molecule directly or injects photons into the nanocavity field. We compare the intracavity dynamics of CO2 and H2O molecules and demonstrate that the driving energy required for dissociation is up to three orders of magnitude lower inside the cavity by injecting photons into the nanocavity than by driving the molecule directly. We show that photodissociation enhancements are due to the interplay between nuclear anharmonicity, stronglight-mattercoupling, and vacuum field polarisation according to the dipolar nature of the molecular bond. Our results provide the conceptual and numerical tools for understanding how to control chemical processes and to design nanophotonics experiments at the few-molecule level within a cavity quantum electrodynamics perspective.

</details>


### [104] [Restoring Heisenberg Scaling in Hamiltonian Estimation despite Noise Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15487)
*Yan Luo,Lingna Wang,Hongzhen Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在噪声参数未知、需与哈密顿量参数联合估计的情形下，噪声参数的不确定性可能破坏原本可达的Heisenberg标度；作者对任意量子控制下的马尔可夫动力学给出了各哈密顿量参数保留该标度的充要条件及最优渐近精度，并在条件失效时提出借助对哈密顿量参数不敏感、却携带独立同分布噪声副本的参考系统来恢复该标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制可抵消噪声对相干性与传感精度的破坏，但既有策略普遍假定噪声已知；实际中噪声往往仅被部分刻画，其参数须与目标哈密顿量参数联合估计，因此需要厘清噪声参数不确定性对量子计量基本极限的影响。

Method: 针对一般马尔可夫动力学与任意量子控制，推导每个哈密顿量参数保持Heisenberg标度的充要条件，并给出条件成立时的最优渐近精度；条件不满足时，设计引入噪声参考系统的协议，并证明对哈密顿量参数不敏感、携带传感器噪声独立副本的参考系统可恢复已知噪声下的最优精度。

Result: 表明即使相干性得以保持，噪声参数的不确定性仍足以摧毁原本可达的Heisenberg标度；给出标度保持的可判定判据与相应渐近精度表达式；所提参考系统协议可恢复已知噪声情形下的最优精度（渐进意义上）。

Conclusion: 噪声的不完全知识会实质性改变量子度量的基本极限，所提条件与参考系统方案为在真实环境中实现量子增强精度提供了理论判据与可行途径，对噪声不确定性下的量子传感具有广泛意义。

Abstract: Noise degrades quantum coherence and limits the precision of quantum sensors. Quantum control can counteract these effects, but existing strategies typically assume that the noise is known. In practice, noise is often only partially characterized, so its parameters must be estimated jointly with the Hamiltonian parameters of interest. Here we show that uncertainty in the noise parameters can destroy otherwise attainable Heisenberg scaling, even when coherence is preserved. For general Markovian dynamics under arbitrary quantum control, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for each Hamiltonian parameter to retain this scaling and determine its optimal asymptotic precision when the condition holds. When the condition fails, we propose a protocol that uses a suitable noisy reference system to restore this scaling, and prove that a reference insensitive to the Hamiltonian parameters but subject to an independent copy of the sensor's noise recovers the optimal precision attainable with known noise. Our results reveal how incomplete knowledge of the noise affects the fundamental limits of quantum metrology, with broad implications for achieving quantum-enhanced precision in realistic environments.

</details>


### [105] [Feedback Control and Neural-Network-Based Prediction of Quantum Entanglement in Fano-mirror cavity optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15516)
*Ya-Feng Jiao,Ze-Yu Zhu,Jie Wang,Qing-Shou Tan,Wen-Qiang Fang,Le-Man Kuang,Hui Jing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于带相干反馈回路的Fano镜腔光力系统的双体光力纠缠方案，利用Fano镜形成的闭合三体相互作用回路中的量子干涉，结合相干反馈与光学频率失配增强纠缠，并用监督式前馈神经网络从可测光信号预测纠缠大小，避免完整量子态层析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观尺度物体质心运动的量子纠缠在基础科学与量子技术中意义重大，但宏观非经典态的制备与验证仍极具挑战；需要一种全光学、实验可行的方法来产生、调控与表征宏观光力纠缠。

Method: 采用含相干反馈回路的Fano镜腔光力系统，腔模与Fano光学模通过相干与耗散相互作用耦合；Fano镜与腔模、机械模构成闭合三体相互作用回路，直接与间接相互作用路径间发生量子干涉；引入相干反馈并调节光学频率失配以进一步调控纠缠；训练监督式前馈神经网络，由易测光信号预测纠缠量。

Result: 量子干涉显著增强腔-机械纠缠并产生鲁棒的Fano-机械纠缠；相干反馈与适当的光学频率失配可进一步增强纠缠；训练后的神经网络能仅凭可测光信号准确预测纠缠大小，放宽了量子态层析的要求。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了全光学、实验可行的宏观光力纠缠产生、调控与表征途径，在量子信息处理中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement involving the centre-of-mass motion of macroscopic-scale objects plays an essential role in both fundamental science and emerging quantum technologies, yet generating and verifying such macroscopic nonclassical states remain formidable challenges. Here, we report a theoretical scheme to effectively generate, manipulate, and predict bipartite optomechanical entanglement based on a Fano-mirror cavity optomechanical system with a coherent feedback loop, where the cavity and Fano optical modes couple through coherent and dissipative interactions. We show that the Fano mirror forms a closed tripartite interaction loop with the cavity and mechanical modes, whereby the quantum interference between direct and indirect interaction pathways substantially enhances the cavity-mechanical entanglement and gives rise to a robust Fano-mechanical entanglement. More importantly, we find that coherent feedback combined with an appropriate optical frequency mismatch can further enhance the entanglement, and a trained supervised feedforward neural network can be used to predict the entanglement magnitude from the readily measurable optical signals, relaxing the requirement of full quantum-state tomography. Our work, establishing an all-optical and experimentally feasible approach to generate, manipulate, and characterize the macroscopic optomechanical entanglement, holds great potential for applications in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum non-Gaussian coherence via dissipation to single qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15547)
*Sirachat Kaewkongtham,Kimin Park,Darren W. Moore,Atirach Ritboon,Radim Filip*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出以脉冲高斯控制交替作用于线性振子与最小非线性元件的耗散（未测量基态量子比特、共振JC耦合），可确定性产生高阶量子非高斯相干，包括高保真(|0⟩+|N⟩)/√2（N≤4）、二项码态与逻辑magic态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子非高斯相干（QNG coherence）是玻色系统中先进量子技术的关键资源，且与高斯态所提供的相干在操作上本质不同；然而在多数实验平台上其确定性制备仍是严峻挑战。

Method: 将脉冲高斯控制与耗散过程交替组合：耗散由最小非线性元件——一个未被测量的基态量子比特经共振Jaynes-Cummings相互作用引起。该耗散过程本身相位不敏感、单独不产生局部相干，但与高斯操作结合后可确定性产生目标非高斯态，并用QNG相干层级进行认证。

Result: 确定性产生高质量的低Fock态二分量叠加(|0⟩+|N⟩)/√2（最高N=4），保真度>0.98；同时高保真（>0.98）制备二项码态与逻辑magic态，均通过QNG相干层级认证。

Conclusion: 相位不敏感的最小非线性耗散与高斯控制协同即可确定性生成高阶QNG相干及编码资源态，为玻色量子技术提供了一条可实验实现的确定性资源制备途径。

Abstract: Quantum non-Gaussian (QNG) coherence is a critical resource for advanced quantum technologies with bosonic systems, operationally distinct from the coherence provided by Gaussian states. For many experimental platforms, its deterministic generation remains a formidable experimental challenge. Here we propose that pulsed Gaussian control of a linear oscillator alternating with dissipation by the smallest nonlinear element -- an unmeasured ground-state qubit coupled via resonant Jaynes-Cummings (JC) interactions -- deterministically generates high-order QNG coherence. Although such dissipative processes are phase-insensitive and do not generate local coherence alone, when added to Gaussian operations they deterministically produce high-quality binary superpositions of lower Fock states $(|0\rangle + |N\rangle)/\sqrt{2}$ up to $N=4$ with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$), certified by the QNG coherence hierarchy, as well as binomial code states and logical magic states with high fidelity ($\mathcal{F} > 0.98$).

</details>


### [107] [Defect-induced stabilization of Wigner negativity and parity-protected phase-locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15624)
*Beatrice Donelli,Stefano Gherardini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 局域缺陷型宇称保持δ势将量子van der Pol振子的连续U(1)对称性破缺为离散ℤ₂对称性，从而产生宇称保护的偶宇称稳态量子相干与Wigner函数负性，并伴随宇称保护的量子锁相；论文给出基于光镊中超冷类碱土原子运动态的可行实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子van der Pol振子具有连续U(1)对称性，其稳态缺乏受保护的量子相干与非经典性。作者希望引入一种能模拟局域缺陷且不破坏宇称的机制，在保留宇称对称性的前提下获得受保护的非经典稳态与量子相干。

Method: 在量子van der Pol振子中引入宇称保持的δ形势（模拟局域缺陷），使连续U(1)对称性降为ℤ₂对称性；结合非线性耗散通道，分析缺陷与耗散通道的协同作用对稳态宇称、相干及相位锁定的影响；并提出在光镊中利用类碱土原子运动态的实验实现：以态选择接触相互作用工程化局域缺陷，以Raman边带驱动非线性耗散通道。

Result: ℤ₂对称性保证了受保护的偶宇称稳态量子相干，稳态呈现Wigner函数负性，即非经典态；缺陷与非线性耗散通道的非平凡耦合导致宇称保护的量子锁相，使振子能级同时锁定于两个相反相位。

Conclusion: 宇称保持的局域缺陷可作为一种对称性调控手段，将连续对称性约化为离散宇称对称性，从而稳定地产生并保护量子相干、Wigner负性与双相位锁定等非经典效应；该机制在超冷类碱土原子光镊平台上具有现实可实施性。

Abstract: We show that a parity-preserving delta-shaped potential, mimicking the presence of a localized defect, reduces the continuous $U(1)$-symmetry of a quantum van der Pol oscillator to the discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetry. This mechanism guarantees protected even-parity stationary quantum coherences, resulting in the generation of non-classical steady-states characterized by Wigner function negativity. Thanks to a non-trivial interplay between the defect and non-linear dissipative channels, this kind of negativity entails parity-protected quantum phase locking, meaning that the oscillator's energy levels simultaneously lock into two opposite phases. Finally, we present a realistic experimental proposal involving the motional state of ultracold alkaline-earth-like atoms in optical tweezers, where the localized defect is engineered via state-selective contact interactions, while the non-linear dissipative channels are driven by Raman sidebands.

</details>


### [108] [Exact Mixed-Locality Encodings for Resource-Constrained QAOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15663)
*Jingcong Liu,Yiding Su,Ruojun Zhang,Hafiz Muhammad Waseem,Bo Chen,Carsten Maple*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一类点态精确的哈密顿量表示族：将部分高阶交互用共享Rosenberg辅助比特替换、其余保持原生形式，并给出单步惩罚条件与三次残差闭式刻画其精确性；进而用静态可行性理论证明存在"仅混合表示可行"的目标族，并在编译与路由实验中量化其范围，同时指出资源可行不等于解质量更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶目标存在多种精确的哈密顿量编码，它们在量子比特数、惩罚项、门与线路深度上各不相同；在资源受限硬件上运行QAOA时，表示选择本质上是一个算法决策问题，需要理论依据来判定何时混合/选择性约简编码比纯原生或全约简编码更可行。

Method: 构造点态精确的选择性约简表示族（共享Rosenberg辅助比特+原生交互共存）；给出尖锐的单步惩罚条件及三次残差闭式，证明各阶段精确性；在"直接对"类中建立静态可行性理论，识别联合预算下混合表示可行、而原生与全约简两端点均不可行的无限目标族；另给出固定综合方案下将分离性推广到门数与深度的构造；在12比特线/环/网格目标上编译，并做路由实验与无噪声depth-one评测。

Result: 编译实验在30个加权最大三可满足实例中23个、全部30个构造实例、30个三次自旋玻璃实例中2个上发现经认证的"仅混合可行"区域；路由实验在五个随机种子下于固定候选集内保留52.9%的仅混合可行预算点；无噪声depth-one评测中原生编码取得最佳平均目标值，主预算下联合资源-质量测试未发现仅混合的成功案例。

Conclusion: 资源可行性是选择混合编码的可靠理由，但解质量需单独评估；实际选型应区分"能否放进硬件预算"与"是否能获得更优解"这两个独立问题。

Abstract: Higher-order objectives possess many exact Hamiltonian encodings, and these encodings differ in the qubits, penalties, gates, and depth that they require. Representation selection is therefore an algorithmic decision for the quantum approximate optimisation algorithm on resource-constrained hardware. This work develops a pointwise-exact family of representations in which selected higher-order interactions are replaced by shared Rosenberg auxiliaries while the remaining interactions stay native. A sharp single-step penalty condition and a closed form for cubic residuals certify exactness at every stage of such a selective reduction. Within the direct-pair class, a static feasibility theory identifies infinite families of objectives for which a mixed representation meets joint budgets that exclude both the native and the fully reduced endpoint. A separate construction extends this separation to gate and depth limits under a fixed synthesis. Compilation on 12-qubit line, ring, and grid targets finds certified mixed-only regions for 23 of 30 weighted maximum three-satisfiability instances, all 30 constructed instances, and 2 of 30 cubic spin-glass instances under the stated class and penalty rule. A separate routing experiment retains 52.9% of mixed-only budget points across five seeds within fixed candidate sets. In noiseless depth-one evaluation, the native encoding attains the best mean objective under the tested protocols, and the joint resource-and-quality test finds no mixed-only success at the primary budget. Resource feasibility is thus a sound reason to select a mixed encoding, whereas solution quality requires a separate assessment.

</details>


### [109] [Reply to the Comment by Hradil and Řeháček](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16716)
*Jens Arnbak Holbøll Nielsen,Jonas S. Neergaard-Nielsen,Tobias Gehring,Ulrik L. Andersen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对Hradil与Řeháček的批评进行反驳，指出其关于压缩真空相位传感量子优势消失的结论在数学上不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对他人声称"在恰当计入测量重复次数与总光子资源后，压缩真空相位传感实验的量子度量学优势消失"提出反批评。

Method: 概念与数学层面的论证反驳：分别检验资源核算与有限样本统计两类质疑的推理有效性。

Result: 无论考虑资源核算还是有限样本统计，均无法否定已展示的量子增强局域相位灵敏度。

Conclusion: Hradil与Řeháček的结论缺乏数学依据，原有实验所展示的量子增强局域相位灵敏度依然成立。

Abstract: Hradil and Řeháček argue that the quantum metrological advantage demonstrated in our squeezed-vacuum phase-sensing experiment disappears, when measurement repetitions and total photon resources are properly accounted for. We show that this conclusion is mathematically unfounded and that neither resource accounting nor finite-sample statistical considerations invalidate the demonstrated quantum-enhanced local phase sensitivity.

</details>


### [110] [Critical excited states in quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15683)
*Marek Kuchař,Michal Macek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究强耦合量子比特-玻色子系统中基态与激发态量子相变附近的临界增强传感，考虑有限尺寸效应设计驱动协议，利用发散磁化率提升参数估计信噪比，并证明激发态临界可借助单模玻色压缩规避“绝热性死亡”，实现超越常规基态临界量子度量的计量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规临界量子度量依赖基态临界性，受“绝热性死亡”（adiabaticity death）限制：临界点能隙闭合导致驱动变慢、协议时间发散，实际有限尺寸系统难以维持绝热。因此需要寻找可规避该瓶颈、且在实验可及驱动下仍具计量增益的临界资源。

Method: 在强耦合量子比特-玻色子模型中，分别考察基态与激发态量子相变；引入有限尺寸标度处理能隙与磁化率；基于发散磁化率与实验相关产生元（generator）设计有限时间驱动协议，以信噪比/费舍尔信息为核心指标比较基态与激发态临界方案的计量表现，并分析单模玻色压缩的作用。

Result: 激发态临界性在单模玻色压缩机制下提供可实现的计量优势；该方案处于规避“绝热性死亡”的参数区间，其信噪比提升不依赖发散的绝热时间，因而在有限尺寸、实验可及条件下优于常规基态临界度量。

Conclusion: 激发态量子相变可作为实用化的临界计量资源：配合有限尺寸修正的驱动协议与玻色压缩，可在不受绝热性限制的区间内提升参数估计精度，为强耦合光-物质平台的临界传感提供可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate criticality-enhanced sensing near ground- and excited-state quantum phase transitions in a strongly coupled qubit-boson system. Accounting for finite-size effects, we design driving protocols that exploit diverging susceptibilities to enhance signal-to-noise ratios in parameter estimation using experimentally relevant generators. We show that the excited-state criticality provides a metrological advantage through single-mode bosonic squeezing in regimes that circumvent the 'death of adiabaticity', limiting conventional critical quantum metrology with ground-state criticality.

</details>


### [111] [Non-Convex Optimization via Quantum Tunneling Search on a Photonic Chip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16791)
*Xi-Jun Yuan,Shuo Zhou,Yizhou Liu,Xuan Chen,Hong-Jing Du,Xiao-Wen Shang,Wen-Hao Zhou,Yu-Xuan Fu,Bao-Jing Liu,Ze-Feng Lan,Hang Song,Fang-Song Nie,Yu-Lin Yu,Xiao-Yun Xu,Jian-Peng Dou,Tongyang Li,Hao Tang,Xian-Min Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子隧穿搜索（QTS）算法，结合量子隧穿行走与局部退火，在飞秒激光直写波导阵列上实验演示了1D和2D非凸优化景观的高效求解，并展现出相对经典SGD的量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中大量目标函数与约束具有非凸性质，其景观存在众多局部极小与鞍点，严重阻碍基于梯度下降的方法探索全局最优。

Method: 提出QTS算法，基于量子隧穿行走与局部退火利用隧穿效应穿越势垒；实验上通过飞秒直写技术调控折射率，将目标函数映射为波导哈密顿量的势能项，在间距10微米的29×1与21×9波导阵列上输入特定初态；进一步引入变化哈密顿量方法增强搜索。

Result: 平均概率分布在1D和2D非凸景观上有效凸显各局部极小；变化哈密顿量方法实现了更高效搜索乃至全局最小值的识别；实验验证了相对经典随机梯度下降（SGD）的量子加速。

Conclusion: QTS是一种成功且高效的非凸优化求解器，展示了近期模拟量子模拟器在机器学习领域的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Non-convex optimization plays an important role in modern machine learning since plenty of real-world problems have objective functions or constraints with non-convex properties. The non-convex nature of landscapes gives rise to numerous local minima and saddle points, dramatically hindering the ability of gradient descent-based methods to explore the landscape and obtain global optima. Here we present quantum tunneling search (QTS), an algorithm based on quantum tunneling walk and local annealing, which effectively explores the non-convex landscape by leveraging the quantum tunneling effect. In experiments, we map an objective function to the potential term of the waveguide Hamiltonian by controlling the refractive index with the femtosecond direct laser writing technique. With a proper input state, the average probability distributions on 10-micrometer-spaced 29 x 1 and 21 x 9 waveguide arrays highlight the local minima of various 1D and 2D non-convex landscapes, respectively. By further introducing the varying Hamiltonian approach, more efficient search and even the identification of the global minimum are achieved. Our work experimentally verifies that QTS is a successful and efficient non-convex optimization solver, demonstrating quantum speedups over classical stochastic gradient descent (SGD), which serves as a practical application of near-term analog quantum simulators in the field of machine learning.

</details>


### [112] [Enhanced Entanglement Gate Fidelity via Correlated and Synchronized Atom Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15819)
*Christopher W. Wächtler,Frederic Hummel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在里德堡阻塞双量子比特受控相位门中，运动关联在Lamb-Dicke参数最低阶下可抑制运动诱导的误差，从而降低门失真度，使原子运动关联成为一种高保真中性原子门的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子镊阵列中的里德堡阻塞门受原子热运动引起的误差限制，而工程化的集体耗散可产生运动关联甚至同步。作者希望探究这种运动关联能否作为抑制门误差的资源。

Method: 在Lamb-Dicke参数的最低阶展开下，解析推导受控相位门失真度中运动贡献的表达式，并证明其仅由双原子运动占据数与运动关联决定、与具体门协议无关；随后以关联热态和同步量子van der Pol振子为例进行验证。

Result: 门失真度中的运动贡献仅依赖于两原子运动占据数与关联，且与门协议无关；对关联热态和同步van der Pol振子，在有限温度下运动关联均相对于独立热原子情形降低了门失真度。

Conclusion: 原子运动关联可作为实现高保真中性原子门的一种资源，并可能应用于模拟量子模拟器。

Abstract: Engineered collective dissipation can correlate and even synchronize the motion of quantum systems. We show that such motional correlations can suppress motion-induced errors in two-qubit controlled-phase gates based on the Rydberg blockade in neutral-atom tweezer arrays. To leading order in the Lamb-Dicke parameter, the motional contribution to the gate infidelity is determined solely by the two-atom motional occupations and correlations, and is independent of the gate protocol. We illustrate this result for correlated thermal states and synchronized quantum van der Pol oscillators. In each case, motional correlations reduce the gate infidelity relative to independent thermal atoms at finite temperature. Our results establish atomic-motion correlations as a resource for high-fidelity neutral-atom gates and may also find applications in analog quantum simulators.

</details>


### [113] [Hong$-$Ou$-$Mandel-like interference between two independent narrowband thermal states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16794)
*Sheng-Hsuan Huang,Thomas Dirmeier,Andrea Aiello,Kaisa Laiho,Dmitry V. Strekalov,Gerd Leuchs,Christoph Marquardt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出在单光子功率水平下利用热态间的HOM类干涉测量两个窄带光源频率差的方法，可从时间符合计数中提取频率差的绝对值，并可用于寻找两个预报单光子源的零失谐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子网络需要大量不可区分的窄带光子源以直接接口原子量子存储，但独立窄带源之间的频率匹配（频率差测量与零失谐锁定）通常十分困难，且需在单光子功率量级下完成。

Method: 研究两个热态之间的Hong-Ou-Mandel类（HOM-like）干涉，通过测量时间符合计数随延迟的分布来提取频率差；并与理论模型对比，同时将热态HOM类干涉的拍频振荡曲线与预报单光子源HOM干涉曲线进行比较。

Result: 时间符合计数可提取两光源频率差的绝对值，实验结果与理论模型吻合良好；热态HOM类干涉的拍频信号具有与预报单光子源HOM干涉相似的振荡轮廓。

Conclusion: 该方案为单光子功率下窄带光源频率差的测量提供了可行途径，并证实其可用于确定两个预报单光子源的零失谐，从而支持构建不可区分的大规模量子网络光源。

Abstract: Narrowband photonic sources are promising candidates in quantum networks for a direct interface with atomic quantum memories. However, building a large-scale quantum network requires many indistinguishable sources, and matching the frequencies of independent narrowband sources is generally challenging. Here, we propose a method to measure the frequency difference between two narrowband light sources in the single-photon power regime by studying Hong$-$Ou$-$Mandel-like (HOM) interference between thermal states. Our results show that the absolute value of the frequency difference can be extracted from the temporal coincidence counts, and these results agree well with our theoretical model. We also demonstrate that the beat signal of the HOM-like interference between two thermal states has a similar oscillation profile to the HOM interference between two heralded single-photon sources, confirming that this method can be used to find the zero detuning of two heralded single-photon sources.

</details>


### [114] [k-partite quantum homomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15839)
*Amin Karamlou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入k-分体同态博弈，将k-XOR博弈推广到任意关系结构，用态依赖分级量子单子刻画完美策略，并证明同步量子优势与k-分体量子优势在CSP实例层面不可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-分体XOR博弈与线性BCS博弈源自Z_2上的线性方程组，但对应不同的量子松弛；线性BCS博弈已被推广为关系结构上的同步同态博弈（由量子单子给出组合语义）。作者希望把k-XOR博弈从线性系统推广到任意关系结构，从而建立多体量子CSP与量子同态的框架。

Method: 引入k-分体同态博弈；在张量积模型与交换算子模型中，借助k-分体关系结构范畴上合适的态依赖分级量子单子的Kleisli态射刻画完美策略；再以"伪心灵感应"意义下的量子优势（存在完美量子策略而无完美经典策略）为研究对象，比较同步量子优势与k-分体量子优势，并考察具体实例及有界宽度模板。

Result: 同步量子优势与k-分体量子优势在CSP实例层面不可比：GHZ实例具三体量子优势但无同步交换算子优势；魔方实例具同步量子优势，但对任意玩家数与任意正常着色均无k-分体交换算子优势。另给出一个六元素模板，其CSP为NP完全且具二体量子优势。最后证明任何有界宽度CSP模板均不具k-分体量子优势，即使在交换算子模型下亦然。

Conclusion: k-分体同态博弈提供了统一刻画多体量子CSP与量子同态的单子论框架；两类量子优势互不包含，且量子优势的存在性与模板宽度紧密相关——有界宽度模板不可能具备k-分体量子优势。

Abstract: $k$-partite XOR games and linear binary constraint system (BCS) games arise from systems of linear equations over $\mathbb{Z}_2$, providing distinct quantum relaxations of the same classical constraint satisfaction problem (CSP). Linear BCS games have been generalised to synchronous homomorphism games on arbitrary relational structures, with compositional semantics provided by the quantum monad.
  We introduce $k$-partite homomorphism games, extending $k$-XOR games from linear systems to arbitrary relational structures. This yields a framework for studying multipartite quantum CSPs and quantum homomorphisms. We characterise perfect strategies in both the tensor-product and commuting-operator models as Kleisli morphisms of suitable state-dependent graded quantum monads on a category of $k$-partite relational structures.
  We then study quantum advantage in the pseudotelepathic sense: the existence of a perfect quantum strategy without a perfect classical strategy. We show that synchronous and $k$-partite quantum advantage are incomparable at the level of CSP instances. The GHZ instance admits three-player quantum advantage but no synchronous commuting-operator advantage, whereas the magic-square instance admits synchronous quantum advantage but no $k$-partite commuting-operator advantage for any number of players or proper colouring. We also exhibit an explicit six-element template whose CSP is NP-complete and admits two-player quantum advantage. Finally, we prove that no bounded-width CSP template admits $k$-partite quantum advantage, even in the commuting-operator model.

</details>


### [115] [Lorentz Boosts and Governing Equations of the Quantum Geometric Tensor in 4D Energy-Momentum Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16967)
*Yiming Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将量子几何张量推广到四维能量-动量空间，通过洛伦兹变换由静态Dirac/Weyl系统生成频率度规、四极频率-动量剪切度规和类电磁Berry-Maxwell场，并给出场方程与流守恒律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量中的量子度规与Berry曲率分别在能带几何与反常输运中扮演核心角色，但既有研究局限于三维动量空间；作者希望把频率作为独立坐标纳入，构建能量-动量空间中的统一量子几何框架。

Method: 以实空间时空平面波相位的洛伦兹不变性固定能量-动量空间中的洛伦兹变换；对静态Dirac与Weyl系统施加洛伦兹助推，生成频率度规、四极频率-动量剪切度规与类电性Berry-Maxwell场；识别五个洛伦兹不变缩并，由静态Weyl几何与洛伦兹协变性推导量子度规和Berry曲率的场方程及流守恒律；并建立QGT高斯谱振幅与四维时空局域高斯事件的对应。

Result: 得到五个洛伦兹不变缩并、量子度规与Berry曲率的协变场方程和流守恒律；揭示频率度规与剪切度规的生成机制；给出QGT谱振幅与时空局域事件之间的映射；讨论了在时空晶体与含时介质中的实验实现途径。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了类引力度规与类电磁规范场统一的四维量子几何，将几何自洽性与物理事件动力学联系起来，并指出时空晶体/含时介质可作为实验检验平台。

Abstract: In solids, the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), $Q_{μν}=g_{μν}-\frac{i}{2} Ω_{μν}$, combines the quantum metric and Berry curvature. Its imaginary part governs anomalous velocity and quantized Hall transport, while its real part determines the quantum weight and geometric contributions to flat-band superfluid stiffness and dielectric response. Here, we extend the QGT to four-dimensional (4D) energy-momentum space ($ω/c,k$), treating frequency and momentum as independent coordinates. Invariance of the real-spacetime plane-wave phase fixes their Lorentz transformation in energy-momentum space. Lorentz boosts of static Dirac and Weyl systems then generate frequency metrics, quadrupolar frequency-momentum shear metrics, and electric-like Berry-Maxwell fields. We identify five Lorentz-invariant contractions and derive the governing field equations and flow conservation laws for both the quantum metric and Berry curvature from static Weyl geometry and Lorentz covariance. We further connect Gaussian spectral amplitudes of the QGT to localized Gaussian events in 4D spacetime. Finally, we discuss experimental realizations in spacetime crystals or time-varying media and clarify how this unified quantum geometry of a gravitational-like metric and electromagnetic-like gauge fields connects geometric self-consistency with physical event dynamics.

</details>


### [116] [Revealing Bell Nonlocality Does Not Require the Same Physical Measurement to be Performed Twice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15871)
*Paweł Cieśliński,Carlotta Versmold,Jan Wójcik,Eun Mi Kim,Jan Dziewior,Paweł Kurzyński,Tomasz Paterek,Harald Weinfurter,Wiesław Laskowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明即使测量方向从不重复，Bell 不等式的违反仍可存在并保持操作意义，并用极化纠缠光子实验（每个随机选取的分析方向只探测一对粒子）予以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 Bell 非局域性分析假设一小组预定义测量方向能被精确复现，但受基本原理或技术原因（如测量装置变化）影响，这些方向可能完全无法复现，需要考察此时 Bell 违反是否仍然成立。

Method: 理论上放宽"测量方向可精确复现"的假设，允许测量方向永不重复；实验上采用偏振纠缠光子源，对每个随机选择的分析方向仅探测一对粒子对，以检验单次、不重复测量下的 Bell 不等式违反。

Result: 实验证实，在测量方向从不重复的条件下 Bell 不等式仍被违反，且该违反保持其操作意义（可用于相关量子信息任务）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了 Bell 不等式违反对测量不可重复性的鲁棒性，将 Bell 检验与随机化测量框架联系起来，并把量子信息协议（器件认证、通信复杂度、量子密码）拓展到测量控制受限的情形。

Abstract: Bell inequality violations, or Bell nonlocality for short, is not only central to testing the foundations of quantum physics but have also become an essential ingredient of numerous quantum information protocols, from quantum key distribution to the device-independent certification of randomness and secure communication. Existing analyses of Bell nonlocality assume that a small set of predefined measurement directions can be reproduced exactly. But what if these directions cannot be reproduced at all? This may happen for fundamental or technical reasons, such as variations in the measurement apparatus. Here we show that the violation of a Bell inequality remains possible and that it also retains its operational significance, even when the measurement directions never repeat. We experimentally confirm these results using polarization-entangled photons, detecting only one particle pair per randomly chosen analyzer direction. Our results establish the robustness of Bell inequality violations against imperfect measurement repeatability and further connect Bell tests with the broader framework of randomized measurements. Moreover, they extend quantum information protocols to settings with limited measurement control, including quantum device certification, communication complexity, and quantum cryptography.

</details>


### [117] [Superradiant photon bunching in nanolasers: from a polarization-order hierarchy of the master equation to Dicke Markov chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15898)
*Matias Bundgaard-Nielsen,Jesper Mørk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究纳米激光器中量子发射体集体Dicke态跃迁图像的有效性，从激光主方程推导按极化阶数截断的模型层级：完全消去相干得到Dicke马尔可夫链（仅通过增强/抑制跃迁速率引入集体效应），保留光学极化并消去双光子相干得到布居-极化模型。该极化阶层级可作为累积量展开的替代方案，且在最高阶回到主方程；Dicke马尔可夫链可解析求解至数百发射体、采样至数千发射体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米激光器中少量至数百个量子发射体共享单一光模，形成集体亮态与暗态，在阈值下产生超辐射脉冲与强聚束光子统计。作者希望厘清这一集体行为在多大程度上可理解为集体Dicke态之间的随机跳跃，从而在保持物理图像直观性的同时准确复现主方程的统计特性。

Method: 从激光主方程出发，按极化阶数（即发射体与光之间相干所涉及的光子数）构造模型层级：逐一消去各阶相干，完全消去后得到Dicke马尔可夫链——在光子数与Dicke态上的随机跳跃过程；保留一阶极化、消去二阶（双光子）相干则得到布居-极化模型。同时采用对称性约化的主方程作为数值精确参考，并比较各方法的数值性能。

Result: Dicke马尔可夫链预言超热聚束及对发射体数目的强奇偶依赖，在最相干区域（常规速率方程预言的超热聚束为零）可复现约80%的聚束；保留一阶极化的布居-极化模型几乎完美复现全主方程的聚束。对称性约化主方程将模拟范围扩展至阈值下34个、阈值上24个发射体；Dicke马尔可夫链可直接求解数百发射体、采样数千发射体。

Conclusion: 极化阶层级为累积量展开提供了替代方案，且不同于后者能在最高阶严格回到主方程。Dicke马尔可夫链以直观的集体态跳跃图像捕捉纳米激光器阈值下聚束的主要部分，而一阶极化修正即可达到近乎精确的统计复现；结合对称性约化主方程，可覆盖从数十到数千发射体的宽广参数范围。

Abstract: In nanolasers, a few to a few hundred quantum emitters share a single mode of light. Coupled identically to the mode, the emitters do not radiate independently but form collective bright and dark states, producing superradiant bursts and strongly bunched photon statistics below the laser threshold. We investigate to what extent this behavior can be understood as random jumps between collective Dicke states. From the laser master equation, we derive a hierarchy of models defined by the polarization order, i.e., the number of photons involved in the coherence between the emitters and the light, and include the coherence to various orders. With all of the coherences eliminated, we obtain a Dicke Markov chain, a stochastic jump process over the photon number and the Dicke states, in which collective effects enter only through enhanced or suppressed transition rates. It predicts superthermal bunching and a strong even--odd dependence on the emitter number. It recovers about 80\% of the bunching in the most coherent regime, where conventional rate equations predict no superthermal bunching. Keeping the first polarization order, i.e., the optical polarization, and eliminating the second order, i.e., the two-photon coherences, yields a population-polarization model, which recovers the bunching of the full master equation almost perfectly. This hierarchy of polarization orders is an alternative to the cumulant expansion and, unlike it, recovers the master equation at the highest order. As a numerically exact reference, a symmetry-reduced master equation extends the simulation range all the way to 34 (24) emitters below (above) threshold. The Dicke Markov chain can be solved directly for hundreds of emitters and sampled for thousands of emitters, further extending the range of numerical methods. We compare the numerical performance of the methods in detail to guide the choice of technique.

</details>


### [118] [Implementation of a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD under finite-size parameter estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17250)
*Christiano M. S. Nascimento,Maron F. Anka,Gabriel A. V. Menezes,Davi J. G. de Sousa,Nelson A. Neto,Artur A. Matoso,Gustavo C. Amaral,Valéria L. Silva,Guilherme P. Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验演示了基于独立激光器与本地本振（LLO）架构的单维（1D）高斯调制CV-QKD系统，在12 km标准单模光纤上实现渐近安全下1.421 Mbit/s、有限码长下0.364 Mbit/s的隐私放大密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯调制CV-QKD需在两个光场正交分量上编码，对态制备、调制和标定要求高，且易引入制备缺陷。1D高斯调制仅在一个正交分量编码，可简化发射机，但缺乏集独立激光相干接收、实用协商、有限码长参数估计与隐私放大于一体的完整实验验证。

Method: 搭建12 km标准单模光纤上的1D高斯调制CV-QKD系统，采用独立激光器LLO架构与集成化离线经典后处理链，涵盖相干探测、数字信号处理、信息协商、参数估计、基于物理性约束的安全性分析及隐私放大。

Result: 渐近安全处理下隐私放大密钥率为1.421 Mbit/s；有限码长情形下为0.364 Mbit/s。

Conclusion: 结果为单维高斯调制的性能—复杂度权衡提供了实验基准，并为开发低复杂度CV-QKD发射机奠定实验基础。

Abstract: Continuous-variable quantum key distribution (CV-QKD) is a promising technology for quantum-secure communications, offering high secret-key rates and straightforward integration with coherent optical communication systems. Conventional Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD encodes information in both optical-field quadratures, increasing requirements for state preparation, modulation, and calibration, while potentially introducing additional preparation imperfections. Unidimensional (1D) Gaussian modulation offers a simpler alternative by encoding information in only one quadrature, while the conjugate quadrature constrains the protocol's security. However, an integrated experimental 1D implementation combining independent-laser coherent reception with practical reconciliation, finite-size parameter estimation, and privacy amplification remains challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a 1D Gaussian-modulated CV-QKD system over 12~km of standard single-mode fiber using a local local oscillator (LLO) architecture with independent lasers and an integrated offline classical post-processing chain. The implementation includes coherent detection, digital signal processing, information reconciliation, parameter estimation, physicality-constrained security analysis, and privacy amplification. The 1D system achieves a privacy-amplified rate of $1.421$~Mbit/s under the asymptotic security treatment and $0.364$~Mbit/s under the finite-size scenario. These results establish an experimental baseline for the performance--complexity trade-off associated with unidimensional Gaussian modulation and provide an experimental basis for developing lower-complexity CV-QKD transmitters.

</details>


### [119] [Quasi-Cyclic Qudit Lifted Product Codes with Almost Linear Minimum Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15952)
*Shantom K. Borah,Abhinav Vaishya,V. Lalitha,Asit Kumar Pradhan,Bane Vasic,Narayanan Rengaswamy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 文章提出循环群代数上lifted product构造的qudit推广，可在任意素数幂维数下生成准循环qudit CSS码；利用加性码界定了编码率，并证明全叠加情形下码族具 Θ(log N) 逻辑qudit与近线性最小距离 Θ(N/log N)。此外研究了加性译码器与线性合并伴随式译码器，蒙特卡洛仿真显示在等符号错误概率的去极化信道下qudit码的帧错误率低于对应qubit码，且线性译码器对全叠加码带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码是低开销容错量子计算的有前景路径，而qudit相比qubit具有信息表示更高效、魔态蒸馏更优、代数结构更丰富等优势。渐近优的QLDPC码依赖非阿贝尔lift的LP构造，但实践中循环lift因产生的准循环码具循环结构、便于硬件译码器（尤其检查节点运算昂贵的非二进制译码器）利用而更具吸引力。因此需要将LP构造推广到qudit并保持循环结构。

Method: 在循环群代数上构造LP的qudit推广，扩展超图积与LP码至任意素数幂维数，并对任意叠加阶数（stacking order）生成准循环qudit CSS码；通过与加性码（additive codes）的联系界定编码率；借助适用于一般LP因子的距离界，分析由expander图上lifted Tanner码构成的全叠加qudit LP码族；研究一般qudit码的加性译码器与全叠加码的线性合并伴随式译码器；在等符号错误概率的去极化信道下用蒙特卡洛仿真比较qudit码与qubit码的帧错误率。

Result: 给出任意素数幂维数的准循环qudit CSS码构造；证明全叠加情形下由expander图lifted Tanner码构造的qudit LP码族具有 Θ(log N) 个逻辑qudit和几乎线性的最小距离 Θ(N/log N)；仿真显示在等符号错误概率去极化信道下qudit码的帧错误率低于可比qubit码，且线性合并伴随式译码器在全叠加码上带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 循环群代数上的qudit LP构造在保持准循环结构（利于硬件译码）的同时，实现了渐近良好的参数（对数逻辑qudit、近线性距离），并实证上优于对应qubit码；线性合并伴随式译码器可进一步提升全叠加码的性能，为低开销容错量子计算提供了实用可行的非二进制QLDPC方案。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are a promising route to fault-tolerant quantum computation with low overhead, and qudits offer advantages over qubits including more efficient information representation, improved magic state distillation, and richer algebraic structure for code design. Asymptotically good QLDPC codes arise from the lifted product (LP) construction with non-abelian lifts, but cyclic lifts remain attractive in practice because the resulting quasi-cyclic codes have circulant structure that hardware decoders exploit, which matters especially for non-binary decoders with costly check node operations. We introduce a qudit generalization of the LP construction over cyclic group algebras, which extends hypergraph product and LP codes to arbitrary prime-power dimensions and yields quasi-cyclic qudit CSS codes for every stacking order. We bound encoding rates via a connection to additive codes, and show that in the fully stacked case, qudit LP codes built from lifted Tanner codes on expander graphs form families with $Θ(\log N)$ logical qudits and almost linear minimum distance $Θ(N/\log N)$, via a distance bound that holds for a general class of LP factors. We also study an additive decoder for general qudit codes and a linear merged-syndrome decoder for fully stacked codes. Monte Carlo simulations under a depolarizing channel with equal symbol error probability show that qudit codes achieve lower frame error rates than comparable qubit codes, and that the linear decoder gives further gains for fully stacked codes.

</details>


### [120] [A deployed four-city entanglement-based QKD network with real-time key delivery, benchmarked against finite-key security bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17265)
*Gilberto Borges,Saeid Salari,Ekta Panwar,Samgeeth Puliyil,Kartik Kakade,Peter Rapčan,Mario Ziman,Djeylan Aktas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 报道斯洛伐克国家量子通信基础设施中基于纠缠的干线：在现网光纤上以中心节点波长路由构建无可信中继、全互联三城子网并四城同时运行；全部密钥率经完整后处理与ETSI GS QKD 014实时交付，109 km 达13.5 bit/s，无直连光纤城市间（32.1 dB累积损耗）达4.3 bit/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数已有QKD演示仅由原始符合计数（raw coincidences）估算密钥率，未反映完整后处理链的真实性能；同时希望摆脱可信中继、实现多节点同时运行的可扩展网络拓扑，并提供面向现网部署的可工程化组网设计方法。

Method: 在斯洛伐克西部四城现网电信光纤上部署偏振纠缠源（中心节点产生），按波长被动路由形成三城无可信中继全互联子网，第四城经中心节点接入，所有链路同时运行；提出用于被动波长路由的矩阵形式化方法，可算法化构造唯一连通与全连通拓扑；采用部分非局域色散补偿；密钥经完整后处理链生成并通过ETSI GS QKD 014接口实时交付；与有限密钥安全界基准对比，并评估以纠错替代"探测-丢弃"策略的影响。

Result: 109 km链路获得13.5 bit/s；无直连光纤的两城间在32.1 dB累积损耗下获得4.3 bit/s。基准分析表明：所实现的启发式隐私放大可超过有限密钥界；将探测-丢弃替换为纠错可使有限密钥率提升4.1–6.6倍，并在142.9 km链路上获得原先为零的正有限密钥率。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了基于纠缠、无可信中继的多城QKD现网部署可行性，并强调报告经完整后处理的端到端密钥率是评估实际系统性能的必要标准；矩阵化路由设计方法与纠错后处理改进为后续城际量子网络的可扩展性与成码率提升提供了明确路径。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) generates shared secret keys whose security rests on the laws of physics rather than on computational assumptions. We report the entanglement-based segment of the Slovak national quantum communication infrastructure, connecting four cities in western Slovakia over deployed telecommunication fiber. Polarization-entangled photons generated at a central node are passively routed by wavelength to form a trusted-node-free, fully meshed sub-network of three cities, with the fourth connected through the central node and all links operated simultaneously. We also introduce a matrix formalism for passive wavelength routing that enables the algorithmic construction of uniquely and fully connected topologies. Unlike most previous demonstrations, which estimate key rates from raw coincidences, all rates reported here are obtained after the complete post-processing chain, with keys delivered in real time via ETSI GS QKD 014. With partial non-local chromatic dispersion compensation, we obtain 13.5 bit/s over 109 km, and 4.3 bit/s over 32.1 dB of accumulated loss between two cities without a direct fiber link. Benchmarking against finite-key security bounds shows that the implemented heuristic privacy amplification can exceed the finite-key bound, and that replacing detect-and-discard with error correction would increase the finite-key rate by a factor of 4.1-6.6, and yield a positive finite-key rate on the 142.9 km link where the implemented scheme yields none.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum geometric bounds and uncertainty relations for velocity variance and spread of Wannier function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15977)
*Felipe P. Abreu,Luis F. Cárdenas-Castillo,David Porlles,Wei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用量子Cramér-Rao界的推导与Efimov-Dodonov论证，导出混合态协方差矩阵对角积、迹及每个对角元的多组不确定关系；取观测量为对称对数导数后，得到由平均Uhlmann曲率给出的量子Fisher信息矩阵的几何界（纯态下退化为Berry曲率对量子度规的界），并揭示速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学和规则及其受Berry曲率与速度算符对易子约束的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定关系多针对纯态且局限于少数可观测量，缺少对多观测量协方差矩阵结构（对角积、迹、逐元素）的统一约束；同时量子Fisher信息矩阵与几何量（Uhlmann曲率、Berry曲率）之间缺少系统的界，物理上对速度方差、Wannier函数展宽等量的几何起源也缺乏统一解释。

Method: 基于量子Cramér-Rao界的推导，结合Efimov与Dodonov的论证，构造适用于混合态、多可观测量的不确定关系，覆盖协方差矩阵的对角乘积、迹与各对角元；进一步将可观测量选取为对称对数导数（SLD），把不确定关系转化为平均Uhlmann曲率对量子Fisher信息矩阵的几何界；在纯态极限下得到Berry曲率对量子度规的几何界；并将形式体系应用于速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽，导出对应的光学和规则。

Result: 得到混合态多可观测量协方差矩阵的对角积、迹与逐元素不确定关系；由平均Uhlmann曲率给出量子Fisher信息矩阵的多类几何界，纯态下化为Berry曲率对量子度规的界；导出速度方差与价带Wannier函数展宽的光学和规则，并证明其分别受Berry曲率及速度算符对易子约束；在自旋正则系综、拓扑材料、常见半导体与过渡金属二硫族化物等体系中数值验证了这些几何界与不确定关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混合态不确定关系与量子几何量之间的统一桥梁：协方差矩阵型不确定关系经SLD选择自然给出Fisher信息矩阵的Uhlmann曲率几何界，并在纯态下衔接Berry曲率—量子度规图像；所得光学和规则为速度方差与Wannier展宽提供了几何上界，且在多类典型材料体系中得到验证，表明该框架具有普适性。

Abstract: By utilizing the derivation of quantum Cramér-Rao bound and an argument put forward by Efimov and Dodonov, we derive several uncertainty relations for mixed states that constrain the diagonal product, trace, and each diagonal element of the covariance matrix of multiple observables. By choosing the observables to be the symmetric logarithmic derivative, the uncertainty relations imply various geometric bounds on quantum Fisher information matrix caused by mean Uhlmann curvature, which in pure states become geometric bounds on quantum metric caused by Berry curvature. Our formalism reveals optical sum rules for velocity variance and spread of valence band Wannier functions, and how they are bounded by the Berry curvature and commutator of velocity operators. Canonical ensembles of spins, topological materials, common semiconductors, and transition metal dichalcogenides are used to verify these geometric bounds and uncertainty relations.

</details>


### [122] [Communication inequalities: robust quantum advantage, measurement testing and dimension witnessing with minimal setups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.15995)
*Albert Rico,Javier Fernández,Mani Zartab,John Calsamiglia,Some Sankar Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对通信场景（CS）这一量子行为最简单的观测设置，推导了仅依赖输入-输出统计中四个参数（对应关联与反关联事件）的维度不等式，首次给出区分经典与量子统计的一般方法，并据此获得SIC-POVM认证与维度见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信场景可视为观察量子行为的最简设置，但与Bell场景或含共享随机的制备-测量场景不同，目前尚缺乏区分经典统计与量子统计的通用方法，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 推导仅依赖于输入-输出统计中四个参数的维度不等式，这些参数对应关联事件与反关联事件；在给定维度假设下，通过饱和不等式进行SIC-POVM认证，并分析其解析噪声鲁棒性；同时利用不等式构造维度见证。

Result: 在维度假设下，饱和其中一个不等式可认证SIC-POVM，并给出解析的噪声鲁棒性；所导出的不等式同时对关联与反关联统计给出维度见证，且无需测量不相容性。

Conclusion: 该工作以简洁的维度不等式填补了通信场景中经典—量子统计分离方法的空白，为SIC-POVM认证和维度见证提供了统一且鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: Communication scenarios (CS) are arguably the simplest setting in which quantum behaviors can be observed. Yet, in contrast to Bell scenarios or prepare-measure scenarios with shared randomness, no general method is known to separate classical and quantum statistics. Here we close this gap by deriving dimensional inequalities to separate classical and quantum statistics in CS. These depend only on four parameters of the input-output statistics, corresponding to correlated and anticorrelated events. Under dimensional assumptions, saturating one of the inequalities certifies SIC-POVMs with analytical noise robustness.
