The inequalities also yield a dimension witness for both correlated and anti-correlated statistics, without the need of measurement incompatibility.

</details>


### [123] [Quantum Material Generation via Coherent Hamiltonian Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16016)
*Jiang Yuhan,Matthew Otten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子材料生成算法 QMage，在单一相干量子线路中借助哈密顿量引导，对成分、晶胞与原子位置构成的晶体构型空间做相干搜索，输出满足目标性质容差的材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动生成模型只能学习已知材料分布、外推能力差，且生成结构未必稳定；材料性质由哈密顿量决定，而量子计算机可由哈密顿量直接计算性质，因此需要一种从第一性原理出发、以目标性质为导向的生成式搜索方法。

Method: 将每个晶格构型的哈密顿量块编码，隔离目标本征态并评估其性质，用定点搜索（fixed-point search）放大满足容差的构型并测量；整个过程为一个相干量子线路，统一框架覆盖形成能、带隙、弹性、导电性等多类目标，并按容错量子计算时代以逻辑量子比特数与 Toffoli 深度估算代价。

Result: 给出从经典常规基准到经典方法不可靠目标的逻辑量子比特与 Toffoli 深度成本估计，表明该框架可统一处理多种性质目标。

Conclusion: QMage 从第一性原理生成候选材料而非由已知材料泛化，有望在目标性质难以经典预测的情形下实现材料发现。

Abstract: Discovering new materials with targeted properties is a central goal of materials science, yet the space of candidates is vast. Data-driven generative models learn the distribution of known materials, but they extrapolate poorly, and a generated structure is not known to be stable. A material's properties follow from its Hamiltonian, and a quantum computer can calculate them from it. We introduce the quantum material generative algorithm (QMage), a coherent search, guided by the Hamiltonian, over crystalline configurations of atomic composition, unit cell structure, and atomic positions, for one whose calculated properties meet the targets to within a given error tolerance. The computation is a single coherent quantum circuit in which each configuration's Hamiltonian is block-encoded, its target eigenstate isolated and properties evaluated, and the satisfying configurations amplified by fixed-point search and measured. One framework covers many targets, from formation energy and band gaps to elasticity and conductivity. We design the algorithm for the mature fault-tolerant era and estimate its cost in logical qubits and Toffoli depth from a classically routine benchmark to targets where established methods are unreliable. The approach generates candidate materials from first principles, rather than generalizing from those known, and could enable materials discovery when the target properties are difficult to predict classically.

</details>


### [124] [Operator uncertainty and cross-domain information in quantum signals: Second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16049)
*Mostafizur Rahaman Laskar,Sajad Daei,Gabor Fodor,Mikael Skoglund,Franco Nori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 结合熵不确定关系与连续变量协方差描述，导出到达时间与横向波数间互信息的上界（两倍较小不确定性乘积的对数），给出高斯波包饱和条件并讨论噪声下抖动限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时空耦合量子波形设计建立可量化的信息容量上界，判定在规范模型内达到目标信息量的必要充分条件，并指导实际光学实现。

Method: 将熵不确定关系与连续变量高斯协方差描述相结合，对有限二阶矩与有限熵的态推导到达时间与横向波数互信息的二阶矩上界；构造带双线性时空相位的高斯波包检验饱和性；分析局域高斯测量最优性、Rényi-2熵与量子Fisher信息；引入加性探测噪声与时间抖动模型。

Result: 互信息被log[2·min(ΔTΔΩ, ΔXΔK)]上界；双线性相位高斯波包对所有容许标准差预算均饱和该界，成为可达目标信息的充要条件；该态下联合测量在局域高斯测量中最优，给出约化态Rényi-2熵并达固定输入宽度下耦合的量子Fisher信息；加性噪声下抖动将信息限制为抖动与脉宽之比所定水平。

Conclusion: 结果为时空耦合量子信号设计提供统一框架，并提出基于脉冲前沿倾斜与透镜的光学实现方案。

Abstract: We derive second-moment bounds for quantum waveform design by combining entropic uncertainty relations with the covariance description of continuous-variable systems. For any state with finite second moments and entropies, the mutual information between the arrival time and the transverse wave number, which are compatible observables from different canonical pairs, is bounded above by the logarithm of twice the smaller of the time-frequency and position-wave-number uncertainty products. We demonstrate that a Gaussian wave packet with a bilinear space-time phase saturates this bound for every admissible set of standard-deviation budgets, and that the bound therefore provides a necessary and sufficient condition for reaching a target information within the canonical model. For this state, the joint measurement of the arrival time and the transverse wave number is optimal among local Gaussian measurements, yields the Rényi-2 entropy of the reduced state, and attains the quantum Fisher information for the coupling at fixed input widths. We also show that, with additive detector noise, timing jitter limits the recorded information to a level set by the ratio of jitter to pulse duration. These results provide a framework for designing spatiotemporally coupled quantum signals, and we propose an optical realization based on pulse-front tilt and a lens.

</details>


### [125] [TGen-SQD: Physics-Informed Transferable Configuration Generator for Sample-Based Quantum Diagonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16080)
*Niloy Deb Roy Mishu,Lei Jiang,Mohsen Heidari,Ruizhe Zhang,Fan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: TGen-SQD提出物理信息驱动的生成器，跨多个哈密顿量联合训练，权重冻结后可直接用于已见与零样本几何构型，降低SQD的确定子空间选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQD避免迭代变分电路优化，但其精度与成本依赖于选取能捕获基态的紧凑行列式子空间；现有机器学习选择器依赖任务特定物理线索且每个哈密顿量都需重训练。

Method: 提出TGen-SQD：物理信息生成器，在多个哈密顿量上联合训练；从CI加权数据中学习，利用轨道级与系统级描述符引导生成；训练后权重冻结用于推理。

Result: 在20量子比特H10链上，已见与未见几何构型的平均能量误差分别为40.18±1.52与43.97±1.20 mHa，在匹配采样与提议预算下较SOTA基线PIGen-SQD分别降低12.7%与11.7%。

Conclusion: 跨哈密顿量联合训练的单一冻结生成器可有效替代任务特定选择器，提升SQD精度并支持零样本几何泛化。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) avoids iterative variational circuit optimization, but its accuracy and cost depend on selecting a compact determinant subspace that captures the ground state. Existing machine-learning selectors use task-specific physical cues and typically retrain for each Hamiltonian. We introduce TGen-SQD, a physics-informed generator jointly trained across multiple Hamiltonians. It learns from configuration-interaction-weighted data and uses orbital- and system-level descriptors to guide generation. After training, its weights remain frozen for inference on seen and zero-shot geometries. On a 20-qubit H10 chain, TGen-SQD achieves mean energy errors of 40.18 +/- 1.52 and 43.97 +/- 1.20 mHa on seen and unseen geometries, respectively, 12.7% and 11.7% lower than the state-of-the-art PIGen-SQD baseline under matched shot and proposal budgets.

</details>


### [126] [Testing Quantum Advantage in High-Frequency Trading with Market-Derived Nonlocal Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16113)
*Daiwei Zhu,Brendan Pankovich,Alireza Marefat Khah,Haakam Aujla,Ramis Movassagh,Martin Roetteler,Johannes Borregaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"市场衍生非局域博弈"，用真实订单簿数据与联合动作回测确定博弈的输入与支付，而非预设理想化模型；在两代理三股票跨场所套利场景中，实测数据下未观察到显著的量子-经典差距，但可控调整流动性竞争与跨股票再定价后，可识别支持量子优势的机制并完成端到端回测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多停留在理论层面，用玩具市场模型指定博弈与支付结构，未检验模型对真实市场行为的反映程度，因而量子纠缠在通信受限的分布式协调（如碎片化市场高频交易）中的实际价值缺乏实证支撑。

Method: 构建市场衍生非局域博弈：以历史订单簿数据及联合动作回测定义博弈输入与支付；交易策略本身基于市场因素（经典），量子关联仅用于协调代理决策；计算精确经典最优值与量子上界；对存在量子优势可能的博弈，提出优化算法搜索显式纠缠策略并进行端到端历史数据回测；在双代理三股票跨场所套利设定中演示，并通过可控改变流动性竞争与跨股票再定价进行机制分析。

Result: 由观测市场数据导出的博弈在所测交互尺度上不存在已分辨的量子-经典差距；通过流动性竞争与跨股票再定价的可控变化，方法能识别支持量子优势的机制区间、构造显式纠缠策略，并检验其优势在修改后的历史数据端到端回测中是否依旧存在。

Conclusion: 以市场数据内生定义博弈为量子协调策略提供了可回测、可证伪的评估框架；真实市场条件下量子优势并不自动显现，其存在依赖于特定微观结构机制，需通过端到端回测而非理论模型来判定。

Abstract: Non-classical correlations enabled by entanglement could be exploited for distributed coordination tasks with communication constraints such as high-frequency trading in fragmented markets. However, previous studies have largely specified the game and its payoff structure theoretically, using toy market models without testing how well these models reflect observed market behavior. Here, we introduce market-derived nonlocal games, in which historical order-book data and backtests of joint actions determine the game's inputs and payoffs, rather than a prespecified idealized model. We start from classical trading strategies based on market considerations, using quantum correlations only to coordinate the agents' decisions. We compute exact classical optima and quantum upper bounds for the payoff. For games with a possible quantum advantage, we introduce an optimization algorithm to find explicit entangled strategies and end-to-end backtest them on historical data. We demonstrate the approach in a two-agent, three-stock cross-venue arbitrage setting. The game derived from the observed market data shows no resolved quantum-classical gap at the measured interaction scales. Controlled variations in liquidity competition and cross-stock repricing then show how the approach identifies regimes supporting quantum advantage, constructs explicit strategies, and tests whether their advantage survives end-to-end backtesting on modified historical market data.

</details>


### [127] [Phase Interference and Transverse Catalysts Govern the Noise Fragility of Long-Range Variational Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16115)
*Longhui Shen,Fangzhou Jin,Xiaoxia Yi,Bin Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: A variational circuit's single-fault fragility is set by how fault phases interfere on the target Hamiltonian (phase-matched longitudinal "halo" + transverse catalysts), not by how far faults spread; suppressing both catalysts eliminates the ignited plateau, minimizes fragility, and halves dephasing error, with fragility × gate-count exposure predicting noise-induced energy shifts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-range entangling gates speed up variational state preparation but accelerate operator spreading, prompting the assumption that nonlocality implies noise fragility. The work tests whether single-fault fragility is truly governed by fault spread distance or by a different mechanism tied to fault-phase interference on the target Hamiltonian.

Method: Definition and measurement of single-fault fragility F; analytical proofs on Heisenberg chains comparing faults with identical extensive support; identification of a phase-matched longitudinal halo and the transverse catalysts (spatially nonuniform single-qubit rotations, pair-flip/XX-YY components) that ignite it, versus uniform rotations and magnetization-conserving exchange gates; noisy training with full-width long-range ZZ gates under catalyst pruning and annealing protocols; linking to gate-count exposure λ = pK.

Result: Two faults of identical extensive support in Heisenberg chains can cause bounded or extensive energy damage with exact ratio N/2. The longitudinal halo stays energetically dark until ignited by transverse symmetry-breaking catalysts. In noisy training, suppressing either catalyst alone leaves circuits trapped on an ignited plateau sustained by remote XY couplings; suppressing both removes the plateau, gives minimal fragility despite larger fault operators, and halves dephasing error. Annealing out transverse catalysts rescues all trapped trajectories, whereas annealing the whole longitudinal sector does not.

Conclusion: Fragility is controlled by fault-phase interference rather than fault spreading. Training proceeds via a race between catalyst pruning and gradient force balance. With λ = pK, a single fragility measurement predicts noise-induced energy shift across architectures and noise strengths, recasting ansatz design as a trade-off between coherent expressivity and gate count.

Abstract: Long-range entangling gates shorten variational state preparation, but they also accelerate operator spreading across the register, linking nonlocality to a presumed noise fragility. Here we show that the single-fault fragility $F$ of a variational circuit is governed by how fault phases interfere on the target Hamiltonian, not by how far faults spread. For Heisenberg chains we prove that two faults with identical extensive support can incur either bounded or extensive energy damage, in the exact ratio $N/2$. A phase-matched longitudinal halo remains energetically dark until ignited by transverse catalysts that break a symmetry of the remote register, namely spatially nonuniform single-qubit rotations or the pair-flip component of two-qubit $XX/YY$ gates, whereas uniform rotations and magnetization-conserving exchange gates leave it strictly dark. In noisy training with full-width long-range $ZZ$ gates, suppressing either catalyst alone leaves circuits trapped on an ignited plateau sustained by remote $XY$ couplings, whereas suppressing both eliminates the plateau, achieves minimal fragility despite larger fault operators, and halves the dephasing error. Training is driven by a race between catalyst pruning and gradient force balance, as annealing the transverse catalysts out of trapped circuits rescues every trajectory while annealing the entire longitudinal sector does not. Combined with the gate-count exposure $λ=pK$, a single fragility measurement accurately predicts the noise-induced energy shift across circuit architectures and noise strengths, reducing ansatz design to a trade-off between coherent expressivity and gate count.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16117)
*Matthias M. Müller,Tommaso Calarco,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该章节综述量子控制这一领域，从控制问题定义、误差来源与抑制方法，到绝热方法、量子最优控制和机器学习等前沿求解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统动力学脆弱且难以精确调控，而量子控制既是众多量子技术应用的基石，也能揭示量子系统中能量结构与时间动力学之间的基本联系，因此需要系统梳理该领域的问题与求解方法。

Method: 采用综述性组织方式：先引入量子控制问题，再依次讨论基本误差来源及其抑制方法，最后介绍解决控制问题的先进技术，包括绝热方法、量子最优控制与机器学习方法。

Result: 本文为章节性综述，未报告具体实验或数值结果，主要贡献在于给出量子控制问题的框架性梳理及误差抑制与求解技术的分类与介绍。

Conclusion: 量子控制是量子技术的核心基础，其对能量—时间动力学联系的揭示具有基础物理意义；结合绝热、最优控制与机器学习等方法的综合手段是当前解决控制问题的主要途径。

Abstract: Quantum Control is the art of steering the fragile dynamics of a quantum system in a precise way toward a desired final state or transformation. It is a cornerstone of many quantum technology applications and at the same time allows us to shed light on the fundamental connection between the energy structure and the time dynamics of quantum systems at the heart of some of the most interesting phenomena of quantum mechanics. In this chapter on quantum control, we introduce the control problem, go through basic error sources and methods to suppress these errors and then discuss state-of-the-art techniques to solve the control problem, like adiabatic methods, quantum optimal control and methods of machine learning.

</details>


### [129] [Infinite or indefinite: the query cost of simulating the quantum switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16229)
*Dantong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子开关相对普通量子电路模型在查询复杂度上的优势：证明对一般量子信道无法用有限次调用精确模拟量子开关，近似模拟需Ω(d^{1/4})次调用，并将其应用于噪声鉴别任务获得可证明的查询优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子开关是不定因果序的典型例子，无法用任何"每个操作仅按确定顺序使用一次"的量子电路描述，光子实验已实现其物理实现。作者希望量化：在电路模型下，用量子计算机重现量子开关需要多少次对黑箱操作的调用，并寻找该优势在实际相关任务（如噪声刻画）中的体现。

Method: 设定黑箱为d维系统上的一般量子信道，允许电路使用经典前馈与无限辅助量子存储。基于半钻石范数度量误差，证明三类查询下界：(1)最坏情况下不存在有限调用次数可精确模拟；(2)任意固定误差小于1/2的近似需Ω(d^{1/4})次调用；(3)普通电路提取一个比特可能需指数多次调用，而开关几次即可。并构造噪声鉴别任务——实际噪声为固定未知基下的退相位、理论模型为去极化信道，二者纠缠保真度与平均门保真度相同——比较两者区分所需调用次数。

Result: 若其中一个黑箱已知为酉操作，三次调用即足够。对一般信道：无法用有限次调用精确模拟；误差低于1/2的近似需Ω(d^{1/4})次调用（随量子比特数指数增长）；存在比特提取任务需指数多次调用。噪声鉴别中，固定噪声强度下量子开关以与d无关的设备使用次数区分两种信道，而电路模型需Ω(d^{1/16})次调用。

Conclusion: 量子开关在查询复杂度上相对电路模型具有可证明的指数级优势，且该优势在量子纠错所需的噪声刻画任务中具有实际相关性，作者呼吁未来将此类优势推广到更实用的计算任务。

Abstract: The quantum switch applies two operations in a coherent superposition of their causal orders and is a simple example of indefinite causal order. No quantum circuit that uses each operation once in a definite order can even describe it, yet photonic experiments have demonstrated its physical realization. We ask how many calls to the black-box operations a quantum computer in the circuit model needs to reproduce the switch. If one of the black boxes is known to be unitary, three calls suffice. For general channels on a $d$-dimensional system, we prove three results. In all three, circuits may use classical feed-forward and unlimited ancillary quantum memory. First, no finite number of calls suffices for exact simulation in the worst case. Second, approximation to any fixed error below $1/2$, measured in half the diamond norm, requires $Ω(d^{1/4})$ calls, exponential in the number of qubits. Third, an ordinary circuit can require exponentially many calls to obtain a single bit that the switch extracts with a few uses. We consider a device whose actual noise is dephasing in a fixed unknown basis and test it against a theoretical depolarizing model. The two channels have the same entanglement and average gate fidelities, so these figures alone do not distinguish them. At fixed noise strength, the quantum switch tells them apart with a number of device uses independent of $d$, whereas the circuit model requires $Ω(d^{1/16})$ calls. Noise characterization is needed for quantum error correction, and this gives a provable query advantage of the switch in a task relevant to practical quantum computing. We invite future work that extends such advantages to more practical computing tasks.

</details>


### [130] [Hierarchical Decoding for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16271)
*Xulong Tang,Narges Alavisamani,Joan Camps,Matthew Cha,Elise Jennings,Jack O'Connor,Humberto Munoz Bauza,Josh Izaac,Namit Anand,Ron Bewtra,Jeremiah Coleman,Johannes Heinsoo,Michael Ferguson,Ben Lau,P. Aaron Lott,Leonhard Neuhaus,Lee J. O'Riordan,Muhammad Osama,Masoud Mohseni,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文是一篇关于容错量子计算中经典解码器的综述，用统一分类梳理解码算法、执行策略与码构造，区分"解码器级层次"与"码级层次"，并比较各类解码硬件平台的性能与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要经典解码器跟上综合征数据流，并向量子控制系统提供纠错信息与逻辑测量结果；规模化应用需高吞吐量，同时控制从读出到可用纠错的端到端延迟，而现有文献术语和评测口径分散，缺乏共享的设计基础。

Method: 采用综述与分类框架：按共性分类组织解码算法、执行策略和码构造；区分解码器级层次（多解码阶段或资源协作解单一码）与码级层次（码构造本身引入多解码层级）；先综述单码分层解码器及其级联预解码与窗口化、流式、分布式实现，再研究分层码与级联码在各码层间通过硬判决、软可靠性信息或综合征信息协调解码的方式；同时分析比特模态对解码负载与时序需求的影响，并评述CPU、GPU、FPGA、ASIC及低温平台的适配性。

Result: 系统归纳了各解码方案在已报道评测条件下的性能与权衡，阐明单码分层解码与多级码解码的协调机制差异，梳理不同硬件平台满足吞吐与延迟需求的途径，并厘清文献间的关联。

Conclusion: 该综述为量子纠错设计探索提供了共享基础，并指出性能报告规范与系统集成方面的空白，以引导未来研究走向实用化量子纠错。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires classical decoders that can keep up with syndrome data streams and supply correction information and logical measurement outcomes to the quantum control system. Meeting these requirements at scale requires high throughput while controlling the end-to-end latency from readout to usable correction. In this paper, we organize decoding algorithms, execution strategies, and code constructions using common classifications and examine their performance and trade-offs under the reported evaluation conditions. We distinguish decoder-level hierarchy, where multiple decoding stages or resources cooperate to decode a single code, from code-level hierarchy, where the code construction itself introduces multiple decoding levels. We first review single-code hierarchical decoders and their cascaded predecoding and windowed, streaming, and distributed implementations. We then study hierarchical and concatenated codes, focusing on how decoding is coordinated across code levels through hard decisions, soft reliability information, or syndrome information. We also examine how qubit modalities shape decoding workloads and timing requirements, and review how different decoding platforms meet these requirements, including central processing units (CPUs), graphics processing units (GPUs), field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), application-specific integrated circuits (ASICs), and cryogenic platforms. By assessing design trade-offs, this paper clarifies connections across the literature and provides a shared foundation for QEC design exploration. It also identifies gaps in performance reporting and system integration to guide future research toward practical quantum error correction.

</details>


### [131] [Scaling Pêlagos: Multi-GPU Orchestration for Relay Belief-Propagation Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16285)
*Xulong Tang,Dionysios Diamantopoulos,Tristan Müller,Apo Kayi,Nelson Mimura Gonzalez,Muhammad Osama,Seetharami Seelam,Haralampos Pozidis,Blake R. Johnson,Yasuko Eckert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将 Pêlagos 中继置信传播（Relay-BP）解码器扩展为多 GPU 进程隔离编排、并行主机预处理与共享内存输出方案，在单/双 gross 表面码电路上于 8 块 AMD MI300X/MI355X 上实现 5.68×–7.01× 端到端加速，GPU 解码吞吐接近线性扩展，并部署于 OLCF 的 Lux 超算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要满足实时约束的低逻辑错误率 QEC 解码器，而启发式解码算法必须依靠大规模仿真评估性能与调参。多 GPU 是扩展仿真的自然途径，但朴素地按综合征批分发生成主机端预处理与结果收集开销，限制了扩展性。

Method: 在 Pêlagos Relay-BP 解码器基础上引入三项工程改造：进程隔离的多 GPU 编排、并行主机端预处理、以及基于共享内存的输出收集；在 8 块 AMD Instinct MI300X 与 8 块 MI355X 的多 GPU 环境中，对单 gross（[[144,12,12]]）与双 gross（[[288,12,18]]）码电路（R=50）进行端到端与纯 GPU 解码时间的吞吐测量。

Result: 单 gross 电路 R=50 下，MI300X 与 MI355X 分别取得 5.68× 和 6.23× 端到端加速；双 gross 电路分别为 6.92× 与 7.01×。若以 GPU 解码时间计吞吐，双 gross 加速达 7.58× 与 7.45×，显示接近线性的 GPU 扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过高效多 GPU 解码可实用地加速大规模 QEC 仿真；Pêlagos 已部署于 OLCF 基于 MI355X 的 Lux 超算，作为高通量 QEC 仿真的探路应用，为 AI+HPC 基础设施提供了具体量子计算用例。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires quantum error-correction (QEC) decoders that achieve low logical error rates under real-time operating constraints. These constraints motivate heuristic decoding algorithms whose error-correction performance must be evaluated through large-scale simulation. High-throughput decoding accelerates this evaluation and the exploration of decoder parameters. Using multiple graphics processing units (GPUs) is a natural way to scale these simulations, but naively distributing syndrome batches introduces host-side preprocessing and output-collection overheads that limit scaling. To address these limitations, we extend the Pêlagos relay belief-propagation (Relay-BP) decoder with process-isolated orchestration, parallel host preprocessing, and shared-memory output. We evaluate both one-gross ($[[144,12,12]]$) and two-gross ($[[288,12,18]]$) codes in a multi-GPU environment. Specifically, for the one-gross circuit at $R=50$, eight AMD Instinct MI300X GPUs and eight AMD Instinct MI355X GPUs achieve optimized end-to-end speedups of $5.68\times$ and $6.23\times$, respectively. For the two-gross circuit, the corresponding speedups are $6.92\times$ and $7.01\times$. When throughput is calculated using GPU decoding time, the corresponding two-gross speedups are $7.58\times$ and $7.45\times$, indicating near-linear GPU scaling. We deploy Pêlagos on Lux, Oak Ridge National Laboratory's AMD Instinct MI355X-based supercomputer, as a pathfinding application for high-throughput QEC simulation. This deployment provides a concrete quantum-computing use case for the AI + HPC infrastructure supporting the Genesis Mission's scientific-discovery goals. These end-to-end measurements demonstrate a practical path to accelerating large-scale QEC simulation through efficient multi-GPU decoding.

</details>


### [132] [Collective information backflow from a local population record: Identifiability and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16334)
*Yifan Shi,Pengju Chen,Duan Wang,Quanzhen Ding,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究复合量子系统中"信息回流"诊断与有限布居记录之间的不匹配，指出在已知浴族内布居记录足以预测回流诊断，但短时含噪记录无法实现一致精确推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息回流通常需通过成对演化态定义，而实验往往只能获得受限的布居记录，二者存在信息不匹配，需厘清布居记录在何种条件下足以支撑回流诊断。

Method: 考虑两个全同量子比特集体耦合到指数关联函数的真空浴；以理想亮态布居轨迹确定与有限时间迹距离回流诊断相关的浴参数组合，在固定时间网格与态对库上评估；利用支持向量学习或直接物理拟合，从稀疏布居记录预测诊断；并用双假设界分析有限测量统计造成的限制。

Result: 未分辨的失谐符号不改变该诊断；稀疏布居记录可经支持向量学习或物理拟合预测诊断；对某个六时刻、600次总射击的协议，在指定两浴之一处，任何基于该观测的估计器期望绝对误差至少约0.13。

Conclusion: 在已知浴族内布居可以是充分的，但短时含噪记录不允许一致精确的推断；因此信息回流诊断的可识别性依赖于浴族知识与测量统计。

Abstract: Information backflow in a composite quantum system is defined through pairs of evolving states, whereas an experiment may provide only a restricted population record. We study this mismatch for two identical qubits coupled collectively to a vacuum reservoir with an exponential correlation function. An ideal bright-state population trajectory determines the bath-parameter combinations relevant to a finite-time trace-distance backflow diagnostic of the full two-qubit channel, evaluated on a fixed time grid and state-pair bank. The unresolved detuning sign leaves this diagnostic unchanged. This relation allows sparse population records to predict the diagnostic using support-vector learning or direct physical fitting. Finite measurement statistics impose a different restriction: a two-hypothesis bound identifies reservoirs with similar short population records but substantially different backflow. For one six-time protocol with 600 total shots, every estimator using those observations has expected absolute error at least approximately 0.13 at one of two specified reservoirs. Thus a population can be sufficient within a known bath family without permitting uniformly accurate inference from a short noisy record.

</details>


### [133] [Training quantum neural networks one job at a time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16365)
*Muhammad Faryad,Rabab Alomairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将混合量子-经典训练视为执行效率问题：在固定作业数（jobs）与每作业采样数（shots）下，比较"每作业如何填充电路"对优化进展的影响，提出批式参数移位模板优化器，其在仿真与IBM硬件上均优于SPSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器通过云队列访问，每次往返（job）代价高，且一个job可携带多个电路。经典优化器因此不仅决定每次shot学到多少，还决定每个job学到多少。作者希望从"如何从每次昂贵交互中获得最大优化进展"的角度重审优化器设计，把优化器设计与开销摊销、加速器资源利用联系起来。

Method: 在量子神经网络分类器上，给定相同job数与相同每job shots，仅改变job内电路的填充方式。将QAOA中有效的批式模式搜索直接移植到多参数（几十个）模型会失效；因此保留其原理——在发送job前固定一批参数向量。对由Pauli旋转构成的电路，自然批次为parameter-shift模板（stencil）；并进一步提出结构感知的模板搜索方法（将同类行读作正弦曲线）。与SPSA等同时扰动法对比，分析梯度误差随每job shots的缩放行为。

Result: 批式parameter-shift的梯度误差随每job shots呈反比下降，而SPSA的误差存在不随shots增加而消除的下限。仿真中，对24–48个参数，批式parameter-shift训练所需job数为SPSA的1/5至1/2，且该排序在两种噪声水平下均成立；结构感知模板搜索同样优于SPSA。在IBM ibm_fez上，6与8量子比特的三组交错运行中，批式优化器在50个job的第4至第20个即达到SPSA的最终训练损失；8量子比特测试精度明显更高，6量子比特差异不显著。所有硬件数据可由原始shots复现。

Conclusion: 在云访问的量子处理器上，优化器的设计应以"每job的信息产出"为核心指标；预先固定参数向量批次的批式参数移位模板能有效摊销往返开销，误差随shots反比收敛，在仿真与硬件上均显著节省job数并提升分类精度，为混合量子-经典训练提供了执行效率视角。

Abstract: When a quantum processor is reached through a cloud queue, a variational algorithm pays for every round trip, and a single round trip can carry many circuits. The classical optimizer therefore decides how much is learned per job, not only per shot. We study this for a quantum neural network classifier. We compare optimizers that are given the same number of jobs and the same shots per job, and that differ only in how they fill each job. This comparison treats hybrid quantum-classical training as an execution-efficiency problem: how to obtain useful optimization progress from each costly interaction with a quantum processor. The resulting perspective connects optimizer design to overhead amortization and efficient use of accelerator resources. A direct port of a batched pattern search that worked for the quantum approximate optimization algorithm stalls once the model has a few dozen parameters. What does transfer is the principle behind it: fill each job with a batch of parameter vectors that is fixed before the job is sent. For circuits built from Pauli rotations the natural batch is the parameter-shift stencil. We show that its gradient error falls as the inverse of the shots per job, while the error of simultaneous-perturbation methods (SPSA) has a floor that no number of shots removes. In simulation, batched parameter-shift training needs between a fifth and a half of the jobs SPSA needs, for 24 to 48 parameters, and the ordering holds at two noise levels. A new structure-aware stencil search reads the same kind of rows as sinusoids and also beats SPSA. On IBM's ibm_fez processor, in three interleaved runs at six and eight qubits, the batched optimizer reached SPSA's final training loss within 4 to 20 of its 50 jobs. Its test accuracy was clearly higher at eight qubits; at six qubits the difference was not significant. All hardware numbers can be re-derived from the raw shots.

</details>


### [134] [Generation and conversion of vortex light in coherently prepared hydrogen atoms and hydrogen-like ions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16367)
*Fan Meng,Guo-Feng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Λ型三能级原子介质中，利用两低态间的方位角相关相位相干性，可在理想模型下实现拉盖尔-高斯型光束的涡旋产生与拓扑荷转换（l2=l1+m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索以空间原子相干（而非传统光学元件或非线性介质）作为控制涡旋光轨道角动量的新途径，实现高斯模到涡旋模的产生以及入射涡旋OAM模式的转换。

Method: 提出相干制备的三能级Λ型原子介质理论方案；两低态的相对相位随方位角变化，携带相干绕数m，使生成场产生附加相位缠绕；采用解析推导并结合氢原子跃迁参数的数值示例考察产生与转换两种过程；在共振条件下分析传播中的吸收行为及EIT效应。

Result: 理想模型给出l2=l1+m：高斯入射可产生涡旋，入射涡旋可转换为不同OAM模式；解析结果与数值示例均验证两过程。共振条件下，暗态成分占比提高、亮态成分被抑制，吸收显著减弱并出现EIT。空间相干作为初始条件被假定，其具体实验制备仍待解决。

Conclusion: 该方案为通过空间原子相干操控涡旋光提供了一个潜在理论框架，但空间相干的实验实现与完整传播/损耗定量评估仍属开放问题。

Abstract: We propose a theoretical scheme for generating and converting optical vortices in a coherently prepared three-level Λ-type atomic medium. An azimuthally varying relative phase between the two lower states imparts additional phase winding to the generated field, yielding l2=l1+m in the ideal model, where l1 is the input topological charge and m the coherence winding number. Thus, a Gaussian input can generate a vortex, while an incident vortex can be converted to a different orbital angular momentum (OAM) mode. Analytical results and numerical examples using hydrogen transition parameters illustrate both processes. Under resonant conditions, the model predicts strongly suppressed absorption during subsequent propagation as the bright-state component is attenuated and electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) emerges. Spatial coherence is assumed as an initial condition; its detailed experimental preparation remains open. This scheme offers a potential framework for controlling vortex light through spatial atomic coherence.

</details>


### [135] [No Convergence in Learned Eavesdropping-Detection Dynamics for BB84 Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16372)
*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将窃听者与检测器建模为交替最佳响应博弈，发现双方学习动态在20轮内不收敛，可剥削性围绕最佳响应方差波动而非趋于不动点；低噪声下窃听窗口始终开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作表明强化学习窃听者可在误码率低于中止阈值的情况下截获BB84量子密钥分发并保留可用密钥。本文考察一个同样具备自适应能力的“学习型检测器”能否关闭该隐蔽窗。

Method: 将交互形式化为表格式窃听者与阈值自适应表格式检测器之间的交替最佳响应博弈；采用Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning三种学习规则、三种信道噪声水平与统一虚警策略，度量“可剥削性”（新攻击者对最终检测器最佳响应时的检测率与可提取密钥比例），并设置两组冻结对手的对照实验。

Result: 20轮内动态不收敛，可剥削性不趋近不动点且轮间波动不衰减；两组对照显示各方波动与最佳响应过程自身方差相当。行为依噪声而异：低噪声下隐蔽窗保持开放；中噪声下检测器平均可压制攻击者但博弈仍未收敛；高噪声下抑制来自信道而非学习策略。中噪声呈现显著反持续性，低噪声结果与最佳响应噪声一致，提示“最后行动者信息不对称”机制。

Conclusion: 学习型检测器无法从单轮结果获得认证；关闭隐蔽窗需要打破信息结构而非在适应速度上胜过攻击者。

Abstract: In prior work we showed that a reinforcement-learning eavesdropper can intercept BB84 quantum key distribution while holding the quantum bit error rate below the abort threshold and keeping a usable distillable key. In this paper we ask whether an equally adaptive learning detector can close this stealth window. We cast the interaction as an alternating best-response game between a tabular eavesdropper and a tabular threshold-adapting detector. We measure exploitability, which is the detection rate and distillable key fraction achieved by a fresh attacker best-responding to the final detector. Across three learning rules (Q-Learning, SARSA, Double Q-Learning), three channel-noise levels, and a uniform false-alarm discipline, the dynamics do not settle out to twenty rounds. The exploitability does not approach a fixed point, and its round-to-round swings do not decay. Two controls, one retraining the attacker against a frozen defender and one retraining the defender against a frozen attacker, show that the swings on each side match the variance of the best-response procedure itself. The dynamics fluctuate at the scale of best-response variance rather than converging, with neither player settling to a fixed response. The behavior is noise-dependent, and the stealth window stays open at the low noise where practical QKD operates. The defender contains the attacker on average at intermediate noise without the contest settling, and at high noise the channel rather than the learned policy suppresses the attack. As a candidate mechanism we examine a last-mover information asymmetry, supported by significant anti-persistence at intermediate noise and consistent with best-response noise at low noise. The results suggest that a learned detector cannot be certified from any single round, and that closing the window calls for breaking the information structure rather than out-adapting the attacker.

</details>


### [136] [Loss of the quadratic speedup in Grover search under local and coherent noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16398)
*Imama Tul Birrah Khan,Muhammad Faryad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 解析地刻画退极化、独立局域噪声与相干旋转误差下Grover算法的噪声影响：噪声不减慢旋转，仅使相干权重随深度几何衰减；局域噪声等效为被寄存器尺寸放大的全局信道，因而每比特可容忍错误率随数据库规模下降得比 N^{-1/2} 更快一个寄存器尺寸的幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法仅需 O(√N) 次查询即可搜索非结构数据库，但噪声会侵蚀这一优势。作者希望解析地回答“噪声使优势衰减得多快”，并统一解释不同噪声模型下已有标度结果的来源，说明为何某些错误危害更大。

Method: (1) 全域退极化信道作用于每层门之后时与门对易，含噪态等于无噪态乘以随线路深度几何衰减的相干权重；(2) 对独立比特错误，证明理想Grover算符使被推出二维搜索平面的振幅冻结，从而精确给出单次错误的效应；(3) 对大数据库将噪声搜索约化为平面内分量的Bloch型方程，算法为旋转、噪声表现为泄漏与退相干；(4) 推导比特翻转、相位翻转、退极化与振幅阻尼噪声的速率并求解方程；(5) 对旋转角相干误差给出随深度线性展宽的成功概率带。

Result: 退极化下Grover旋转速度不变，仅权重衰减、最佳停止点前移，√N 查询标度仅在累积噪声较小时保持；单次错误中相位翻转在搜索早期危害最大、比特翻转在后期危害最大、组合错误在任何时刻均有害；得到四类噪声速率的闭式解；局域噪声领头阶等效为强度放大寄存器比特数的全局信道，可容忍单比特错误率随 N 下降快于 N^{-1/2}（额外一个寄存器尺寸的幂次）；相干角度误差下成功率带宽随深度线性增长。结果以闭式复现并统一了此前针对特定噪声模型的标度结论。

Conclusion: Grover算法的噪声鲁棒性可由“旋转不减速+权重几何衰减+平面外振幅冻结”的简单图像统一描述；局域噪声的等效放大效应与错误类型的时相依赖性共同决定了可扩展的错误阈值，为容错量子搜索的误差预算提供了闭式解析依据。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm searches an unstructured database with a number of oracle queries that grows only as the square root of its size. Noise erodes this advantage, and we ask, analytically, how quickly. When a depolarizing channel acts on the whole register after every gate layer, it commutes with the gates, and the noisy state is the noiseless one with a coherent weight that decays geometrically with depth. The Grover rotation is not slowed. Its weight decays, the best stopping point moves earlier, and the expected query cost keeps its square-root scaling only while the accumulated noise over the circuit stays small. For independent errors on the qubits we show that the ideal Grover operator freezes any amplitude pushed out of the two-dimensional search plane. This gives the exact effect of a single error: phase flips do most damage early in the search, bit flips late, and combined errors at any time. For large databases it also reduces the noisy search to Bloch-type equations for the part of the state that stays in the plane, in which the algorithm is a rotation and the noise appears as leakage and dephasing. We derive the rates for bit-flip, phase-flip, depolarizing, and amplitude-damping noise and solve the equations. Local noise acts, to leading order, like a global channel strengthened by the number of qubits, so the tolerable error per qubit falls faster than the inverse square root of the database size by one power of the register size. For coherent errors in the rotation angle we bound the success probability by a band that widens linearly with depth. Our results recover, in closed form, scalings found earlier for particular noise models, and they explain why some errors are more harmful than others.

</details>


### [137] [Probing the extreme quantum violation of the Leggett-Garg inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16401)
*Akarshit Baranwal,Vishnu Purushothaman,A. K. Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了声称量子理论可超越时间Tsirelson界(TTB)的两项研究，指出其论证存在概念缺陷，修正后宏观实在论界达到代数最大值，TTB并不被违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leggett-Garg不等式用于检验宏观实在论与量子理论的不相容性，其量子最大违背常被称为时间Tsirelson界(TTB)。近期有研究通过破简并测量及酉算符叠加声称TTB在量子理论内可被超越，这与Bell不等式Tsirelson界的情形形成反差，需要澄清这些声称是否成立。

Method: 对PRL 113, 050401 (2014)（破简并测量）与PRL 135, 220202 (2025)（酉算符叠加）两项工作的推理进行批判性概念分析；在正确处理相关概念问题后，重新推导LG不等式的宏观实在论界，并考察所提方案是否仍在标准LG框架之内。

Result: 两篇文献所声称的TTB违背论证存在概念性缺陷；恰当处理后，LG不等式的宏观实在论界达到代数最大值，因此并不存在TTB违背；这些"违背"所依赖的框架超出了标准LG框架。

Conclusion: 所谓TTB违背并非真正在标准LG框架内实现，与检验宏观实在论和量子理论不相容性的问题无关；标准LG框架下TTB不被违反。

Abstract: The Leggett-Garg (LG) inequalities were formulated for testing the incompatibility between the classical worldview of macrorealism and quantum theory. By analogy with the Tsirelson bound for Bell inequalities, the maximal quantum violation of LG inequalities is commonly referred to as the temporal Tsirelson bound (TTB). Nevertheless, several studies have claimed that, unlike the Tsirelson bound, the TTB can be exceeded within quantum theory. In particular, by employing degeneracy-breaking measurement in [\href{ https://doi.org/10.1103/PhysRevLett.113.050401}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{113}, 050401 (2014)}] and by using the superposition of unitaries in [\href{https://doi.org/10.1103/vydp-9qqq}{Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{135}, 220202 (2025)}], it is argued that the TTB can be violated. In this work, we critically examine the reasoning underlying these purported TTB violations and show that the corresponding arguments suffer from conceptual shortcomings. We further show that once these conceptual issues are addressed appropriately, the macrorealist bound of LG inequality reaches the algebraic maximum, and hence no violation of the TTB is obtained. In particular, we demonstrate that the proposed TTB violations rely on frameworks that go beyond the standard LG framework, and therefore are of no relevance for testing the incompatibility between macrorealism and quantum theory.

</details>


### [138] [Engineering Bosonic Steady States through Fock-Space Currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16431)
*Yan Liu,Jiahua Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出统一的Fock空间电流工程框架，实现可自主选择目标Fock态的自稳定，并给出与实现细节无关的稳态设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fock态是量子技术的重要资源，但本征损耗使其稳定化困难；已有稳定方案多针对特定微观过程，缺乏统一物理图像与可迁移的设计原理。

Method: 构建Fock空间电流工程框架，推导稳态电流平衡律：预言布居背景随Fock数反比衰减，并量化局域电流如何选择性增强目标Fock态布居；结合全量子模拟验证。

Result: 电流平衡律定量解释了目标态布居增强与背景标度；不同微观实现服从同一稳态设计规则；全量子模拟展示了从真空出发目标态布居的自主构建。

Conclusion: 给出Fock态稳定化的统一物理图像，并建立通过移动局域跃迁即可选择不同目标态、无需改变整体稳定架构的可迁移设计原则。

Abstract: Fock states are valuable resources for quantum technologies, but intrinsic loss makes their stabilization challenging. Whereas existing stabilization schemes are typically formulated for specific microscopic processes, here we develop a unified Fock-space current-engineering framework for the autonomous stabilization of selectable Fock states. We derive a stationary current-balance law that predicts a population background scaling inversely with Fock number and quantifies how a local current selectively enhances the target Fock-state population. This law reveals how distinct microscopic implementations obey the same steady-state design rule, allowing different target states to be selected by moving a local transition without changing the overall stabilization architecture. Full quantum simulations further support these predictions and demonstrate the autonomous buildup of target-state populations from the vacuum. Our results provide a common physical picture of Fock-state stabilization and establish a transferable design principle for engineering selectable bosonic steady states.

</details>


### [139] [Circuit-Level Analysis of Quantum Error Correction in Second-Generation Quantum Repeaters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16435)
*Suraj Vishwanath,Jonas Hawellek,Roberto Ferrara,Christian Deppe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在电路级仿真了采用基于稳定子量子纠错的第二代量子中继器，比较Steane码与五量子比特码在不同噪声下的性能，指出码的选择需依主导噪声机制而定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二代量子中继器需借助量子纠错提升纠缠分发保真度，但不同编码在不同物理噪声机制下的端到端表现缺乏电路级量化比较，且码选择缺乏设计依据。

Method: 构建包含编码、综合征提取、解码与纠缠交换的两段式中继链路，在电路层面直接注入非相干噪声（退极化、退相位）与相干噪声（系统性过旋转），对比单错纠正的Steane码与五量子比特稳定子码。

Result: 相干噪声下Steane码优于五量子比特码；低噪声非相干情形下，当大量物理比特受噪声影响时五量子比特码端到端保真度更高；高噪声区间不编码反而最优；混合噪声下两类噪声在纠错性能上存在中等程度的交叉效应。

Conclusion: 第二代量子中继器的码选择须适配主导物理噪声机制，且电路复杂度与硬件感知实现方式会影响相干噪声主导区间的性能，为量子中继器纠错设计提供指导。

Abstract: We present a circuit-level simulation of second-generation quantum repeaters with stabilizer-based quantum error correction. We model a two-segment repeater link including encoding, syndrome extraction, decoding, and entanglement swapping, and compare the performance of the single-error-correcting Steane and five-qubit stabilizer codes under different noise scenarios. To that extent, we consider noise sources with incoherent (depolarizing and dephasing) and coherent (systematic over-rotation) errors and incorporate them directly at the circuit level. While the Steane code outperforms the five-qubit code at coherent errors, the five-qubit code achieves higher end-to-end fidelities under incoherent noise in the low-noise regime when a large number of physical qubits is affected by it. At higher noise regimes, employing no code turns out to be the best option. In mixed noise scenarios, we observe moderate cross-effects between the noise classes regarding the error correction performance. We conclude that code selection in second-generation quantum repeaters must be adapted to the dominant physical noise mechanism and that circuit complexity and hardware-aware implementation options influence performance in coherent-error-dominated regimes, providing design insights for error correction in quantum repeaters.

</details>


### [140] [Learning Sparse Graph States with Local Pauli Measurements: Tight Nonadaptive Bounds and an Adaptive Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16456)
*Matteo De Francesco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在X/Y/Z基测量下识别n量子比特图态结构所需副本数，证明非自适应策略的$d^2$依赖是必要的，而自适应策略可突破该壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ouyang与Tomamichel给出$d$-正则图态识别需$O(d^2\log n)$副本、下界$\Omega(d\log n)$，二者存在$d$的二次差距，作者欲判定该二次依赖是否不可避免，并探究自适应测量能否降低副本复杂度。

Method: 基于"单次副本至多揭示邻接矩阵某主子矩阵的二元零度"这一事实构造下界：对随机完美匹配并集用傅里叶估计控制零度；二分图情形用类型方法(method of types)论证。上界方面设计广度优先的自适应学习器。

Result: 当$d^3\ln n\le n$时，任意非自适应策略需$\Omega(d^2\log n)$副本，即使解码器无限制且允许常数比例边报错。对二分隐藏且直径对数的连通$d$-正则图，非自适应仍为$\Theta(d^2\log n)$，而广度优先自适应仅需$O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$；公开二分结构后非自适应降为$\Theta(d\log n)$。

Conclusion: 对非自适应学习器，$d$的二次依赖是本质下界；自适应测量可在隐藏二分的对数直径$d$-正则图上实现近线性于$d$的副本数，揭示了自适应性与图结构先验对图态层析复杂度的决定性作用。

Abstract: We study how many copies of an unknown $n$-qubit graph state are needed to identify its graph when every qubit is measured in the $X$, $Y$ or $Z$ basis. Ouyang and Tomamichel showed that $O(d^2\log n)$ copies suffice for $d$-regular graphs, while any measurement needs $Ω(d\log n)$, and asked whether the quadratic dependence on $d$ is necessary. We show that it is for nonadaptive learners: if $d^3\ln n\le n$, every nonadaptive strategy needs $Ω(d^2\log n)$ copies, even with an unrestricted classical decoder and even if a small constant fraction of the edges may be reported wrongly. We then show that adaptivity between copies can beat this barrier. On connected bipartite $d$-regular graphs of logarithmic diameter whose bipartition is hidden, nonadaptive learners still need $Θ(d^2\log n)$ copies, but a breadth-first adaptive learner needs only $O(d\log^2 n/\log\log n)$; revealing the bipartition brings the nonadaptive cost down to $Θ(d\log n)$. The lower bounds rest on a simple fact: one copy reveals at most the binary nullity of a principal submatrix of the adjacency matrix. We control this nullity for random unions of perfect matchings through Fourier estimates and, in the bipartite case, a method-of-types argument.

</details>


### [141] [Symmetry and quasi-gauge invariance of Krylov complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16474)
*Edoardo Ferretti,Matteo G. A. Paris,Claudia Benedetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别了保持Krylov扩展复杂度不变的两类幺正变换，将其与初态能量分布及Hamiltonian矩的不变性相联系，并在手性连续时间量子行走中应用这些性质，揭示手性相位通过解除谱简并使长时平均复杂度发生不连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov扩展复杂度刻画量子系统在时间演化中探索Hilbert空间的程度，但其在何种幺正变换下不变、以及手性与输运不对称对复杂度的作用机制尚不清楚，作者希望厘清复杂度的不变性结构及其与谱性质的关联。

Method: 识别两类保持复杂度的幺正变换（Hamiltonian的对称算符，以及初态为其本征态的局域U(1)变换），将不变性与初态能量分布和Hamiltonian矩的不变性对应；在循环（circulant）Hamiltonian的手性连续时间量子行走中分析手性相位对谱简并的影响，并用非循环量子路由器考察相位依赖的谱权重。

Result: 手性相位在循环Hamiltonian中通常解除谱简并，导致长时平均复杂度发生不连续变化；对局域初态与非简并谱，复杂度为D-1)/2（D为Hilbert空间维数），且与连通度无关；非循环量子路由器示例表明相位依赖谱权重具有额外作用。

Conclusion: 即使概率时间反演对称性保持，Krylov复杂度仍可改变；其对手性的响应源于谱变化而非直接源于输运不对称。

Abstract: Krylov spread complexity quantifies how extensively a quantum system explores its Hilbert space during time evolution. We identify two classes of unitary transformations that preserve the complexity of the associated dynamics, and relate this property to the invariance of the energy distribution of the initial state and of its associated Hamiltonian moments. These transformations are either symmetry operator of the Hamiltonian, or local $U(1)$ transformations of which the initial state is an eigenstate. The latter naturally arise as quasi-gauge transformations in chiral continuous-time quantum walks, to which the invariance properties are applied. We show that chiral phases in circulant Hamiltonians generically lift spectral degeneracies, leading to a discontinuous change in the long-time-averaged complexity. For a localized initial states and a nondegenerate spectrum, complexity is $\bar{C}=(D-1)/2$, where $D$ is the Hilbert-space dimension, independently of the degree of connectivity. A non-circulant quantum router illustrates the additional role of phase-dependent spectral weights. These examples show that Krylov complexity can change even when probability time-reversal symmetry is preserved, linking its response to chirality to spectral changes rather than directly to transport asymmetry.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Mpemba effect from logical-information exposure in monitored circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16479)
*Bochen Huang,D. L. Zhou,Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究受监控Clifford链中未编码Bell对态相对加扰参考态出现的纠缠熵反转（量子Mpemba效应），指出其由逻辑信息暴露驱动，并证明在任意二设计编码器下反转幅度上限为2 ebits。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mpemba效应指初始更远离稳态的态反而更快趋于稳态。作者关注监测量子多体系统中纠缠熵是否也出现此类反转，并追问其机制与普适边界。

Method: 在受监控Clifford链中运行单周期动力学，比较未编码Bell对态与由更少对制备的加扰参考态的平均纠缠熵；用稳定子态的逻辑奇偶性与Schmidt系数分析反转来源；并证明在独立精确幺正二设计编码器下参考系综的熵领先上限。对128与256量子比特使用稳态熵界进行认证。

Result: 未编码Bell对态的平均纠缠熵低于加扰参考态，且差距随链长线性增长；反转源于逻辑信息暴露——稳定子态每被记录解析出一个独立逻辑奇偶即损失一个ebit；半链内加扰可把该信息散布到多格点同时保持全部Schmidt系数不变。理论上，任何初始平均熵不更大的纯参考系综在任意时刻的熵领先至多2 ebits；当稳态熵为次广延量时，大尺寸下即构成量子Mpemba效应；并在128和256量子比特规模上通过稳态熵界予以认证。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵反转由逻辑信息暴露而非Schmidt谱决定，二设计编码器给出2 ebits的普适领先上限；稳态熵次广延时，未编码反转在大尺寸成为真正的量子Mpemba效应，并在上百量子比特规模得到认证。

Abstract: In the Mpemba effect, a state initially farther from stationarity overtakes one that starts closer. After one cycle in a monitored Clifford chain, an unencoded Bell-pair state has lower mean entanglement entropy than a scrambled reference prepared from fewer pairs. The gap grows linearly with chain length. This reversal is driven by logical-information exposure: a stabilizer state loses one ebit per independent logical parity resolved by the record. Scrambling within each half can spread this information over many sites while preserving every Schmidt coefficient. We prove that with independent exact unitary two-design encoders, any pure reference ensemble with no greater mean initial entropy can lead in mean entropy by at most two ebits at any time. If the stationary entropy is subextensive, the unencoded reversal is a quantum Mpemba effect at large sizes. We further certify this effect at 128 and 256 qubits using stationary-entropy bounds.

</details>


### [143] [Effect of atomic interactions on quantum communication protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16519)
*Zixian Hong,John C. Schotland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立原子介质中光子量子通信的实空间第一性原理框架，并用于分析超密编码的协议容量与BB84的密钥率，二者均随原子-场耦合增强而单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子通信协议分析多采用理想化信道模型，缺乏对原子介质中光与物质相互作用的微观描述，难以刻画耦合强度对通信效率与安全性的内禀影响。

Method: 对耦合到两能级原子系综的电磁场作实空间量子化，导出偏振编码单光子态的有效模型；将框架应用于超密编码（固定Bell态字母表在擦除、退相位、退极化信道下的协议容量）以及BB84（丢弃擦除结果、由微观模型估计内禀损耗率的修正协议，并推导渐近密钥率）。

Result: 超密编码协议容量随原子-场耦合增强单调下降；BB84渐近密钥率随耦合强度单调下降，并在有限临界耦合处降为零。

Conclusion: 原子介质的微观光-物质耦合会显著降低量子通信协议的效率与安全性，且存在使BB84密钥率消失的临界耦合强度，表明微观建模对量子通信性能评估不可或缺。

Abstract: We develop a first-principles framework for analyzing photonic quantum communication in an atomic medium. Starting from a real-space quantization of the electromagnetic field coupled to an ensemble of two-level atoms, we derive an effective model for polarization-encoded single-photon states. We apply this framework to superdense coding to study communication efficiency and to the BB84 quantum key distribution protocol for communication security. For superdense coding, we evaluate the protocol capacity of the fixed Bell-state alphabet under erasure, dephasing, and depolarizing channels and show that it decreases monotonically with increasing atom-field coupling. For BB84, we formulate a modified protocol that discards erasure outcomes and uses the microscopic model to estimate the intrinsic loss rate. We derive the corresponding asymptotic key rate and show that it decreases monotonically with coupling strength and vanishes beyond a finite critical coupling.

</details>


### [144] [Asymptotic Attainability of the Channel RLD Fisher-Information Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16578)
*Masahito Hayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出信道RLD Fisher信息与最优渐近SLD Fisher信息相等（界可达）的充要条件，即一个仅依赖Choi矩阵及其一阶导数的半定可行性判据，并由此构造了信道RLD信息与每使用最优渐近SLD信息之比值无界的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道RLD Fisher信息是可计算的、对因果有序且参数无关策略下每信道使用最优渐近SLD Fisher信息的上界，但该界并不总能达到，即使在Choi矩阵正定时也如此。因此需要精确刻画等号成立的条件。

Method: 基于Zhou与Jiang的渐近优化框架，推导仅涉及Choi矩阵及其一阶导数的半定可行性条件；允许RLD矩阵最大特征值退化、Choi矩阵秩亏且导数支撑在其值域上，并允许信道族在评估点附近秩变化。对正定关联矩阵的广义退相干信道，条件化为非负证书权重（和为1）的线性方程组。在完全退相干点计算任意有限次使用的最优SLD信息，并证明重复无辅助探针即可达到。

Result: 得到等号成立的精确判据及其推论；在完全退相干点，任意有限次使用的最优SLD信息可由重复、无辅助的探针策略达到，即使面对自适应策略亦然。显式例子表明信道RLD信息与每使用最优渐近SLD信息之比无界：或随维度增长，或在固定qubit维度下随最小Choi特征值趋于零而发散。

Conclusion: 信道RLD Fisher信息作为最优渐近SLD Fisher信息的上界一般不紧；其紧性由Choi矩阵与一阶导数的半定可行性精确决定，且该上界可与真实渐近值相差任意大。

Abstract: The channel right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information is a computable upper bound on the optimal asymptotic symmetric-logarithmic-derivative (SLD) Fisher information per channel use over causally ordered, parameter-independent strategies. The bound is not always attained, even if the Choi matrix is positive definite. We determine exactly when equality holds using a semidefinite feasibility condition involving only the Choi matrix and its first derivative. The condition allows a degenerate largest eigenvalue of the RLD matrix and rank-deficient Choi matrices with derivatives supported on their range. The original channel family may change rank nearby. For generalized dephasing channels with a positive-definite correlation matrix at the evaluation point, the condition becomes a system of linear equations for nonnegative certificate weights that sum to one. At a completely dephasing point we also calculate the optimal SLD information for every finite number of uses and show that a repeated, ancilla-free probe attains it, even against adaptive strategies. Explicit examples give an unbounded ratio of the channel RLD information to the optimal asymptotic SLD information per use, either as the dimension grows or at fixed qubit dimension as the smallest Choi eigenvalue tends to zero. We derive the equality criterion and its consequences from the asymptotic optimization of Zhou and Jiang.

</details>


### [145] [Gatemon qubits based on in-situ ternary InAsSb/Al nanowire Josephson junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16595)
*Zhenhai Sun,David Feldstein-Bofill,Sangeeth Kallatt,Sabbir A. Khan,Morten Kjaergaard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单次外延生长中，利用阴影限定的原位 InAsSb/Al 约瑟夫森结制备了 gatemon 量子比特，并观测到栅压可调的相干微波响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元 InAsSb/Al 纳米线兼具可组分调控的半导体性质和原位外延近邻超导性，是混合量子电路与介观超导的理想候选，但基于原位 InAsSb/Al 结的相干、栅可调器件尚未实现。

Method: 通过单次生长过程中的阴影限定（shadow-defined）工艺制备原位 InAsSb/Al 约瑟夫森结 gatemon；采用栅压依赖微波谱学、Rabi 振荡与 Ramsey 干涉测量表征比特跃迁频率与相干性。

Result: 栅致静电调节使比特跃迁频率移动数 GHz；观测到 Rabi 振荡与 Ramsey 干涉，代表性 T1 = 0.79 μs、T2* = 0.91 μs；三块芯片上五个 gatemon 的弛豫时间约 0.2–1 μs，并随比特频率升高而下降，量级与自发辐射进入电磁环境的电路估算一致。

Conclusion: 原位 InAsSb/Al 结可作为相干、栅可调的电路元件，并确立 cQED 架构作为探测三元弱链动力学的微波手段。

Abstract: Ternary InAsSb/Al nanowires combine compositionally engineerable semiconductor properties with epitaxial proximity superconductivity, making them attractive for hybrid quantum circuits and mesoscopic superconductivity. Here, we realize gatemon qubits based on shadow-defined in-situ InAsSb/Al Josephson junctions in a single growth process. Gate-dependent microwave spectroscopy demonstrates electrostatic tuning of the qubit transition over several gigahertz. Rabi oscillations and Ramsey interference show coherent qubit control, with representative values T1 = 0.79 us and T2* = 0.91 us. Across five gatemons distributed over three chips, the measured relaxation times range from approximately 0.2 us to 1 us and decrease with increasing qubit frequency. Their magnitude is comparable to a circuit estimate of spontaneous emission into the electromagnetic environment. These results establish in-situ InAsSb/Al junctions as coherent, gate-tunable circuit elements and introduce cQED architecture as a microwave probe of the dynamics of the ternary weak link.

</details>


### [146] [Beauty and charm jet identification with end-to-end training on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16607)
*Lucio Anderlini,Pietro Asinari,Gianluca Bertaina,Davide Calonico,Marco Coïsson,Alessio Gianelle,Donatella Lucchesi,Giovanni Passaleva,Matteo Maria Piredda,Lorenzo Sestini,Michele Veltri,Davide Zuliani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在IQM 5比特超导量子计算机上实现了用于LHCb实验b/c-jet分类的变分量子电路，首次在真实量子硬件上完成全流程（训练与测试），并证明硬件噪声未显著劣化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重味喷注（源自b、c夸克碎裂的粒子束）鉴别是LHC实验中最具挑战的分类任务之一；经典机器学习已成功应用，量子机器学习被视为潜在替代方案，但问题维度高（通常数百个观测量），而当前量子计算机比特数有限，能否胜任此类任务尚存疑问。

Method: 设计变分量子电路（VQC）用于b-jet与c-jet分类，基于LHCb实验的模拟数据，在Politecnico di Torino安装的IQM 5比特超导量子计算机上执行机器学习的训练与测试全部阶段；并提出用于评估量子硬件上训练时间与电路优化的框架。

Result: 首次实现基于LHC实验模拟数据的喷注鉴别量子算法，且训练与测试全过程均在真实量子硬件上完成；结果显示硬件引入的量子噪声未显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为在具备更多比特、可编码超越经典模拟能力信息的更大型超导量子设备上的应用铺平道路，并为探究高能物理分类任务中能否最终实现量子优势提供了第一步。

Abstract: The identification of heavy-flavor jets, i.e. collimated streams of particles originating from the fragmentation of beauty and charm quarks produced in proton-proton collisions, represents one of the most challenging classification tasks in measurements at Large Hadron Collider experiments. In recent years, advanced machine learning techniques have been successfully employed to address this problem, while quantum machine learning has emerged as a promising alternative approach. However, the high dimensionality of the problem, typically involving hundreds of observables, raises an open question: whether the current generation of quantum computers, characterized by limited qubit count, can effectively tackle such complex tasks. In this work, a variational quantum circuit for the classification of b- and c-jets at the LHCb experiment is implemented on a 5-qubit superconducting quantum computer manufactured by IQM and installed at the Politecnico di Torino. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a quantum algorithm for jet identification using simulated data from an LHC experiment in which all stages of the machine-learning workflow, including training and testing, are performed on real quantum hardware. The results demonstrate that the impact of hardware-induced quantum noise does not significantly degrade the performance. A framework for the evaluation of the training time on the quantum hardware and for the circuit optimization is also proposed. These findings pave the way for future applications on larger quantum devices based on superconducting qubits, featuring a larger number of qubits enabling the encoding of information beyond the reach of classical simulation and providing a step toward investigating whether quantum advantage may eventually be achievable in high-energy physics classification tasks.

</details>


### [147] [A Quantum Algorithm for Computing All Eigenvalues and Eigenstates of Sparse Hermitian Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16610)
*Krishnakanta Barik,Goutam Paul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子算法，在稀疏访问假设下以 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min) 时间求出稀疏厄米矩阵的全部谱（去重特征值）并为每个特征值在对应特征子空间内制备一个特征态，避免经典特征向量的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏厄米矩阵的特征值/特征向量计算是代数基本问题，也是大量科学与计算应用的核心子程序。经典稠密方法（Householder三对角化+QR迭代）需 O(n³)；利用稀疏性的Lanczos虽可将特征值计算降至 O(n²s)，但恢复全部特征向量仍需 O(n³)。本文希望在全谱与特征态上获得优于经典的渐近复杂度。

Method: 量子算法：特征值以规定精度的二进制串输出（谱=不同特征值集合），对应特征向量保持为量子态而非重构经典向量；算法支持估计期望值 ⟨x|M|x⟩（|x⟩为已制备特征态，M为可观测量）。复杂度分析基于标准化矩阵的最小谱隙 Δ_min，并在标准稀疏访问模型下与经典 O(n³) 方法对比。

Result: 运行时间为 Õ(n s² log²n / Δ_min)。当 1/Δ_min = O(n)（n 个不同特征值下的最优标度，接近均匀间隔谱，如完美态转移自旋链与 Sylvester–Kac 矩阵），且稀疏度 s = o(√n / log n) 时，渐近复杂度优于经典 O(n³) 全特征对计算。

Conclusion: 该量子算法在全谱与特征态求解上对一类稀疏、谱隙不太小（谱近均匀）的厄米矩阵实现渐近加速，且以量子态形式输出特征向量、支持可观测量的期望值估计，避免了经典重构开销。

Abstract: Computation of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a sparse Hermitian matrix is a fundamental problem in algebra, arising both as a problem of independent interest and as a key subroutine in a wide range of computational and scientific applications. In this work, we develop a quantum algorithm that determines the spectrum of a sparse Hermitian matrix, defined as the set of its distinct eigenvalues, and for each eigenvalue, prepares a corresponding eigenvector as a quantum state in its eigenspace. The eigenvalues are represented as binary strings with a prescribed precision, while the corresponding eigenvectors are kept as quantum states, eliminating the need to reconstruct their classical counterparts. The algorithm instead enables estimation of expectation values $\braket{x|M|x}$, where $\ket{x}$ is a prepared eigenstate and $M$ is an observable. For an $n \times n$ $s$-sparse Hermitian matrix $H$, dense methods based on Householder tridiagonalization and QR iteration compute all eigenvalues and eigenvectors in $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. Exploiting sparsity, the Lanczos method computes all eigenvalues in $\mathcal{O}(n^2 s)$ time in exact arithmetic, but recovering all eigenvectors still requires $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ time. In contrast, our algorithm runs in time $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n s^2 \log^2 n / Δ_{\min})$, where $Δ_{\min}$ denotes the minimum spectral gap of the normalized matrix. For inverse gaps up to $Δ_{\min}^{-1} = \mathcal{O}(n)$, the most favorable scaling for $n$ distinct eigenvalues, attained by nearly uniformly spaced spectra such as those of perfect-state-transfer spin chains and the Sylvester-Kac matrix, and for sparsity up to $s = o(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$, the proposed algorithm achieves an improved asymptotic runtime over the classical $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ approach for computing all eigenvalues and eigenvectors, under standard sparse-access assumptions.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum tunneling of a measurement-driven active particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16626)
*Tobias Nadolny,Matteo Brunelli,Christoph Bruder,Michel Fruchart*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并演示了一种“量子活性粒子”：自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼将粒子运动锁定于自旋取向，量子Zeno效应下的连续测量使取向保持持久，单条量子轨迹呈现run-and-tumble运动；其持久速度导致在势垒前积累而非像自由量子粒子那样反射，且量子隧穿使其透射率高于经典类似粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性粒子（active particles）的核心特征是由取向决定的持久运动，但此前研究主要局限于经典框架；作者希望将这一关键性质推广到量子体系，检验量子效应（Zeno连续测量、自旋-轨道耦合、隧穿）如何生成并调控活性运动。

Method: 构建单量子活性粒子模型：利用自旋-轨道耦合与阻尼使粒子运动方向锁定于自旋取向；通过量子Zeno效应下的连续测量使自旋取向保持持久，从而在个体量子轨迹层面产生run-and-tumble式活性运动；进而考察粒子撞击势垒时的动力学，并对比隧穿与经典情形的透射率。

Result: 单条量子轨迹表现出run-and-tumble活性运动；粒子撞击势垒时，其持久速度导致在势垒附近积累，与自由量子粒子的反射行为形成对比；量子隧穿使该活性粒子的透射率高于对应的经典活性粒子。

Conclusion: 量子活性粒子可在单量子层面实现“运动-取向锁定+持久取向”的活性运动机制，并展现出区别于自由量子粒子与经典活性粒子的势垒输运特征，为量子活性物质（quantum active matter）研究提供了可操作的单粒子范例。

Abstract: Active particles can exhibit persistent motion set by their orientation. We demonstrate this key property for a quantum active particle: The particle's motion is locked to the orientation of its spin due to spin-orbit coupling and damping, and the orientation is made persistent through continuous measurement via the quantum Zeno effect. As a result, individual quantum trajectories feature an active run-and-tumble motion. When the particle impinges on a potential barrier, its persistent velocity leads to accumulation near the barrier, in contrast with the reflection of a free quantum particle. We show that quantum tunneling enhances the active particle's transmission rate compared to that of an analogous classical particle.

</details>


### [149] [Geometric Theory of Quasiperiodically Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16683)
*Chengzhi Ye,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文基于绝热捷径（counterdiabatic driving）构建了研究任意准周期驱动的一般框架，给出精确有效平均能量算符Æ与规范势，并据此建立动力学不变空间的四重分类及非阿贝尔Floquet理论推广与遍历性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期驱动系统蕴含丰富的量子物理现象，但与周期驱动（Floquet）系统不同，其缺乏用有效时不变哈密顿量及其本征态描述演化的通用理论，因此难以刻画其动力学不变空间（Floquet态的推广）。

Method: 以counterdiabatic driving为基础，构造识别并确定任意准周期驱动之动力学不变空间的框架；利用精确的有效平均能量算符Æ与相应规范势分解幺正演化，并在频率格点算符空间中通过变分原理计算这些量；进而依据不变空间的几何与拓扑结构提出四重分类（非）阿贝尔与（非）扭曲类，并引入以不变本征空间合并为标志的遍历性概念。

Result: 得到阿贝尔非扭曲类将Floquet定理直接推广至不可通约准周期驱动；进一步导出Floquet理论的非阿贝尔推广；并提出与混合经典相空间中遍历性出现相类似的遍历性概念（其起点由动力学不变本征空间合并标记）。

Conclusion: 所提出的有效数学框架、变分原理与非阿贝尔遍历性并不局限于准周期驱动，可推广至更广泛的系统。

Abstract: Quasiperiodically driven systems give rise to a rich set of physical phenomena in contemporary quantum physics, with applications ranging from nonequilibrium dynamics to Hamiltonian engineering and control. However, unlike periodically driven (Floquet) systems, their study is hindered by the absence of a generic theory to describe their evolution using an effective time-independent Hamiltonian and its eigenstates. Based on counterdiabatic driving, we construct a general framework to identify and determine the dynamically invariant spaces of arbitrary quasiperiodic drives (i.e., the generalization of Floquet states). We decompose their unitary evolution using the exact effective average-energy operator Æ and the corresponding gauge potential, which we compute via a variational principle in frequency-lattice operator space. We then formulate a four-fold taxonomy of quasiperiodic drives based on the geometry and topology of their dynamically invariant spaces into (non)abelian and (un)twisted classes. The abelian untwisted class admits a direct extension of Floquet's theorem to incommensurate quasiperiodic drives; going beyond, we derive a nonabelian generalization of Floquet theory. Finally, we propose a concept of ergodicity whose onset is marked by coalescing dynamically invariant eigenspaces, in direct analogy to the emergence of ergodicity in a mixed classical phase space. The effective mathematical framework, variational principle, and nonabelian ergodicity we put forward apply beyond quasiperiodic drives.

</details>


### [150] [Persistent-mediator architecture for universal fault-tolerant quantum computation with color codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16691)
*Zhi-Cheng He,Jingbo Wang,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出跨维度"持久中介"架构：2D 色码数据寄存器与 3D 中介模块在每个周期边界保持各自编码，仅通过瞬态受保护子系统耦合，实现可重复、确定性的逻辑 T 门循环而无需后选择与破坏性读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D 色码只能提供横向 Clifford 门，3D 码才能提供横向 T 门；已有跨维度方案或需切换计算编码，或需消耗一个 3D 编码态，导致反复制备辅助态与末端读出的开销。核心问题：3D 模块能否在保持编码与保护的同时，为独立 2D 寄存器连续介导 T 门。

Method: 构造膜状联合逻辑测量，配合匹配的跨维度 CZ、互补的弦状 surgery 与前馈，闭合确定性逻辑 T 门循环；用稳定子分析确定逻辑作用与保持的空间距离；在 Clifford 替代模型上做唯象级与电路级仿真，并做连续多周期测试。

Result: 循环无需后选择、无破坏性中介读出，且 3D 块恢复到初始逻辑态；有限距离下错误标度接近匹配的 3D 存储基准；连续操作下低错误区逻辑错误近似线性累积。

Conclusion: 持久中介使计算寄存器保持 2D 编码的同时可反复调用高维非 Clifford 能力，为跨维度容错架构提供可复用资源方案。

Abstract: Two-dimensional color codes provide transversal Clifford gates, whereas suitable three-dimensional codes provide a transversal $T$ gate. Representative cross-dimensional schemes either switch the computational encoding or consume a 3D encoded state. Reusing the same protected 3D block would remove repeated auxiliary-state preparation and terminal readout, raising a central question: can the 3D module remain encoded and protected while mediating successive $T$ gates on a separate 2D register? Here we introduce a cross-dimensional persistent-mediator architecture that retains the 2D data register and 3D mediator as distinct encoded modules at each cycle boundary, coupled only through a transient protected subsystem. We construct a membrane-like joint-logical measurement and combine it with a matched cross-dimensional $CZ$, complementary string-like surgery, and feed-forward to close a deterministic logical $T$-gate cycle. The cycle avoids post-selection and destructive mediator readout and restores the 3D block to its initial logical state. Stabilizer analysis establishes the logical action and preserved spatial distance. Phenomenological and circuit-level simulations of a Clifford surrogate show finite-distance scaling close to the matched 3D memory benchmark, while sequential tests show approximately linear logical-error accumulation in the low-error regime. Persistent mediation enables repeated access to higher-dimensional non-Clifford capability while keeping the computational register in its 2D encoding.

</details>


### [151] [Thermodynamically Consistent Quantum-Classical Hybrid Dynamics: A Metriplectic Approach to Generalized Lindblad and Bloch-Torrey Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16708)
*Azeddine Zaidni,Ayoub Arraji,Philip J. Morrison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子–经典混合动力学的度规辛（metriplectic）框架，通过能量守恒与熵产生非负保证热力学一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子–经典混合动力学缺少统一的热力学一致性描述（能量守恒与熵产生非负），且量子与经典子系统间的耦合机制不清晰。

Method: 将混合Poisson括号与度规辛4-括号相结合，令量子–经典耦合仅通过耗散过程产生；在该混合表述下构造Lindblad主方程与Bloch–Torrey方程的广义版本。

Result: 得到开放量子系统的热力学一致广义Lindblad方程与Bloch–Torrey方程，实现量子弛豫与经典扩散的统一描述并给出熵产生的清晰解释。

Conclusion: 度规辛4-括号框架为量子–经典混合耗散动力学提供了统一、热力学一致的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a metriplectic framework for quantum--classical hybrid dynamics that ensures thermodynamic consistency through conservation of energy and non-negative entropy production. The approach combines a hybrid Poisson bracket with a metriplectic $4$-bracket, with coupling between quantum and classical subsystems arising solely through dissipative processes. Within this hybrid formulation, we produce thermodynamically consistent generalizations of the Lindblad master equations and the Bloch--Torrey equations for open quantum systems. This provides a unified description of quantum relaxation and classical diffusion, together with a clear interpretation of entropy production.

</details>


### [152] [Probabilistic distillation of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16719)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在"无叠加（superposition-free）操作"资源理论框架下，确定将一个非正交态叠加（如光学猫态）转化为另一个的最优概率，并给出对称叠加的通用最优概率、两参考态完全解与多参考态的部分不可能性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠加态（如光学猫态）作为量子资源的价值取决于其各分量的干涉方式，但缺乏刻画"由干涉能力定义的资源"在态间相互转化的定量理论；需要确定在不能确定性地增强破坏性干涉的操作下，叠加态之间转换的最优概率。

Method: 构建以"叠加无关操作"为自由操作的理论：此类操作不能以确定性方式使分量间的干涉更破坏性且同时保留全部分量。对分量置换对称的源态给出并证明最优转换概率公式；以相干态为参考态时，逐分量映射到相干态的操作（如光子损耗、光子减除）均为叠加无关；对两参考态情形给出完全解，并分析等模系数叠加与集体操作。

Result: 两参考态情形完全求解：等模叠加是可互不等价的资源——奇猫态可经归一化光子减除确定性变为偶猫态，偶猫态变奇态的概率在小振幅下趋于零，偶猫态与Yurke–Stoler猫态两方向均不能确定性互转。多拷贝集体操作优于逐拷贝转换，唯相干性蒸馏例外（无增益）。三及以上等正重叠参考态时，无态可确定性到达所有其他态；破坏性干涉最强的等模叠加可确定性得到最大相干态，且对任意全参考态叠加的转换至少不劣于该态，但最坏情况下不更优。

Conclusion: 干涉能力构成独立的资源序：等模叠加并不等价，确定性转换仅在特定方向可行；集体操作一般有增益但不能提升相干性蒸馏；参考态数增加时确定性普适转换不可能，等模破坏性干涉叠加在典型情形达到最优、在最坏情形不超越最大相干态。

Abstract: How well a superposition of nonorthogonal states, such as an optical cat state, serves as a resource depends on how its components interfere. Here we determine the optimal probability of converting one such superposition into another by superposition-free operations. These operations cannot make the components of a superposition interfere more destructively with certainty while keeping all of them, and this principle fixes the optimal probability for every source that is symmetric under permutations of its components. When the reference states are coherent states, as in a cat state, every operation that maps each of them to a coherent state, such as photon loss or photon subtraction, is superposition-free. For two reference states we solve the problem completely. Superpositions with coefficients of equal modulus, the counterparts of the maximally coherent states of coherence theory, are therefore inequivalent resources: an odd cat state becomes even with certainty by a normalized photon subtraction, whereas an even one becomes odd only with a probability that vanishes for small amplitudes, and the even and the Yurke$-$Stoler cat states cannot be converted into each other with certainty in either direction. Collective operations on several copies can do better than converting them one at a time, except in the distillation of coherence, which gains nothing from them. For three or more reference states with equal positive overlaps, no state reaches every other state with certainty; the equal-modulus superpositions that interfere most destructively still yield a maximally coherent state with certainty and reach every superposition of all reference states at least as well as such a state does, but in the worst case no better.

</details>


### [153] [Ground-State Spin Squeezing and Collective Excitations in $\mathbb{Z}_2$-Symmetric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16738)
*Arkadiusz Kosior,George Mihailescu,Karol Gietka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文厘清了基态自旋压缩与集体激发及多体能隙的关系，指出在二次型集体模式下压缩由两个横向涨落代价之比决定、集体能隙由其几何平均决定，并证明存在压缩增强与能隙增大同时发生的“开隙分支”（具更好热鲁棒性与更快绝热制备潜力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态自旋压缩是量子增强测量的重要资源，但通常与量子临界点附近激发能隙闭合相联系；能隙闭合会增大态制备难度并削弱热鲁棒性。作者希望找出压缩与集体激发之间的物理机制，明确何时更强压缩伴随更大能隙。

Method: 以各向异性Lipkin–Meshkov–Glick模型为起点，分析二次型集体模式中两个横向涨落代价的比值与几何平均；讨论$\mathbb Z_2$宇称对产生不等代价的配对项的允许性；推导压缩、极化与按集体自旋跃迁强度加权的激发能之间的精确关系；对XXZ链采用弱耦合与自旋波分析，并结合幂律相互作用（含最近邻极限）的精确有限尺寸计算；对比Ising链情形。

Result: 二次型模式下压缩由横向涨落代价之比决定、集体能隙由几何平均决定；XXZ链中压缩可在集体能隙与多体能隙同时增大时发展；精确计算确认存在负耦合分支，两能隙重合且随压缩改善而增大；最近邻情形下该分支在较高温度仍保持压缩，优于基态压缩相当但能隙闭合的分支；Ising链中交错激发可降低多体能隙而不直接贡献集体涨落。

Conclusion: 基态压缩既可伴随集体能隙闭合也可伴随其开启；存在能隙开启分支，兼具更强热鲁棒性与更快绝热制备的潜力。

Abstract: Ground-state spin squeezing is a resource for quantum-enhanced measurements, but is commonly associated with a closing excitation gap near a quantum critical point. A closing gap can complicate state preparation and reduce thermal robustness. Here we identify the physical mechanism linking squeezing to collective excitations and establish when stronger squeezing accompanies a larger many-body gap. Using the anisotropic Lipkin--Meshkov--Glick model, we show that, for a quadratic collective mode, the ratio of two transverse fluctuation costs determines squeezing, whereas their geometric mean sets the collective gap. The pair terms responsible for unequal costs are allowed, but not guaranteed, by $\mathbb Z_2$ parity. To extend this picture beyond a single mode, we derive an exact relation between squeezing, polarization, and excitation energies weighted by their collective-spin transition strengths. For XXZ chains, weak-coupling and spin-wave analyses explain how squeezing can develop while both the collective and many-body gaps increase. Exact finite-size calculations for power-law interactions, including the nearest-neighbor limit, confirm a negative-coupling branch on which the two gaps coincide and increase as squeezing improves. For nearest-neighbor interactions, this branch also retains squeezing at higher temperatures than a gap-closing branch with comparable ground-state squeezing. In the Ising chain, by contrast, a staggered excitation can lower the many-body gap without directly contributing to collective fluctuations. Our results explain why ground-state squeezing can accompany either a closing or an opening collective gap, and identify a gap-opening branch with enhanced thermal robustness and the potential for faster adiabatic preparation.

</details>


### [154] [Reduced filtering using classical control hierarchy with linear optimality for continuous quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16751)
*Manuel Guatto,F. A. Cárdenas-López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: This paper recasts continuous quantum error correction as a classical control-theoretic state hierarchy of an exact bilinear reduced model, exact conditional filter, and standard estimation/feedback design. It proves the exact filter closes at dimension 3·2^(n-k-1), set by the syndrome space, exposes and fixes two feedback failure mechanisms, and introduces a committed-recovery controller requiring only 2^(n-k) class probabilities that attains the highest entanglement fidelity among tested methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Continuous quantum error correction promises reduced thresholds and hardware overhead versus digital approaches, but its complex model estimation severely limits practicality. The paper aims to make continuous QEC tractable by replacing the burdensome model estimation with a standard control-theoretic framework.

Method: For any [[n,k,d]] stabilizer code with all generators continuously monitored and driven by a single Pauli recovery channel, the authors build an exact bilinear reduced model and an exact conditional filter, proving the filter closes at dimension 3·2^(n-k-1) (syndrome-space defined and minimal within invariant-subspace filtering). They then analyze the feedback loop, identifying that strong monitoring damps the rotational partner used for steering and injects back-action diffusion along the recovery path, and that linearizing at the codespace leaves the syndrome uncontrollable (stall). Fixes: lowering measurement strength during correction, and designing the gain at a shifted operating point. In the zero-measurement limit during correction, they prove a syndrome-only filter is exact at every recovery boundary, yielding a committed-recovery controller.

Result: Lowering measurement strength during correction raises entanglement fidelity of partner-steering LQR control by 0.096 in the three-qubit code with three monitored parities. A gain at a shifted operating point removes the code-space linearization degeneracy. The committed-recovery controller achieves 0.947 vs 0.920 (measurement-based estimator protocol) in the three-qubit bit-flip code, and 0.726 vs at most 0.407 (continuous controllers) in the five-qubit perfect code.

Conclusion: Continuous QEC can be reformulated as classical control with a dimension-reduced filter governed by the syndrome space, correcting key feedback failure mechanisms. The committed-recovery controller, requiring only 2^(n-k) class probabilities, delivers the highest entanglement fidelity among tested methods, substantially improving practicality of continuous quantum error correction.

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction promises reduced thresholds and hardware overhead compared to the digital paradigm, but its complex model estimation has greatly reduced its practicality. We recast this problem as a classical control-theoretic state hierarchy made of an exact bilinear reduced model, an exact conditional filter, and standard estimation and feedback design. For any $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code whose generators are all continuously monitored and which is driven by a single Pauli recovery channel, we prove that the exact filter closes at dimension $3\cdot2^{\,n-k-1}$. This dimension is set by the syndrome space instead of the Hilbert space and is minimal within invariant-subspace filtering. The reduced model exposes and corrects two failure mechanisms of the feedback loop. Strong monitoring damps the rotational partner through which the controller steers the stabilizer and injects back-action diffusion along the recovery path; lowering the measurement strength during correction raises the entanglement fidelity of partner-steering LQR control by $0.096$ in the three-qubit code with three monitored parities. Linearizing at the codespace leaves the syndrome uncontrollable, so the controller can stall; a gain designed at a shifted operating point removes this degeneracy. In the limit of zero measurement during correction, we prove that a syndrome-only filter is exact at every recovery boundary. The resulting committed-recovery controller needs only $2^{\,n-k}$ class probabilities and gives the highest entanglement fidelity compared among the methods tested in the literature, $0.947$ against $0.920$ for a measurement-based estimator protocol in the three-qubit bit-flip code, and $0.726$ vs at most $0.407$ for continuous controllers in the five-qubit perfect code.

</details>


### [155] [Inter-city 18.9 km free-space QKD over a dense urban corridor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16774)
*Ignacio H. López Grande,Nicolas Linale,Giacomo Paganini,Achraf El Bouazzaoui,Adrián S. Blanca,Ilektra Karakosta-Amarantidou,Manu Canals i Codina,Adam Vallés,Andrea Gorni,Jorge Fuenzalida,Evelyn A. Ortega,Valerio Pruneri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在巴塞罗那都市区18.9 km自由空间链路上，用航空合规光学架构、785 nm室温单光子探测器和无自适应光学接收端，实验演示诱骗态BB84 QKD，40分钟外场测试平均信道衰减约19 dB、平均QBER 1.31%，估算有限码长密钥率超过11 kbit/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面自由空间QKD是光纤网络的有效补充，但在密集城区的长距离水平链路部署困难：大气湍流、严苛的瞄准跟踪要求与运行约束显著提升系统复杂度。作者希望验证一套相对简单、可部署的架构能否在密集城区实现城际自由空间QKD。

Method: 采用诱骗态BB84协议与面向部署的模块化QKD发射机，配合通用自由空间光学终端和独立的GPS授时同步架构；工作波长785 nm，使用室温单光子探测器，接收端无需自适应光学；光学架构满足航空合规要求。文中给出发射机性能与链路光学特性，并开展40分钟外场测试，基于实测信道参数进行有限码长密钥率估算。

Result: 18.9 km城市链路外场测试40分钟，平均信道衰减约19 dB，平均QBER 1.31%，估算平均有限码长秘密密钥率超过11 kbit/s。

Conclusion: 结果表明，采用相对简单的架构即可在密集城区实现城际自由空间QKD，验证了该技术在城市尺度部署的可行性，并具备与异构光学基础设施集成的潜力。

Abstract: Terrestrial free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides a flexible complement to fiber-based networks, but its deployment over long horizontal paths in dense metropolitan environments remains challenging, as atmospheric turbulence, demanding pointing and tracking requirements, and operational constraints can substantially increase system complexity. We experimentally demonstrate the operation of a decoy-state BB84 system over an 18.9~km free-space link across a densely populated urban area in the metropolitan region of Barcelona using an aviation-compliant optical architecture. The system operates at 785~nm using room-temperature single-photon detectors and requires no adaptive optics at the receiver. The modular QKD transmitter, based on a deployment-oriented design, is interfaced with a versatile free-space optical terminal and an independent GPS-disciplined synchronization architecture, facilitating its integration into heterogeneous optical infrastructures. We present the transmitter performance and optical-link characteristics, together with a 40-minute field test characterized by an average channel attenuation of approximately 19~dB and a mean QBER of 1.31\%. From the measured channel parameters, we estimate an average finite-size secret key rate exceeding 11~kbit/s. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inter-city free-space QKD over dense urban areas using a comparatively simple architecture.

</details>


### [156] [Quantum Keynesian Beauty Contest with Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.16829)
*Hiu Ming Lau,Kareem Raslan,Kieran Hymas,Qiang Sun,Azhar Iqbal,Derek Abbott,James Q. Quach,Andrew D. Greentree*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在量子博弈论框架下研究凯恩斯选美竞赛模型(p-Beauty Contest)，提出基于连续变量多模压缩的量子实现，通过全局变换抑制理性博弈导致的暴涨/暴跌行为，并证明该机制可用经典全局中介者等效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典p-BC模型中理性参与者必然导致p>1时的暴涨或p<1时的暴跌策略，这种非效率均衡是博弈论中的经典难题。作者希望借助量子过程重新审视该模型，寻求缓解这种极端理性行为的机制。

Method: 构建p-BC的量子连续变量实现：对多模压缩算符（随压缩度增加）生成的全局变换作用于参与者报价；推导两方、三方及平均场型多参与者情形下单值测量下收益概率分布的解析表达式；并构造一个由经典全局中介者实现的完全类似博弈作为对照。

Result: 重缩放收益概率分布反映市场热度；p>1与p<1时策略不对称——p>1下最佳响应为高估收益，p<1下则为低估收益，后者与经典预测一致；而全收缩变换在两种情况下均能抑制暴涨/暴跌。单值测量的量子策略集相对期望值测量设定发生改变，量子化同时修正了两位与N位参与者的均衡。经典信道通过全局中介者可复现相同行为。

Conclusion: 全局中介（而非量子性本身）是抑制暴涨/暴跌现象的核心博弈论机制；量子p-BC的解析结果揭示了测量范式（单值vs期望值）对理性行为与均衡的影响，为量子博弈设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: The development of quantum computing has opened up opportunities to re-explore conventional game models by the integration of quantum processes. Through the established framework of quantum game theory, we examine the problem of speculative pricing in the Keynesian Beauty Contest model -- the $p$-Beauty Contest ($p$-BC).
